In particular, with high probability, each student component converges near its closest teacher component, and we provide conditions under which the student distribution converges in total variation distance. Our proofs rely on a new Lyapunov-based analysis of the gradient descent dynamics, showing that the reverse Fisher divergence has a much more favorable optimization landscape than the forward Fisher divergence.

</details>


### [64] [On the Oracle Complexity of Interpolation-Based Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19878)
*Dongmin Lee,William Lu,Anuran Makur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分段多项式插值的非精确梯度下降法（PPI-GD），通过在数据域中查询等距点的一阶预言机构造多项式插值来近似全梯度，在损失函数足够光滑且数据维度为训练样本数的多对数函数时，在强凸和非凸场景下优于多种GD变体的预言机复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究指出经验风险最小化（ERM）损失函数在训练数据上的光滑性（而非优化参数上的光滑性）可用于改善梯度下降法的预言机复杂度，本文旨在利用这一发现设计更高效的优化算法。

Method: 提出PPI-GD算法：每次迭代在数据域等距点处查询一阶预言机构造梯度样本，通过d维张量积多项式插值在适当大小的数据块上构建多项式插值函数来近似全梯度，并采用非精确梯度下降框架进行分析。

Result: 在强凸和非凸损失函数下，当数据空间维度不超过训练样本数的多对数函数时，PPI-GD的预言机复杂度在关键区域中优于多种GD变体；同时将双三次样条插值的误差分析推广到了d维张量积多项式插值场景。

Conclusion: PPI-GD有效利用数据域光滑性降低了预言机复杂度，所发展的张量积多项式插值误差分析技术具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: Recent work on first-order optimizers for empirical risk minimization (ERM) has suggested that smoothness of ERM loss functions in the training data, rather than in the optimization parameters, can be leveraged to improve the oracle complexity of gradient descent (GD) methods. In this paper, we propose an inexact gradient method, piecewise polynomial interpolation-based gradient descent (PPI-GD), which approximates the full gradient in each iteration by querying the first-order oracle at equidistant points in the data domain to construct polynomial interpolants of the resulting gradient samples over appropriately sized patches of the data domain. We analyze the oracle complexity of PPI-GD for strongly convex and non-convex loss functions when the data space dimension is bounded by a polylogarithmic function of the number of training samples, and find it to outperform several GD variants in key regimes when the loss function is sufficiently smooth. Furthermore, our analysis extends several techniques from the error analysis of bicubic spline interpolants to the setting of $d$-variate tensor product polynomial interpolants which may be of independent interest in interpolation analysis.

</details>


### [65] [Matching Markets meet Cumulative Prospect Theory: Towards Optimal and Adversarially Robust Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19883)
*Ananya Kunisetty,Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究竞争性双边匹配市场中基于累积前景理论（CPT）的多智能体多臂赌博机问题，提出了考虑人类非线性偏好权重的最优遗憾界算法，并进一步扩展到对抗性市场中的鲁棒学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体多臂赌博机研究较少考虑人类决策中的非线性偏好特征，而CPT作为行为经济学中广泛使用的模型能够更真实地刻画人类风险敏感行为，因此需要在匹配市场框架下引入CPT进行理论分析。

Method: 利用CPT的α-Hölder连续权重函数对奖励进行非线性扭曲，分析现有最优算法在该设置下的遗憾界；通过精心选择探索阶段的活跃臂集消除主项对臂数K的依赖，实现最优遗憾界；进一步针对已知和未知总腐败预算两种对抗性市场场景提出鲁棒算法。

Result: 获得了玩家最优遗憾界O(K log T (1/Δ)^{2/α})；改进后的算法在K远超玩家数N时去除了K的依赖并达到最优遗憾；在已知和未知腐败预算的对抗性市场中均建立了对数级玩家最优遗憾保证。

Conclusion: CPT权重扭曲下的多智能体匹配市场问题可以通过合理算法设计实现最优遗憾，且该框架可扩展至对抗性腐败场景，验证了CPT作为风险敏感度量的有效性。

Abstract: We study a multi-agent multi-armed bandit problem in the competitive setup with two-sided matching markets under a human centric decision making model. To capture human preferences, we use cumulative prospect theory (CPT) that weighs the actions of the agent in a nonlinear fashion using a ($α$-Hölder continuous) weight function. CPT has been widely used in behavioral economics and risk sensitive machine learning to emulate human preferences. We analyze the state-of-the-art learning algorithm with CPT weight distorted rewards and obtain a player optimal regret of $\mathcal{O}(K\log T \left(\frac{1}Δ\right)^{2/α})$, where $K$ denotes the number of arms, $T$ is the learning horizon, and $Δ$ represents (suitably defined) players' minimum preference gap. Noticing the dependence on $Δ$ to be sub-optimal, we further improve this regret by judiciously selecting the active set of arms during exploration, which removes the dependence on $K$ in the dominant term and achieves an improved (optimal) regret guarantees in the setting where the number of arms $K$ is significantly larger than the number of players $N$. In addition, we consider adversarial markets where the observed rewards of the agents may be corrupted. We propose and analyze algorithms for robust markets with CPT as risk sensitive measure in both settings where the total corruption budget is known and where it is unknown, and establish logarithmic player-optimal regret guarantees in both cases.

</details>


### [66] [SL-S4Wave: Self-Supervised Learning of Physiological Waveforms with Structured State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19888)
*Feng Wu,Harsh Deep,Eric Lehman,Sanyam Kapoor,Guoshuai Zhao,Rahul Krishnan,Gari Clifford,Li-wei H Lehman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SL-S4Wave，一种结合对比学习与结构化状态空间模型的自监督学习框架，用于长序列多通道生理波形（如ECG、EEG）表示学习，在心律失常检测等任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法（如基于CNN的编码器）难以捕捉医学时间序列数据中的长程依赖和噪声不变特征，而结构化状态空间模型虽擅长长序列建模但未能充分利用多通道生理波形的独特特性。

Method: 提出SL-S4Wave框架，编码器采用基于结构化状态空间模型的架构，通过多尺度子核实现多层全局卷积，同时捕获细粒度局部模式和长程时间依赖；结合对比学习进行自监督预训练。

Result: 在心律失常检测任务上一致优于监督和自监督基线；显著减少标注样本仍保持高性能；在长波形片段上表现鲁棒；有效迁移到未见心律失常类型；在多个EEG任务上也表现优异。

Conclusion: SL-S4Wave通过结合结构化状态空间模型与对比学习，有效解决了长序列多通道生理波形建模中的关键挑战，具有良好的标签效率、长序列建模能力和跨领域泛化性。

Abstract: Modeling long-sequence medical time series data, such as electrocardiograms (ECG), poses significant challenges due to high sampling rates, multichannel signal complexity, inherent noise, and limited labeled data. While recent self-supervised learning (SSL) methods, based on various encoder architectures such as convolutional neural networks, have been proposed to learn representations from unlabeled data, they often fall short in capturing long-range dependencies and noise-invariant features. Structured state space models (S4) excel at long-sequence modeling, but existing S4 architectures fail to capture the unique characteristics of multichannel physiological waveforms. In this work, we propose SL-S4Wave, a self-supervised learning framework that combines contrastive learning with a tailored encoder built on structured state space models. The encoder incorporates multi-layer global convolution using multiscale subkernels, enabling the capture of both fine-grained local patterns and long-range temporal dependencies in noisy, high-resolution multichannel waveforms. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that SL-S4Wave (1) consistently outperforms state-of-the-art supervised and self-supervised baselines in a challenging arrhythmia detection task, (2) achieves high performance with significantly fewer labeled examples, showcasing strong label efficiency, and (3) maintains robust performance on long waveform segments, highlighting its capacity to model complex temporal dynamics in long sequences that most existing approaches fail to efficiently model, and (4) transfers effectively to unseen arrhythmia types, underscoring its robust cross-domain generalization. We additionally evaluate SL-S4Wave on multiple EEG tasks, achieving superior performance over strong baselines, demonstrating generalizability of our approach beyond cardiac waveforms.

</details>


### [67] [Adversarial Bandit Optimization with Globally Bounded Perturbations to Convex Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19891)
*Zhuoyu Cheng,Kohei Hatano,Eiji Takimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性赌博机优化问题，其中损失函数可能非凸非光滑。每轮学习器选择动作并仅观察该动作的损失，损失由凸且β-光滑的底层部分和对抗性扰动组成，扰动受全局预算约束。作者将后动作扰动模型从线性损失推广到一般凸β-光滑损失，建立了期望遗憾保证，并通过修改标准赌博机优化算法及分析来控制扰动带来的额外遗憾。无扰动时，结果退化为标准赌博机凸优化的遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将全局预算的后动作扰动模型从线性损失扩展到更一般的凸且β-光滑损失函数，研究非凸非光滑损失下的对抗性赌博机优化问题。

Method: 修改标准赌博机优化算法，开发新的分析方法以控制扰动带来的额外遗憾，明确刻画扰动预算对遗憾界的影响。

Result: 建立了期望遗憾保证，显式刻画了扰动预算的影响；无扰动时退化为标准β-光滑损失下赌博机凸优化的遗憾界。

Conclusion: 成功将全局预算后动作扰动模型从线性损失推广到凸β-光滑损失，为非凸非光滑对抗性赌博机优化提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study adversarial bandit optimization in which the loss functions may be non-convex and non-smooth. In each round, the learner selects an action and observes only the loss incurred at that action. The loss consists of an underlying convex and $β$-smooth component and an adversarial perturbation that may be chosen after observing the learner's action. The perturbations are subject to a global budget controlling their cumulative magnitude over time.
  This framework extends the globally budgeted, post-action perturbation model from underlying linear losses to general convex and $β$-smooth losses. For this broader class, we establish expected regret guarantees that explicitly characterize the effect of the perturbation budget.
  To establish these guarantees, we modify a standard bandit optimization algorithm and develop an analysis that controls the additional regret caused by the perturbations. In the absence of perturbations, our results reduce to regret guarantees for the standard bandit convex optimization setting with $β$-smooth losses.

</details>


### [68] [Score Approximation for Diffusion Models on Arbitrary Low-Dimensional Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19894)
*Xinhe Mu,Zaijiu Shang,Zhaoqi Zhou,Chuan Zhou,Qi Meng,Guiying Yan,Zhiming Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了得分函数逼近的通用定理：对于任何支撑在任意紧集（上Minkowski维数为d）上的分布，可以使用ReLU网络逼近其得分函数，且网络复杂度仅随d指数增长（而非环境维度），从而打破了维数灾难。结合反向扩散SDE求解理论，表明扩散模型能自适应处理不规则、非光滑的数据结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型理论中的得分逼近复杂度界严重依赖Lipschitz连续密度或光滑流形支撑等限制性假设，而这些假设被真实感知数据中的奇点、尖锐边界和不连通的簇所违反。因此需要建立更通用的理论框架来解释扩散模型在真实世界生成任务中的成功。

Method: 提出一种新颖的离散混合（discrete-mixture）公式化方法，利用上Minkowski维数d刻画数据分布的固有低维结构，并使用ReLU网络进行得分函数逼近。

Result: 证明了得分函数可用ReLU网络逼近，其复杂度仅随数据固有维数d指数增长，打破了环境维度的指数诅咒。

Conclusion: 扩散模型能够自适应地适应不规则、非光滑的数据结构，这解释了其在真实世界生成任务中的卓越表现。

Abstract: The remarkable success of score-based diffusion models has spurred significant efforts to establish their theoretical foundations. However, existing complexity bounds for score approximation rely heavily on restrictive assumptions like Lipschitz continuous densities or smooth manifold supports, which are routinely violated by the singularities, sharp boundaries, and disjoint clusters inherent to real-world perceptual data. This work establishes a universal score approximation theorem that works for any distribution supported on any compact set of upper Minkowski dimension $d$. Using a novel discrete-mixture formulation, we prove that the score function can be approximated with a ReLU network whose complexity grows exponentially only with $d$, thus breaking the exponential curse of ambient dimensionality. Combined with existing theories on accurately solving the backward diffusion SDE for arbitrary compact distributions, our work shows that diffusion models readily adapt to irregular, non-smooth data structures, explaining their competence in real-world generative tasks.

</details>


### [69] [ADaPT: Token-Level Decoupling for Efficient Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19919)
*Tingyun Li,Zishang Jiang,Jinyi Han,Xinyi Wang,Sihang Jiang,Han Xia,Zhaoqian Dai,Shuguang Ma,Fei Yu,Jiaqing Liang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADaPT通过令牌级双过程框架解耦效率与正确性信号，利用模式选择令牌控制快慢推理，在保持推理能力的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大推理模型依赖长链思维导致计算成本高，效率优化方法因序列级别的效率与正确性耦合而损害推理能力，需要一种能解耦两者、不惩罚长正确推理的高效方法。

Method: 提出ADaPT框架：1) 引入模式选择令牌控制快/慢推理；2) 仅对该令牌应用效率相关奖励，避免惩罚正确长推理；3) 推理时通过调整模式选择令牌的生成概率，实现效率-性能帕累托前沿的平滑控制。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，ADaPT显著降低了推理成本，同时保持了强大的推理性能，实现了效率与准确性的更好权衡。

Conclusion: ADaPT通过令牌级解耦策略有效解决了效率与正确性之间的冲突，使单一模型能够在推理时灵活调控效率-性能权衡，为大规模推理模型的实用化部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large reasoning models rely on long chain-of-thought to achieve strong performance, but applying such reasoning uniformly incurs high computational cost. Existing efficiency-oriented methods attempt to shorten or mix reasoning strategies, yet often degrade reasoning capability. We identify the root cause as sequence-level coupling between efficiency incentives and correctness optimization, which implicitly penalizes long but correct reasoning trajectories. To address this issue, we propose Adaptive Dual-Process Thinking (ADaPT), a token-level dual-process framework that explicitly decouples efficiency and correctness signals during training. ADaPT introduces a mode-selection token to control fast and slow reasoning, applying efficiency-related rewards exclusively to this token to avoid penalizing correct long reasoning while encouraging efficiency when appropriate. Moreover, ADaPT enables precise and continuous control over the efficiency-performance trade-off at inference time: by adjusting the generation probability of the mode-selection token, a single trained model can smoothly move along the efficiency-performance Pareto frontier. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ADaPT significantly reduces inference cost while maintaining strong reasoning performance across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [70] [Compositionality Emerges in a Narrow Depth-Connectivity Regime: Architecture Constraints and Solution Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19941)
*Dat H. Do,Rushi Shah,Duc V. Le,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现组合性（compositionality）只在特定的稀疏连接-深度协同的狭窄区间内涌现，偏离该区间时梯度下降会收敛到碎片化解；提出了基于相似度的剪枝（SP）和深度预测启发式方法，并给出了组合稀疏性、体积比和特征干扰界等理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合性被认为是泛化的基础，但标准梯度优化训练的模型很少展现出组合性内部结构，且其形成机制尚不清楚。本文旨在探索组合性涌现的条件及其形成机理。

Method: 通过实验发现组合性在连接稀疏性和网络深度的特定协同区域涌现；提出相似性剪枝（SP）恢复组合性连接结构，以及启发式深度预测器估计最优深度；通过组合稀疏性、体积比论证和特征干扰界建立理论框架。

Result: 组合性仅在特定的稀疏连接-深度协同区间涌现；连接轴：仅在某些特定稀疏网络中，且依赖于保留哪些连接而非单纯稀疏度；深度轴：在狭窄的、目标依赖的深度区间峰值出现，过浅或过深均失败；违反任一条件时梯度下降收敛到碎片化解。

Conclusion: 组合性的涌现需要连接稀疏性和网络深度协同满足特定条件，这为设计能自然展现出组合性的神经网络架构提供了指导。

Abstract: Compositionality is believed to be the foundation for generalization, enabling models to reuse meaningful primitives in novel combinations. Yet, models trained with standard gradient-based optimization rarely, and often only weakly, exhibit compositional internal structure, and it remains unclear how or why such compositionality forms. In this work, we show that compositionality emerges in a narrow connectivity-depth sweet spot. Along the connectivity axis, compositionality only appears in some specifically sparse networks, heavily depends on which connections remain rather than on weights' sparsity alone. Along the depth axis, compositionality emerges within a narrow, target-dependent regime, peaking at specific depths, while both shallower and deeper networks fail. When either the depth or connectivity condition is violated, gradient descent silently converges to fractured solutions rather than compositional ones. To discover and exploit this emergence, we introduce (i) similarity-based pruning (SP) to recover compositional connectivity and (ii) a heuristic depth predictor to estimate where compositionality is most likely to appear. Finally, we support these empirical findings with a theoretical framework based on compositional sparsity, volume-ratio arguments, and feature-interference bounds, explaining why compositional solutions are reachable only in a narrow depth-connectivity regime.

</details>


### [71] [Towards Graph-Based Deep Learning for Map Generalization: Insights from Building Footprints Simplification and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19956)
*Yanning Wang,Zhiyong Zhou,Zhouyu Liu,Mengni Yu,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次探索将图神经网络（GCN、GAT、GraphSAGE）应用于地图综合中的建筑足迹简化（节点移动预测）与聚合（链接预测）任务，发现GraphSAGE在链接预测上表现较好，但节点移动预测仍具挑战；聚合任务比简化更复杂，数据不平衡和后处理需求等问题仍有待解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地图综合中的建筑足迹简化与聚合是制图学基础但具有挑战性的任务，现有深度学习方法在此领域的应用尚属空白，亟需探索更有效的自动化方案。

Method: 将简化任务重构为节点移动预测、聚合任务重构为链接预测，在统一的图学习框架中评估GCN、GAT和GraphSAGE三种代表性图神经网络架构在多尺度建筑数据集上的表现。

Result: GraphSAGE在链接预测精度上表现相对优势；节点移动预测仍存在精度挑战；聚合任务比简化任务复杂度更高、更具挑战性。

Conclusion: 图深度学习在地图综合自动化方面具有潜力但仍有局限，聚合任务难度更大，数据不平衡和后处理是未来需解决的关键问题，本研究为后续方法方向提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Map generalization remains one of the fundamental tasks in cartography, especially for the simplification and aggregation of complex building footprints. This study presents the first exploratory application of graph-based deep learning to both tasks, reformulating simplification as node movement prediction and aggregation as link prediction within a unified graph learning framework. We evaluate representative graph neural network architectures (GCN, GAT, and GraphSAGE) on multi-scale building datasets, showing that GraphSAGE demonstrates relative strengths in link prediction accuracy, while also revealing persistent challenges in precise node movement prediction. Beyond quantitative performance, the results highlight that aggregation poses greater complexity and challenges than simplification, underscoring the difficulty of capturing higher-level spatial relationships in map generalization with current deep learning approaches. Although limitations such as data imbalance and the need for post-processing remain, the study provides valuable insights and methodological directions for advancing automated map generalization with deep learning approaches.

</details>


### [72] [Low-Energy Reduced RISC-V Instruction Subset Processor for Tsetlin Machine Inference at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19964)
*Chanda Gupta,Sanidhya Bhatia,Shaurya Priyadarshi,Himani Panwar,Rishad Shafik,Sudip Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向Tsetlin Machine推理的领域专用RISC-V微处理器架构及设计流程，通过指令精简和数据通路优化，在保持可编程性的同时实现了最高98%的执行时间缩减和平均29.7倍的能耗降低，在边缘AI场景下优于二值化神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Tsetlin Machine的协处理器和加速器设计虽性能高，但通常依赖紧耦合接口、微码式编程和外部主机处理器，灵活性和编程便利性受限。因此，亟需一种兼具可编程性与高效能的TM推理处理器方案。

Method: 基于RISC-V模块化结构，设计精简指令子集的处理器：先通过指令剖析（instruction profiling）指导指令精简，再针对TM推理进行数据通路和控制通路的简化，形成专用处理器架构及完整设计流程。

Result: 在多个数据集上，TM相比BNN达到相当或更高的准确率（如CIFAR-2上88.18% vs 60.0%）；执行时间最高减少98%；平均能耗降低29.7倍。

Conclusion: 该领域专用RISC-V架构有效结合了可编程性与高效能，为边缘AI系统提供了低能耗、高吞吐的TM推理解决方案。

Abstract: Tsetlin Machine (TM) is a logic-based machine learning approach that relies on simple bitwise operations and finite-state automata, which makes it attractive for edge AI deployments. Recent work has focused on co-processor and accelerator designs based on Tsetlin Machines (TMs). Although these designs achieve high performance, they typically depend on tightly coupled interfaces, microcode-style programming, and external host processors, limiting flexibility and ease of programming. In this work, we present a domain-specific RISC-V microprocessor architecture and design flow tailored for TM inference. Leveraging the modular structure of RISC-V, we design a reduced instruction subset processor that retains programmability while targeting improved performance and lower energy consumption for TM workloads. Instruction profiling is employed to guide instruction reduction, followed by datapath and control path simplifications tailored to TM inference. Both the baseline RV32IM core and the proposed reduced core are evaluated across multiple datasets and compared with Binarized Neural Networks (BNNs), which serve as a hardware-efficient baseline due to their reliance on bitwise operations during inference. Results show that TM achieves comparable or higher accuracy (e.g., up to 88.18% on CIFAR-2 compared to 60.0% for BNN) while reducing execution time by up to 98% across multiple datasets. Furthermore, the proposed design achieves an average $29.7\times$ reduction in energy consumption, demonstrating its effectiveness for programmable and efficient edge AI systems.

</details>


### [73] [Kolmogorov-Arnold Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19984)
*Juntian Huang,Jurgen Kurths,Ying Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Kolmogorov-Arnold 储层计算（KARC），将储层替换为基于 Kolmogorov-Arnold 表示定理的显式基函数展开，实现了储层计算与 KAN 网络的原理性融合，在轻量级框架下提升了动力系统预测的保真度与长程依赖捕获能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统储层计算在捕获长程依赖方面能力有限，其对超参数敏感且表示能力受限；下一代储层计算虽去除了递归但导致特征维度过快增长。需要一种兼顾轻量级、高效训练与高表达能力的方法。

Method: 受 Kolmogorov-Arnold 表示定理启发，将储层替换为显式基函数展开；证明了 KARC 是 KAN 网络的轻量级实现，保留了 KAN 的表达能力同时支持闭式高效训练。

Result: 在包含偏微分方程在内的多个挑战性基准任务上，KARC 以可比的运算成本优于现有储层计算方法；还可与生成式扩散模型集成用于文本到图像生成。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了储层计算与 KAN 网络之间的原理性桥梁，为高效、高保真的动力系统预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reservoir computing offers a lightweight framework for forecasting dynamical systems but may struggle to capture long-range dependencies due to limited representational capacity. Conventional reservoir computing recurrently uses trainable reservoirs with hyperparameter sensitivity, while the next-generation reservoir computing removes recurrence at the cost of rapidly growing feature dimensions. Here, we develop Kolmogorov-Arnold Reservoir Computing (KARC), which replaces reservoirs with explicit basis-function expansions inspired by the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem. We rigorously show that KARC is a lightweight design of Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (KANs), preserving the potential expressive capacity of KANs while admitting efficient closed-form training of reservoir computing. At comparable cost, KARC outperforms existing reservoir computing methods on challenging benchmarks including partial differential equations. It can also be integrated with generative diffusion models for text-to-image generation. This work thus establishes a principled bridge between reservoir computing and KANs, enabling efficient and high-fidelity dynamical system forecasting.

</details>


### [74] [Activation- and Influence-Aware Ranks (AIR): Function-Preserving SVD Compression for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19993)
*Nico Harder,Daniel Becking,Karsten Mueller,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIR是一个基于SVD的大语言模型压缩框架，通过引入反向信号影响度量的单次交替最小二乘扫描，在激活感知优化的基础上进一步提升低秩近似的质量，在<=60%参数保留时相比SVD-LLM(W)困惑度提升>18%，且可正交组合端到端方法实现更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SVD-based压缩方法（如SVD-LLM(W)）仅考虑激活感知的全局最优，未引入反向传播信号的影响度量，导致低秩近似在保持模型性能方面仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出Activation- and Influence-Aware Ranks (AIR)框架：在SVD-LLM(W)的激活感知最优解基础上，执行单次闭合形式的交替最小二乘（ALS）扫描，该扫描以逐元素方式整合反向信号影响度量，并具有单调下降保证。AIR是层级局部的，可与端到端方法正交组合。

Result: 在<=60%参数保留时，AIR相较SVD-LLM(W)困惑度提升>18%；达到相同质量所需校准数据减少约90%；AIR单独优于ACIP，AIR+LoRA进一步提升性能；参数节省转化为FLOP、峰值内存和每token延迟的收益。

Conclusion: AIR通过引入影响感知的ALS扫描，在激活感知最优基础上实现了更优的压缩-性能权衡，且具有良好的局部性和可组合性，是一种高效的LLM压缩方案。

Abstract: We present Activation- and Influence-Aware Ranks (AIR), an SVD-based LLM compression framework that guides each weight matrix's low-rank approximation with a backward-signal influence metric. Starting from the activation-aware optimum of SVD-LLM(W), AIR runs a single closed-form alternating least squares (ALS) sweep that integrates influence element-wise under a monotone-descent guarantee. AIR is layer-local and composes orthogonally with end-to-end methods: alone it exceeds ACIP, and AIR+LoRA outperforms it further. AIR improves perplexity over SVD-LLM(W) by >18% at <=60% parameter retention, matches its quality with ~90% less calibration data, and turns parameter savings into FLOP, peak-memory, and per-token latency gains.

</details>


### [75] [Connect the Dots: Training LLMs for Long-Lifecycle Agents with Cross-Domain Generalization Via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20002)
*Yanxi Chen,Weijie Shi,Yuexiang Xie,Boyi Hu,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Connect the Dots"(CoD)框架，通过端到端强化学习训练大语言模型获得元能力，使其能在长期部署中持续探索环境、从经验中学习并自我更新上下文，从而在后续任务中表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期生命周期AI代理需要一种元能力：在环境中连续执行任务的同时，能自主探索、从自身经验中学习并迭代更新上下文。现有LLM训练方法缺乏对这种持续性自我演化能力的建模和训练。

Method: 设计端到端RL训练框架，包含：(1)长rollout序列的RL算法（GRPO风格）与细粒度信用分配；(2)专门用于激励和评估该元能力的任务与环境。训练过程交织"求解任务"和"更新上下文"两个类型的episode。

Result: 实验验证了端到端RL训练在CoD设置中的有效性，展示了该元能力在训练域内、跨域以及从CoD到Ralph-loop设置中的分布外泛化潜力。

Conclusion: CoD框架连接了多项先前工作，为提升LLM和AI代理能力开辟了新方向，并开源了实现代码。

Abstract: This work presents a general framework for training large language models (LLMs) to "Connect the Dots" (CoD), a meta-capability required by long-lifecycle agents: as an LLM-based AI agent gets deployed in an environment, it solves a long sequence of tasks while continuously exploring the environment, learning from its own experiences, and iteratively self-updating its context about the environment, thereby achieving progressively better performance on future tasks conditioned on the updated context. Major components of the CoD framework include: (1) algorithm design and infrastructure for end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL) with long rollout sequences interleaving solve-task and update-context episodes; (2) tasks and environments for incentivizing and eliciting the targeted meta-capability in LLMs during training, as well as for faithfully measuring progress during evaluation. We present proof-of-concept implementations of the CoD framework, including a GRPO-style RL algorithm with fine-grained credit assignment, as well as tasks and environments tailored to the targeted meta-capability (rather than domain-specific LLM capabilities or standard task-by-task RL). Empirical results validate the efficacy of end-to-end RL training in the CoD setting, and demonstrate the potential for out-of-distribution generalization -- within the training domains, across different domains, and from CoD to Ralph-loop settings -- of the elicited meta-capability. Our investigation of CoD connects several lines of prior works, and opens up new opportunities for advancing LLMs and AI agents. To facilitate further research and applications, we release our implementations at \url{https://github.com/agentscope-ai/Trinity-RFT/tree/research/cod/examples/research_cod}.

</details>


### [76] [StreamKL: Fast and Memory-Efficient KL Divergence for Boosting Attention Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20005)
*Guangda Liu,Yiquan Wang,Chengwei Li,Wenhao Chen,Jing Lin,Yiwu Yao,Danning Ke,Wenchao Ding,Jieru Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StreamKL 是首个用于注意力 KL 散度计算的融合 GPU 原语，通过在线公式化和 tile-wise 流式处理，将注意力蒸馏的额外 HBM 开销从 $O(N_QN_K)$ 降至 $O(1)$，前向/反向分别实现高达 $43\times$ 和 $14\times$ 的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力蒸馏方法在计算 KL 散度时需要物化两个注意力分布，导致 $O(N_QN_K)$ 的内存和 IO 开销，在长上下文场景下变得不可接受。

Method: 提出了一种新的在线公式用于耦合的两分布 KL 缩减，实现了单次前向内核（将 query-key tile 通过片上 SRAM 流式处理）；反向传播时按 tile 重计算注意力概率，避免存储二次中间结果；并设计了高效的 GPU 内核与专用优化。

Result: 前向传播加速高达 $43\times$，反向传播加速高达 $14\times$；额外 HBM 占用从 $O(N_QN_K)$ 降至 $O(1)$，支持单 GPU 上的长上下文蒸馏。

Conclusion: StreamKL 有效消除了注意力蒸馏中的二次内存瓶颈，使其在长序列场景下变得高效可行。

Abstract: Attention distillation, which trains one attention distribution to match another by minimizing their Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence, is widely used in knowledge distillation, model compression, continual learning, and sparse-attention LLM training. However, existing approaches materialize both attention distributions before computing the KL reduction, incurring $O(N_QN_K)$ memory and IO costs that become prohibitive at long context lengths. We present StreamKL, the first fused GPU primitive for attention KL divergence that eliminates this quadratic materialization. StreamKL derives a novel online formulation for the coupled two-distribution KL reduction, enabling a single one-pass forward kernel that streams query-key tiles through on-chip SRAM. For the backward pass, StreamKL recomputes attention probabilities tile-by-tile, avoiding storage of quadratic intermediates. We further design and implement efficient GPU kernels with dedicated optimizations. Experiments show StreamKL delivers up to $43\times$ and $14\times$ speedups over baseline methods in the forward and backward passes, respectively. Most importantly, StreamKL reduces the extra HBM footprint of attention distillation from $O(N_QN_K)$ to $O(1)$, enabling long-context distillation on a single GPU.

</details>


### [77] [VIMPO: Value-Implicit Policy Optimization for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20008)
*Zhewei Kang,Aosong Feng,Sergey Levine,Dawn Song,Xuandong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VIMPO是一种无需评论家（critic-free）的策略优化方法，通过KL正则化强化学习的最优条件推导出策略隐含的价值函数，在数学推理基准上优于GRPO，尤其在竞赛级评测和噪声奖励场景下表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RLVR方法在简洁性与信用分配之间存在权衡：GRPO等组相对方法避免训练评论家但仅分配轨迹级优势，而Actor-Critic方法虽提供更密集的学习信号却需要训练值函数，引入额外的不稳定性。作者希望提出一种既保持评论家-free的简洁性，又能实现更精细信用分配的方法。

Method: VIMPO从KL正则化强化学习的最优条件出发，推导出策略隐含的值函数（Policy-Implied Value Function）。对于自回归生成，该值递归可通过策略-参考对数比率（policy-reference log-ratios）表示，并以轨迹末端无剩余奖励的终止条件锚定。由此得到一个简单的值损失函数，无需训练评论家即可融入结果级可验证奖励。同时推导出评论家-free的演员优势，通过值损失（奖励融入）与PPO风格演员更新（策略改进）分离优化。

Result: 在MATH-500、AIME 2024、AIME 2025和OlympiadBench等数学推理基准上，VIMPO全面超越GRPO，在竞赛级评测（如AIME）上提升尤为显著。在噪声奖励场景下，VIMPO仍保持对GRPO的稳定优势。

Conclusion: 策略隐含值优化（VIMPO）能够在保持评论家-free训练实用简洁性的同时，提供更精细的信用分配，是一种兼顾简洁性与信用分配的RLVR优化方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become a central tool for improving the reasoning ability of large language models, but current methods face a trade-off between simplicity and credit assignment. Group-relative methods such as GRPO avoid training a critic, but typically assign a trajectory-level advantage to every token. Actor-critic methods provide denser learning signals, but require a learned value function with its own training instability. We introduce VIMPO, a critic-free policy optimization method that derives a policy-implied value function from the optimality conditions of KL-regularized reinforcement learning. For autoregressive generation, the resulting value recurrence can be written in terms of policy-reference log-ratios and anchored by the terminal condition that no future reward remains at the end of a trajectory. This gives a simple value loss that incorporates outcome-level verifiable rewards without training a critic. The same derivation also yields a critic-free actor advantage, allowing VIMPO to separate reward incorporation through the value loss from policy improvement through a PPO-style actor update. On mathematical RLVR benchmarks, VIMPO improves over GRPO across MATH-500, AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and OlympiadBench, with especially larger gains on competition-style evaluations. Under noisy rewards, VIMPO retains a consistent advantage over GRPO, suggesting that policy-implied value optimization can provide finer credit assignment while preserving the practical simplicity of critic-free training.

</details>


### [78] [Self-Adaptive Scale Handling for Forecasting Time Series with Scale Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20010)
*Xu Zhang,Zhengang Huang,Yunzhi Wu,Xun Lu,Erpeng Qi,Yunkai Chen,Zhongya Xue,Peng Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应尺度处理（AS）模块，解决时间序列预测中不同序列数量级差异（尺度异质性）问题，通过尺度校准（SC）和尺度选择（SS）机制，在保留语义区分性的同时减少逆尺度误差，在蚂蚁财富和支付宝的真实基金销售数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测研究主要针对尺度同质数据，但工业场景中不同序列常存在数量级差异（尺度异质性）。现有归一化方法（全局归一化压缩低尺度信号，窗口归一化破坏语义区分性并放大逆尺度误差）无法有效处理该问题，因此需要一种既能保留语义信息又能减少逆尺度误差的自适应尺度处理方法。

Method: 提出自适应尺度处理（AS）模块，包含两个子组件：1）尺度校准（SC）：通过神经网络学习校准先验均值尺度因子；2）尺度选择（SS）：决定是否应用校准或保留原始因子，避免过度校准。AS模块可无缝集成到现有TSF模型中。

Result: 在蚂蚁财富和支付宝的真实基金销售数据集上进行实验，AS模块能无缝集成到主流TSF模型中，并持续提升其预测性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的AS模块有效解决了时间序列预测中的尺度异质性问题，通过自适应学习尺度因子，兼顾了语义区分性与逆尺度误差控制，并在实际工业数据上验证了其通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Current time series forecasting (TSF) research predominantly focuses on scale-homogeneous data, where different time series share similar numerical magnitude ranges. However, in real-world industrial scenarios such as financial product sales, different time series often differ by orders of magnitude (scale heterogeneity). Since these series share similar temporal patterns, joint modeling is desirable for better data utilization, yet existing scaling methods either compress low-scale signals (global normalization) or destroy semantic discriminability and amplify inverse-scaling errors (window-based scaling). This paper proposes a self-Adaptive Scale-handling (AS) module that learns adaptive scale factors tailored to each input, preserving semantic discriminability while reducing inverse-scaling errors. AS consists of Scale Calibrating (SC), which calibrates prior mean scaling factors through neural networks, and Scaling Selection (SS), which decides whether to apply calibration or retain the original factor, avoiding over-calibration. Experiments on real-world fund sales datasets from Ant Fortune and Alipay show that AS seamlessly integrates into popular TSF models and consistently improves their performance. The code and dataset are available at the link https://github.com/Meteor-Stars/ASTSF.

</details>


### [79] [Hierarchical Control in Multi-Agent Games: LLM-based Planning and RL Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20014)
*Jannik Hösch,Alessandro Sestini,Florian Fuchs,Amir Baghi,Joakim Bergdahl,Konrad Tollmar,Jean-Philippe Barrette-LaPierre,Linus Gisslén*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分层架构，使用预训练大语言模型作为集中式策略控制器来协调多个专用RL技能策略，在2v2对抗环境中达到与手工行为树相当的性能，并显著优于端到端训练的RL，同时被用户感知为更类人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在复杂多智能体环境中面临稀疏奖励、大状态动作空间和协调策略学习困难等挑战，难以扩展。

Method: 分层架构：上层是预训练LLM作为集中式战略控制器，负责选择下层专用的RL技能策略；下层RL策略负责反应式低层执行。

Result: 在2v2 King of the Hill环境中，LLM+RL系统与手工行为树胜率相当（46.4% vs 51.5%, p=0.103），两者均显著优于Flat RL；用户研究中60%的参与者认为LLM+RL代理最类人（p=0.027）。

Conclusion: 预训练LLM推理可以有效编排预训练RL技能，实现竞争性多智能体协调和更高的感知可信度，且无需手工规则工程。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved strong performance in sequential decision-making, yet scaling to complex multi-agent environments remains challenging due to sparse rewards, large state-action spaces, and the difficulty of learning coordinated strategies. We propose a hierarchical architecture where a pretrained large language model (LLM) acts as a centralized strategic controller that selects among specialized RL skill policies for a team of agents, while RL policies handle reactive low-level execution. We evaluate this hybrid system in a competitive 2v2 King of the Hill environment against behavior tree (BT) and \emph{``Flat''} RL (end-to-end training without skill decomposition) baselines. The LLM+RL system achieves task performance statistically equivalent to hand-crafted BT (46.4\% vs 51.5\% win rate, $p=0.103$) while both significantly outperform Flat RL trained without skill decomposition. A user study ($n=15$) reveals that 60\% of participants perceive LLM+RL agents as the most human-like ($p=0.027$), citing behavioral adaptability and tactical variability. These results demonstrate that pretrained LLM reasoning can effectively orchestrate pretrained RL skills, achieving competitive multi-agent coordination and superior perceived believability without manual rule engineering.

</details>


### [80] [Adaptive Distance-Aware Trunk Deep Operator Learning for Long-Span Roadway Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20015)
*Bilal Ahmed,Diab W. Abueidda,Waleed El-Sekelly,Tarek Abdoun,Mostafa E. Mobasher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应主干DeepONet（AD-DeepONet）框架，用于大跨度桥梁在车辆荷载下的局部结构响应快速预测。该框架通过KNN策略动态构建荷载依赖的学习域，利用距离感知特征增强主干网络，并通过刚度-informed Schur补公式实现全场重构。在基准桥和实际 Mussafah 桥上验证，相对误差低于5%，响应评估时间缩短约60倍（推理速度比FEM快四个数量级），支持快速生成影响线/面，适用于大型桥梁分析与数字孪生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大跨度桥梁在车辆荷载下呈现高度局部的结构响应，传统有限元分析计算成本高昂，难以满足影响面生成和结构数字孪生等应用需求。现有科学机器学习方法难以准确捕捉这种局部化响应特征。

Method: (1) 采用KNN策略动态构建荷载依赖的学习域，使网络聚焦于结构影响区；(2) 引入距离感知特征增强主干网络，编码荷载与结构节点的几何关系；(3) 通过刚度-informed Schur补公式实现物理信息驱动的全场重构，将自适应节点预测扩展到整个结构域；(4) 使用降阶等效壳模型生成响应训练数据，保持全局行为的同时大幅降低计算成本。

Result: 在基准桥模型和实际Mussafah桥上验证，相对误差低于5%；总响应评估时间（含全场重构）缩短约60倍；排除后处理重构步骤，AD-DeepONet推理速度比FEM快四个数量级；支持任意车辆荷载配置下全场响应、影响线和影响面的快速生成。

Conclusion: 提出的AD-DeepONet框架能高效准确地预测大跨度桥梁局部结构响应，在计算效率上实现显著提升，具备在大规模桥梁分析和数字孪生应用中的强大潜力。

Abstract: Long-span roadway bridges exhibit highly localized structural responses under vehicular loading, making repeated FE analysis computationally expensive for applications such as influence surface generation and structural digital twins. Existing SciML approaches struggle to accurately capture these localized responses. To address this challenge, this study proposes an adaptive-trunk DeepONet for localized structural response prediction in large-scale bridge systems. The framework dynamically constructs a load-dependent learning domain using a KNN strategy, allowing the network to focus on structural influence zones. The trunk network is further enhanced using distance-aware features that encode the geometric relationship between the load and structural nodes. A physics-based full-field reconstruction is incorporated through a stiffness-informed Schur complement formulation, enabling predictions at adaptive nodes to be extended to the entire structural domain. To enable scalable training, response data are generated using a reduced-order equivalent shell model that preserves the dominant global behavior while significantly reducing computational cost. The proposed framework is validated on both a benchmark bridge model and the real-world Mussafah Bridge. Results show that the method achieves FEM-level accuracy with relative errors below 5%, while reducing the total response evaluation time (including full-field reconstruction) by approximately 60x; excluding the post-processing reconstruction step, the AD-DeepONet inference is up to four orders of magnitude faster than FEM. In addition, the framework enables rapid generation of full-field responses, influence lines, and influence surfaces under arbitrary vehicular loading configurations, demonstrating strong potential for large-scale bridge analysis and digital twin applications.

</details>


### [81] [Exploring the potential of AlphaEarth and TESSERA embeddings for Fine-scale Local Climate Zone Mapping: A case study across five cities in Switzerland](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20034)
*Htet Yamin Ko Ko,Clement Atzberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用TESSERA和AlphaEarth等地球观测基础模型的预计算嵌入特征，结合注意力U-Net架构，将粗分辨率（~100米）的局部气候区（LCZ）地图上采样至10米分辨率。实验在瑞士五个城市进行，结果表明TESSERA嵌入特征表现优于传统Sentinel-1/2复合数据，具有良好的区域迁移性和可扩展性，但跨年份迁移仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多城市仍使用约100米粗分辨率的LCZ记录，无法满足精细尺度城市研究（如气候建模、风险评估、可持续设计）的需求，亟需高效的超分辨率方法。

Method: 对比TESSERA和AlphaEarth的预计算嵌入特征与传统Sentinel-1/2复合数据，采用基于注意力机制的U-Net架构，在瑞士五个城市进行三项实验：多城市迁移性评估、高分辨率参考数据影响分析、年际物候变化的时间鲁棒性测试。

Result: 所有数据集在前两项实验中测试IoU分别达到0.59-0.69和0.77-0.82；TESSERA在两个设定下均优于S1S2和AlphaEarth；嵌入特征模型的跨年迁移仍存在挑战。

Conclusion: EO基础模型嵌入特征可替代耗时的手工特征工程，结合位置感知注意力U-Net能增强区域迁移性和可扩展性，有助于开发全球城市气候应用的精细尺度LCZ地图；提升参考数据质量是进一步精度提升的关键。

Abstract: Understanding urban spatial morphology is critical for climate modeling, risk assessment, and sustainable urban design, and Local Climate Zone (LCZ) mapping provides the basic framework for this. However, many cities still use coarse ~100-m resolution LCZ records, which are unsuitable for fine-scale urban research. In this study, precomputed embeddings from TESSERA (Feng et al., 2025) and AlphaEarth (Brown et al., 2025) are compared to traditional Sentinel-1/2 (S1S2) composites in five Swiss cities to see if they can upscale coarse LCZ maps to 10-m resolution using an attention-based U-Net. Three experiments assess multi-city transferability, the impact of higher-resolution reference data, and temporal robustness to year-to-year phenology changes. We find that all datasets achieve strong performance with test data Intersection-over-Union (IoU) ranging from 0.59-0.69 and 0.77-0.82 in the first two experiments. TESSERA consistently outperforms both S1S2 and AlphaEarth across both settings As expected, we find that the transfer of embedding-based models from one year to another remains an open challenge. Overall, however, our results demonstrate the promising potential of embeddings derived from EO foundation models to reduce time consuming preprocessing, respectively, manual feature engineering tasks and to guide a universal deep learning-based LCZ mapping workflow. When combined with a simple location-aware attention U-Net architecture, the embeddings enhance regional transferability and scalability, supporting the development of comprehensive and reproducible fine-scale LCZ maps for global urban climate applications Improving reference data quality remains the strongest lever for further accuracy gains.

</details>


### [82] [Alzheimer's Disease Diagnosis using a Multimodal Approach with 3D MRI and PET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20037)
*Loukas Ilias,Anthi-Maria Vozinaki,Christos Ntanos,Dimitris Askounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将3D卷积特征提取器与三种融合策略（拼接、门控多模态单元、门控自注意力）以及稀疏门控混合专家分类器结合，用于阿尔茨海默病早期诊断，在NC vs. MCI、MCI vs. AD和NC vs. AD三个二分类任务上取得较好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型通常采用静态拼接方式融合MRI和PET数据，并对所有受试者应用相同的计算，这限制了模型对患者/站点异质性的鲁棒性，且可能造成计算浪费。

Method: 采用3D卷积特征提取器提取MRI和PET特征，对比三种融合策略（拼接、GMU、门控自注意力），使用稀疏门控混合专家分类器进行输入自适应路由，并利用Grad-CAM进行可视化解释。

Result: GMU在NC vs. MCI和NC vs. AD上分别达到80.46%和95.47%的准确率；门控自注意力在MCI vs. AD上达到82.08%；消融实验表明移除MoE会降低所有任务的准确率。

Conclusion: 输入自适应的多模态建模能够有效利用MRI和PET的互补信息，提升阿尔茨海默病诊断性能。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is an irreversible neurodegenerative disorder and a leading cause of death worldwide. Early diagnosis plays an important part especially at the Mild Cognitive Impairment stage, where timely intervention can help slow its progression before it advances to AD. Neuroimaging data, like Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) scans, can help detect brain changes early by providing structural and functional brain changes related to the disease. Yet, many multimodal models still fuse MRI and PET with static concatenation and apply identical computation to all subjects, which limits robustness to patient/site heterogeneity and can waste computation. To address these limitations, we present the first study of combining 3D convolutional feature extractors with three fusion strategies - concatenation, Gated Multimodal Unit (GMU), and gated self-attention - and a sparsely gated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) classifier that performs input-adaptive routing, activating only the most informative experts per case. Finally, we utilize Grad-CAM to visualize disease-related regions, ensuring model interpretability. Experiments are performed across three binary classification tasks (NC vs. MCI, MCI vs. AD, and NC vs. AD). Results show that GMU achieves accuracies of 80.46 % (NC vs. MCI) and 95.47 % (NC vs. AD), while gated self-attention attains 82.08 % on MCI vs. AD. Ablations show that removing the MoE consistently degrades accuracy across all tasks. These findings underscore the value of input-adaptive, multimodal modeling for AD diagnosis by leveraging the complementary nature of MRI and PET.

</details>


### [83] [Comparative Study of Neural Surrogate Architectures for Autoregressive Prediction of Internal Battery States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20053)
*Gihyun Lee,Thorben Menne,Simon Olma,Jakob Hilgert,Sangyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种神经网络架构（MLP、ResNet、U-Net、FNO）作为自回归状态转移算子，用于替代计算昂贵的DFN锂电池模型。在统一训练框架下，U-Net凭借其多尺度空间归纳偏置表现最佳，300步自回归 rollout 后内部状态平均 nRMSE 为3%，并实现5.38倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFN模型高保真但计算成本过高，难以实时部署及扩展到电池包和车队级应用；现有机器学习代理大多学习特定工况下的解近似，缺乏对状态演化动力学的泛化能力。

Method: 在统一框架下（多步展开和电流条件化），将MLP、ResNet、U-Net、FNO四种架构构建为自回归状态转移算子，预测完整DFN内部状态，通过控制变量法隔离空间归纳偏置的影响。

Result: U-Net在300步自回归 rollout 后平均最终步nRMSE为3%，优于其他架构；同时相比数值求解器实现5.38倍加速。

Conclusion: 空间归纳偏置是代理模型性能的关键决定因素，U-Net的多尺度特征层级结构有效捕捉了电池内部状态的空间演化规律，推动了下一代电池管理系统和数字孪生中内部状态可观测性的发展。

Abstract: The Doyle-Fuller-Newman (DFN) model resolves internal electrochemical states in lithium-ion batteries with high fidelity. However, the numerical solution of its governing equations is computationally prohibitive for real-time deployment, limiting scalability from individual cells to pack and fleet-scale applications. While machine learning surrogates can substantially reduce inference latency through GPU acceleration, most existing approaches learn solution approximations tied to specific operating conditions rather than learning generalizable state-evolution dynamics. This work presents a systematic comparison of four neural network architectures (MLP, ResNet, U-Net, FNO) formulated as autoregressive state-transition operators that predict full DFN internal states across a wide range of operating conditions. To ensure a controlled architectural comparison, all models are trained under a unified framework using multi-step unrolling and current-conditioning, isolating the impact of spatial inductive bias. Results demonstrate that the U-Net's multi-scale feature hierarchy achieves a mean final-step nRMSE of 3% averaged across all internal state variables after 300-step autoregressive rollouts, while providing a 5.38x speed-up over the numerical solver. These findings highlight spatial inductive bias as a critical determinant of surrogate performance, advancing the development of surrogates for internal state observability for next-generation battery management systems and digital twins.

</details>


### [84] [PaAno+: Multiscale Encoding and Cross-Variable Attention for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20055)
*Youji Zhu,Hongbing Wang,Wenchao Liu,Xiaodong Liu,Xiangguang Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PaAno，一种基于补丁导向表示学习的轻量级高效时序异常检测模型。该模型通过多尺度卷积特征提取骨干、跨尺度自适应注意力聚合、跨变量融合注意力模块以及时序补丁窗口排序的预训练任务，在TSB-AD基准上实现了SOTA检测精度，同时保持低计算开销，适合资源受限终端的实时异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于Transformer和大模型的检测方法计算开销过大，而现有轻量级替代方案存在特征提取不充分、多变量依赖关系建模不足的问题。

Method: 1) 编码器采用多尺度卷积核构建特征提取骨干捕获层次化时序特征；2) 跨尺度自适应注意力聚合+残差连接优化稳定特征表示；3) 跨变量融合注意力模块显式建模变量间相关性；4) 提出基于时序补丁窗口排序的预训练任务，结合三元组损失优化补丁嵌入空间。

Result: 在TSB-AD基准上，PaAno在单变量和多变量任务上均达到SOTA检测精度，在VUS-PR等指标上取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: PaAno以紧凑网络设计实现了良好的计算效率，可在资源受限终端上部署进行实时异常推断。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection has significant practical value for industrial and medical monitoring, as well as other critical domains. Current Transformer- and large-model-based detection approaches incur excessive computational overhead, while existing lightweight alternatives are constrained by insufficient feature extraction and inadequate modeling of dependencies across multivariate variables. To mitigate the above drawbacks, this study develops a lightweight, efficient anomaly detection model, dubbed PaAno, within the patch-oriented representation learning paradigm. In the encoder module, a multiscale feature-extraction backbone is constructed using convolutional kernels with differentiated receptive fields to capture hierarchical temporal characteristics; subsequent cross-scale adaptive attention aggregation, combined with residual connection optimization, further stabilizes feature representation learning. A cross-variable fusion attention module is embedded to explicitly characterize inter-variable correlations, empowering the model to identify anomalous patterns amid intricate operational conditions. Moreover, a novel pretext task based on temporal patch-window sorting is customized to uncover intrinsic structural properties of time series, and triplet loss is leveraged to optimize the patch embedding space for enhanced feature discrimination. Extensive experiments on the TSB-AD benchmark demonstrate that the proposed PaAno achieves state-of-the-art detection accuracy on both univariate and multivariate tasks, yielding significant performance gains across evaluation metrics, including VUS-PR, relative to the original PaAno. Leveraging a compact network design, the presented model achieves favorable computational efficiency, enabling deployment on resource-limited terminals for real-time anomaly inference.

</details>


### [85] [What Makes Effective Supervision in Latent Chain-of-Thought: An Information-Theoretic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20075)
*Xinghao Chen,Chak Tou Leong,Wenjin Guo,Jian Wang,Wenjie Li,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度分析了Latent CoT中的双重崩溃问题（梯度衰减与表征漂移），将过程监督分解为轨迹监督和空间监督两个维度，提出统一潜变量探针（ULP）量化潜变量轨迹与显式推理步骤间的互信息，揭示了信息-性能绑定关系，论证了生成式重建优于刚性几何压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Latent CoT虽然避免了显式离散推理的冗长问题，但结果监督提供的学习信号弱，导致潜变量轨迹容易发生语义漂移，因此需要更有效的监督机制来保证潜空间推理的鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 从信息论视角分析Latent CoT的失败原因，识别为双重崩溃（梯度衰减+表征漂移）；2) 将过程监督分解为轨迹监督（密集逐步推理信号）和空间监督（保持潜流形语义结构）；3) 提出统一潜变量探针（ULP）量化潜轨迹与显式推理步骤间的互信息。

Result: 实验揭示了信息-性能绑定关系：推理准确度取决于潜链中保留的信息保真度；刚性几何压缩会导致推理空间崩溃，而生成式重建能更灵活地保持信息容量。

Conclusion: 为潜变量推理监督提供了原则性框架，建议从几何模仿转向互信息最大化。

Abstract: Latent Chain-of-Thought (CoT) internalizes reasoning within continuous hidden states, offering a promising alternative to verbose discrete reasoning traces. However, robust latent reasoning remains difficult because outcome supervision provides weak learning signals and leaves latent trajectories prone to semantic drift. In this work, we analyze Latent CoT from an information-theoretic perspective and identify this failure as a dual collapse: gradient attenuation along the optimization path and representational drift in the latent space. We further decompose process supervision into two complementary dimensions: Trajectory Supervision, which injects dense stepwise reasoning signals, and Space Supervision, which preserves the semantic structure of the latent manifold. Our analysis shows that rigid geometric compression can collapse the reasoning space, whereas generative reconstruction provides a more flexible semantic anchor that better preserves information capacity. To measure these effects, we introduce the Unified Latent Probe (ULP), which quantifies the mutual information between latent trajectories and explicit reasoning steps. Experiments reveal a clear Information-Performance Binding: reasoning accuracy depends on the information fidelity preserved in the latent chain. These findings provide a principled framework for latent reasoning supervision and suggest shifting from geometric imitation toward mutual information maximization. Our code is available at \href{https://github.com/EIT-NLP/Supervision-in-Latent-CoT}{this repository}.

</details>


### [86] [Sensorimotor World Models: Perception for Action via Inverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20104)
*Petr Ivashkov,Randall Balestriero,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出传感器运动世界模型（SMWM），通过逆动力学正则化训练端到端潜在世界模型，解决了表示崩溃问题并诱导出与动作对齐的表示，在无需奖励、冻结编码器或复杂正则化器的情况下，实现了紧凑可解释的潜在空间和竞争性的规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型通常难以端到端训练，因为仅以预测未来状态为目标会导致表示崩溃。同时，面向行动的感知要求表示应根据行为相关性而非视觉保真度来塑造。现有方法依赖冻结编码器、指数移动平均或复杂正则化器，缺乏简洁统一的解决方案。

Method: SMWM在潜世界模型训练中引入逆动力学正则化：强制潜在状态保留导致状态转换的动作信息，从而偏向于环境中的可控自由度，丢弃不可控的干扰因素。该正则化同时解决了表示崩溃和动作对齐两个问题。

Result: 在简单2D和3D控制任务上，SMWM学习了紧凑可解释的潜在空间，并实现了具有竞争力的规划性能。模型可从离线、无奖励轨迹中稳定训练，无需冻结编码器、指数移动平均或复杂潜在正则化器。

Conclusion: 逆动力学正则化是一种简洁有效的端到端训练潜世界模型的方法，能够自然地诱导出面向行动的表示，在表示稳定性和任务相关性之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Perception for action suggests that representations of the world should be shaped not by visual fidelity alone, but by their relevance for actions. At the same time, latent JEPA-style world models advocate learning compact predictive states from high-dimensional observations to facilitate the prediction of future states, but end-to-end training of these models is nontrivial because representations may collapse if our only goal is to construct a latent state that is easy to predict. We introduce a sensorimotor world model (SMWM): a latent world model trained end-to-end with inverse dynamics regularization. This single regularizer addresses both issues: it prevents representation collapse and induces action-aligned representations. By forcing latent states to preserve information about the action underlying a transition, it biases the model toward the controllable degrees of freedom of the environment while discarding uncontrollable distractors. This yields stable latent world models trained from offline, reward-free trajectories, without frozen encoders, exponential moving averages, or complex latent regularizers. Empirically, SMWM learns compact, interpretable latent spaces and enables competitive planning performance across simple 2D and 3D control tasks.

</details>


### [87] [Quantile of Means: A Bonus-Free Ensemble Method for Minimax Optimal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20107)
*Asaf Cassel,Aviv Rosenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分位数集成的方法用于有限时域MDP，无需计数即可实现最优方差依赖的遗憾界，为RL中的集成探索提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于计数的RL算法虽理论上最优但难以实用，而集成方法在实践中有效却缺乏理论支撑。本文旨在弥合理论与实践之间的差距。

Method: 基于多臂老虎机中的集成方法，提出面向有限时域马尔可夫决策过程的分位数集成方法，无需显式计数即可驱动探索。

Result: 实现了最优方差依赖的遗憾界，为无计数的集成探索方法提供了理论保证。

Conclusion: 本文为集成探索在RL中的应用提供了理论依据，证明了简单、无计数的分位数集成方法在理论上也能达到最优性能。

Abstract: Optimal Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms typically rely on carefully constructed count-based uncertainty estimates to drive exploration. Although theoretically sound, such estimates are hard to compute in practical settings and therefore offer limited insight for designing exploration heuristics. Meanwhile, ensembling has emerged as a practical approach, but remains without theoretical justification. Building on a recent ensemble-based method for Multi-Armed Bandits, we propose a quantile-based ensemble method for finite-horizon Markov Decision Processes (MDPs). Our simple count-free approach achieves optimal variance-dependent regret bounds, providing theoretical grounding for ensemble-based exploration in RL.

</details>


### [88] [When Calibration Fails the Vulnerable Hospital: Federated Conformal Risk Control via Risk-Curve Shrinkage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20115)
*Nafis Fuad Shahid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于收缩的联邦式保形风险控制（CRC）协议，在联邦部署中平衡各医疗机构的分割质量覆盖与预测集效率，首次在真实多机构脑肿瘤数据上量化了朴素池化CRC和局部CRC的缺陷，并通过单一超参数n0实现最坏情况覆盖与效率的平滑权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习部署中，标准CRC方法将所有站点的校准分数池化为单一阈值，但这种方法无法保证各独立机构的覆盖合规性；而按站点独立进行局部CRC虽能恢复覆盖，却导致预测集膨胀83倍，临床不可用。目前缺乏一种既能保证各机构覆盖又能保持预测集效率的联邦CRC方案。

Method: 提出基于收缩的联邦CRC协议：每个站点仅向服务器传输其经验风险曲线（G个标量），服务器计算经收缩正则化的各站点阈值。通过单一超参数n0平滑权衡最坏情况覆盖与预测集效率；采用留一站点法敏感性分析确定n0=19。还验证了直接拉格朗日优化覆盖预算会失败，以及有限样本校正项的不可或缺性。

Result: 在FeTS-2022数据集（1,251名被试，20个机构）上，朴素池化CRC使40%的机构违反覆盖，最差站点假阴性率超出目标7.8个百分点；局部CRC预测集膨胀83倍；所提收缩协议在n0=19时仅2.7/20个站点违规，预测集拉伸仅2.0倍。去掉有限样本校正项后违规数增至三倍。

Conclusion: 所提收缩联邦CRC协议能有效在联邦场景下平衡多机构的分割质量覆盖与预测集效率，且不泄露患者级图像、掩膜或逐体积分数。边际CRC保证在站点混合假设下仍成立，不同目标和种子的验证确认了每站点覆盖的有效性。

Abstract: Conformal risk control (CRC) provides distribution-free guarantees on segmentation quality by calibrating a prediction-set threshold on held-out data. In federated deployments, the standard approach pools calibration scores across sites into a single threshold. We provide the first quantification, on real multi-institutional brain tumor data (FeTS-2022, 1,251 subjects, 20 institutions), showing that this naive pooled CRC protects the average hospital but violates coverage at 40% of individual institutions, with the worst site exceeding the target false-negative rate by 7.8 percentage points. The naive alternative, per-site local CRC, largely restores coverage but inflates prediction sets by 83x, rendering them clinically useless. We propose a shrinkage-based federated CRC protocol: each site transmits only its empirical risk curve (G scalars) to a server, which computes a shrinkage-regularized threshold per site. A single hyperparameter n0 smoothly trades worst-case coverage for prediction-set efficiency; leave-one-site-out sensitivity analysis identifies n0=19, achieving 2.7/20 violations at 2.0x stretch. We further show that direct Lagrangian optimization of coverage budgets fails, concentrating risk on vulnerable hospitals, and that the finite-sample correction term is essential: removing it triples violations. The marginal CRC guarantee is preserved by construction under the stated site-mixture assumption; per-site coverage is validated across four targets with three seeds. No patient-level images, masks, or per-volume scores leave any site.

</details>


### [89] [Multi-Modal Contrastive Learning for Implicit Earth Embeddings via Location Tying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20167)
*Jonathan Hecht,Lukas Arzoumanidis,Ziyue Li,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种多模态对比学习架构MELT和SALT，用于空间位置编码器的自监督预训练。实验表明增加模态数量并不持续提升性能，位置编码器本身是主要瓶颈，MELT在训练稳定性和扩展性上优于SALT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间预测任务常因缺乏高质量标注数据而受限，现有自监督对比学习方法通常只将地理坐标与单一额外模态对齐，缺乏支持多模态融合的预训练框架。

Method: 提出两种多模态对比学习架构：(1) MELT (Multimodal Embedding via Location Tying) 通过位置绑定实现多模态嵌入；(2) SALT (Sequential Alternating Location Training) 通过顺序交替训练实现位置学习。两种方法均能利用非配对地理空间数据扩展至两种以上模态。

Result: 两种方法在四个下游任务上达到与最强双模态基线SATCLIP相当的性能。增加模态数量并未持续提升性能，对比学习目标在早期即达到峰值，无论模态多样性或预训练数据量如何。

Conclusion: 位置编码器是当前的主要限制因素，MELT比SALT提供更稳定的训练，是未来扩展的更优基础。

Abstract: Spatial prediction tasks are often limited by a lack of high-quality labelled ground-truth observations. To overcome this challenge, self-supervised pre-training is a possible solution, with contrastive learning dominant for location encoders. Those approaches usually align geographic coordinates with just one additional modality. We propose two multimodal contrastive learning architectures: Multimodal Embedding via Location Tying (MELT) and Sequential Alternating Location Training (SALT). These architectures expand this framework beyond two modalities by utilising unpaired geospatial data. Both methods are technically viable and match the performance of the strongest two-modality baseline (SATCLIP) across four downstream tasks. However, increasing the number of modalities does not consistently improve performance, suggesting that the chosen location encoder is the main limitation - the contrastive objective reaches its peak early, regardless of modality diversity or pre-training volume. MELT provides more stable training than SALT and presents a stronger foundation for future scaling.

</details>


### [90] [Predicting gestational age at birth in the context of preterm birth from multi-modal fetal MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20172)
*Diego Fajardo-Rojas,Megan Hall,Daniel Cromb,Mary A. Rutherford,Lisa Story,Emma C. Robinson,Jana Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合多模态胎儿MRI数据和定制机器学习方法的流水线，用于预测出生时的胎龄（GA），并区分足月与早产。在333例对照组和93例早产组中，该流水线取得了R²=0.13、MAE=2.74周，以及准确率0.77、灵敏度0.59、特异度0.82的性能。关键特征包括宫颈长度和胎盘T2*统计量。该工作为将早产预测从分类问题扩展为回归问题提供了概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早产与高死亡率和终身发病风险相关，但其复杂多因素病因导致预测困难，难以实现最优护理。现有文献仅将早产视为分类问题，缺乏更精细的胎龄回归预测方法。

Method: 开发了一个定制机器学习流水线，包括数据插补、特征选择和回归模型，利用多模态形态学与功能胎儿MRI数据。通过消融实验验证设计，采用分层10折交叉验证评估，并将胎龄预测结果分类为足月与早产以计算分类指标。

Result: R²=0.13，MAE=2.74周；分类准确率0.77，灵敏度0.59，特异度0.82。最重要的特征为宫颈长度和胎盘T2*统计量。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用多模态胎儿MRI与机器学习进行胎龄回归预测的可行性，为早产预测提供了新的视角。未来将扩大队列规模以实现早产组内的更细粒度分层。

Abstract: Preterm birth is associated with significant mortality and a risk for lifelong morbidity. The complex multifactorial aetiology hampers accurate prediction and thus optimal care. A pipeline consisting of bespoke machine learning methods for data imputation, feature selection, and regression models to predict gestational age (GA) at birth was developed and evaluated from comprehensive multi-modal morphological and functional fetal MRI data from 333 control cases and 93 preterm birth cases. The GA at birth predictions were classified into term and preterm categories and their accuracy, sensitivity, and specificity were reported. An ablation study was performed to further validate the design of the pipeline. Performance was evaluated using stratified 10-fold cross-validation. The pipeline achieves an R2 score of 0.13 and a mean absolute error of 2.74 weeks. It also achieves a 0.77 accuracy, 0.59 sensitivity, and 0.82 specificity across folds. The predominant features selected by the pipeline include cervical length and statistics derived from placental T2* values. The confluence of fast, motion-robust and multi-modal fetal MRI techniques and machine learning prediction allowed the prediction of the gestation at birth. This information is essential for any pregnancy. To the best of our knowledge, preterm birth had only been addressed as a classification problem in the literature. Therefore, this work provides a proof of concept. Future work will increase the cohort size to allow for finer stratification within the preterm birth cohort. Our code is available at https://github.com/dfajardorojas/ml-for-preterm-birth-.

</details>


### [91] [Computational Methods and Challenges in Cell-Free DNA Analysis for Multi-Cancer Early Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20174)
*Nicko Starkey,Marcin W. Wojewodzic,Krzysztof Rzecki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了2022-2025年间基于cfDNA片段组学和表观遗传特征进行多癌早期检测的计算方法，包括传统统计/机器学习与深度学习框架，指出多模态集成方法最具临床整合潜力，但亟需标准化评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: cfDNA作为一种非侵入性液体活检手段，能够通过单次采血同时检测多种癌症，尤其对当前缺乏筛查方案的癌症具有高灵敏度。为系统梳理该领域近年来的计算分析方法进展，评估其临床转化可行性，并为未来研究提供方向性指导。

Method: 首先概述cfDNA信号的生物学基础，然后分类综述传统统计与机器学习方法、基于自动编码器的深度学习框架等。对每种方法从生物学可解释性、验证策略和临床整合准备度三个维度进行评估。

Result: 多模态集成方法在临床整合方面展现出最强潜力和最高准备度；技术、计算和方法论三类挑战仍然存在；标准化评估协议和报告结果是未来工作的重要需求。

Conclusion: 多模态集成方法最有希望实现临床转化，但领域内迫切需要建立统一的评估标准和报告规范，以促进方法间的公正比较和后续研究的有序推进。

Abstract: Cell-free DNA (cfDNA) is a promising avenue for non-invasive multicancer early detection (MCED), in that, it can enable multiple cancer detection simultaneously from a single blood draw, with particular sensitivity to cancers that currently lack established screening programs. Here we review the computational methods developed between 2022 and 2025 for cfDNA-based MCED. We focus on how fragmentomics and epigenetic features are extracted and analyzed to detect cancer at early stages. We first briefly outline the biological basis of cfDNA signals, then review classical statistical and machine learning approaches alongside deep learning frameworks including autoencoder-based models. For each method we discuss biological interpretability, validation strategy, and readiness for clinical integration. Furthermore, we categorize the current challenges into technical, computational, and methodological while outlining open problems in the field. This review shows that multimodal ensemble approaches have the strongest promise for clinical integration and the highest readiness. However, for better assessment of future work and side-by-side comparison, standardization of evaluation protocols and reporting results will be crucial.

</details>


### [92] [Effective Dimension Governs Generalization in Quantum Kernel Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20183)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示量子视觉模型中两个经验现象——纠缠越强/均匀泛化越好、量子噪声提升测试精度——均源于同一可测量量：有效维度 $d_{\rm eff}$。通过量子核视觉模型，给出谱理论阐释：纠缠结构和量子噪声作为调控 $d_{\rm eff}$ 的两个旋钮，在过拟合区间收缩 $d_{\rm eff}$ 起到类似岭回归的正则化作用。提供了去极化核的精确分解、振幅阻尼的收缩结果、核机器容量界以及容量/对齐风险分解等理论机制，并在实验中验证噪声注入可提升测试精度达+13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前量子视觉模型（量子视觉Transformer和量子卷积网络）报告了两个引人注目但未得到解释的经验现象：(i) 具有更多或更均匀分布纠缠的ansatz泛化性能更好，(ii) 注入量子噪声反而能提升测试精度而非降低。这些现象目前仅被视为偶然发现，通过网格搜索得到，且解释（如果有的话）也是手工完成的。本文试图为这两个现象提供一个统一的可测量理论解释。

Method: 1) 采用量子核视觉模型（量子特征映射+核分类器）作为主要研究框架；2) 提出有效维度 $d_{\rm eff}$ 作为核心可测量量；3) 给出去极化核的精确分解 $K_p=(1-p)^2K+\tfrac{p(2-p)}{D}\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^\top$，证明 $d_{\rm eff}(K_p)\to1$；4) 提供振幅阻尼的收缩结果及其边界；5) 推导核机器容量界和容量/对齐风险分解；6) 在过拟合与欠拟合区间验证效果符号翻转。

Result: 1) 证实纠缠结构和量子噪声通过调节有效维度 $d_{\rm eff}$ 影响泛化性能；2) 去极化噪声下 $d_{\rm eff}$ 单调收缩至1（精确可证）；3) 振幅阻尼可收缩 $d_{\rm eff}$ 并在倒U型甜点处提升测试精度最高达+13%；4) 效果符号在过拟合与欠拟合区间翻转；5) 噪声注入可匹配显式谱滤波前沿；6) 在最多12个量子比特上验证了理论分解。

Conclusion: 本文成功将两个之前被视为偶然的量子视觉模型经验现象统一归因于一个可测量的原则——有效维度 $d_{\rm eff}$，为设计量子视觉模型提供了可量化的指导原则，表明纠缠结构设计和噪声注入本质上是同一正则化框架下的两种调控手段。

Abstract: Recent quantum vision models-quantum vision transformers and quantum convolutional networks-report two striking but unexplained empirical phenomena: (i) ansatze with more, or more uniformly distributed, entanglement generalize better, and (ii) injecting quantum noise can improve test accuracy rather than degrade it. These observations are currently treated as curiosities, discovered by grid search and explained, if at all, by hand. We show that both are manifestations of a single, measurable quantity: the \emph{effective dimension} $d_{\rm eff}$ of the (noise-shaped) quantum feature kernel. Working primarily with quantum-kernel vision models-a quantum feature map read out by a kernel classifier-we give a spectral account in which entanglement structure and quantum noise are two knobs that move $d_{\rm eff}$; in an overfitting regime, contracting $d_{\rm eff}$ acts as ridge-like regularization. We analyze the mechanism: an \emph{exact} decomposition of the depolarized kernel $K_p=(1-p)^2K+\tfrac{p(2-p)}{D}\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^\top$ with $d_{\rm eff}(K_p)\to1$, a contraction result (and its boundary) for amplitude damping, a kernel-machine capacity bound, and a capacity/alignment risk decomposition; the monotone contraction operative in our entangled experiments is verified empirically, not proven in general. Along the one-parameter depolarizing family the collapse is instead exact by construction; we use it only to confirm the kernel decomposition to machine precision and at up to $12$ qubits, not as evidence for $d_{\rm eff}$. Amplitude damping contracts $d_{\rm eff}$ and lifts test accuracy by up to $+13\%$ along an inverted-U sweet spot; the effect's sign flips between the over- and under-fitting regimes; noise injection matches an explicit spectral-filtering frontier. Our results organize two reported anecdotes into a single measurable principle for designing quantum-vision models.

</details>


### [93] [Learner-based Concept Drift Detection: Analysis and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20216)
*Md Moman Ul Haque Khan,Samira Sadaoui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了流式环境中概念漂移的理论特性、多种漂移检测算法及其在合成与真实数据集上的性能表现，旨在深入理解概念漂移特征与检测器行为，提升实际应用中的鲁棒决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习的流式环境中数据分布非平稳（概念漂移）会严重降低模型预测性能，影响鲁棒决策，因此及时高效检测漂移事件至关重要。

Method: 从理论上分析概念漂移特征，并对多类漂移检测算法进行综述，同时在合成与真实数据集（包含突变和渐变等多种漂移场景）上评估其性能。

Result: 对不同类别漂移检测算法在多样化流式场景和漂移特性下的性能表现进行了系统评估。

Conclusion: 本研究增强了对于概念漂移复杂特性及漂移检测器行为的理解，并明确了其在不同上下文中的适用性。

Abstract: Machine learning algorithms deployed for evolving streaming environments must handle the non-stationary data distributions, commonly referred to as concept drift. The presence of concept drift poses a major challenge for many real-world applications because it can severely degrade their predictive performance, hindering their ability to support robust decision-making. Consequently, the timely and efficient detection of drift events is critical for sustaining high accuracy over time. This study examines theoretically the concept drift characteristics and numerous drift detection algorithms across several categories. Furthermore, we evaluate their performance on both synthetic and real-world datasets exhibiting diverse streaming scenarios and drift characteristics, such as abrupt and gradual changes. This study aims to enhance understanding of the complex notion of concept drift characteristics and behavior of drift detectors, along with their applicability to diverse contexts.

</details>


### [94] [Boundary Embedding Shaping with Adaptive Contrastive Learning for Graph Structural Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20283)
*Jiaqing Chen,Zidu Yin,Yichao Cai,Yuhang Liu,Zhen Zhang,Dong Gong,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出边界嵌入塑造（BES）模块，通过自适应对比学习选择性抑制决策边界处的虚假结构噪声，解决图神经网络中边界区域的结构纠缠问题，在节点分类和链接预测任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络中，来自语义无关邻居的虚假相关性会污染节点嵌入，导致结构纠缠问题。这一问题在接近类别边界的节点上最为严重，但现有鲁棒GNN方法对所有节点一视同仁，忽视了边界节点的脆弱性。

Method: 提出边界嵌入塑造（BES），一种自适应对比学习GNN插件模块，通过识别边界区域纠缠作为主要瓶颈，选择性地抑制决策边界处的虚假结构噪声，且仅对模型参数进行最小扰动。

Result: 在节点分类任务上，BES使GCN平均提升3.3%（WikiCS上达5.0%），并在链接预测任务上取得更高精度，整体性能优于现有领先方法。

Conclusion: BES通过聚焦边界节点的结构解纠缠，一致性地改善了边界判别能力，证明了针对边界脆弱性进行专门处理的有效性。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) excel at aggregating neighbor information for classification, yet their performance is hindered by graph structural entanglement, where spurious correlations from semantically irrelevant neighbors contaminate node embeddings. This challenge is most acute for nodes near class boundaries in the embedding space, where amplified structural noise blurs decision boundaries and destabilizes predictions. Existing robust GNN methods largely treat all nodes uniformly, ignoring boundary vulnerabilities. In this paper, to improve classification performance, we tackle graph structural disentanglement by identifying boundary-region entanglement as the primary bottleneck and propose Boundary Embedding Shaping (BES), an adaptive contrastive learning GNN plug-in module that selectively suppresses spurious structural noise at decision boundaries with minimal model parameter perturbation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BES consistently improves boundary discrimination and outperforms existing leading methods. Notably, BES boosts GCN performance by an average of 3.3% in node classification (up to 5.0% on WikiCS) and achieves superior accuracy in link prediction.

</details>


### [95] [Integrating national forest inventory, airborne lidar, and satellite imagery for wall-to-wall mapping of forest structure with computer vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20291)
*Luke J. Zachmann,David D. Diaz,Vincent A. Landau,Chelsey Walden-Schreiner,Tony Chang,Nathan E. Rutenbeck,Katharyn A. Duffy,Kiarie Ndegwa,Andreas Gros,Scott Conway,Guy Bayes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VibrantForests是一个基于卫星的森林结构建模框架，利用激光雷达衍生样本训练模型，以10米分辨率生成美国本土的冠层覆盖度、冠层高度、地上活树生物量、胸高断面积和二次平均直径等森林属性年度图，有效缓解了饱和效应和回归均值问题，为森林和野火规划提供一致的基础数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有森林和野火规划系统依赖多种来源、不同年份和预测质量的拼接数据，导致数据不一致和混淆行为；缺乏能够同时提供大范围、年度更新、一致且管理相关的森林属性估计的方法。

Method: 基于卫星的森林结构模型，使用激光雷达衍生样本进行训练，应用于美国本土连续区域，以10米分辨率并发生成冠层覆盖度、冠层高度、地上活树生物量、胸高断面积和二次平均直径五项森林属性估计。

Result: 模型能够覆盖从稀疏低生物量到稠密高生物量的完整森林条件范围；相比同类被动传感器模型，扩展了饱和阈值范围，减少了回归均值行为（即在稀疏条件下过度估计、稠密条件下低估的常见问题）。

Conclusion: VibrantForests框架通过提供年度、10米分辨率、一致且管理相关的森林属性壁到壁估计，解决了大面积森林和野火规划中的关键数据一致性问题。

Abstract: Remote sensing is increasingly relied upon to deliver actionable science for forest and wildfire risk management across large landscapes. Wall-to-wall, annually updated maps are a persistent need for effective forest management. Many planning systems and data collections combine disparate data sources with different purposes, vintages, and prediction quality, which leads to confounding behavior in operational planning systems. We introduce the VibrantForests framework, developed and applied to map forest attributes and provide a coherent foundation for effective forest and wildfire planning. VibrantForests includes a satellite-based forest structure model trained on lidar-derived samples and applied across the contiguous United States to concurrently generate estimates of canopy cover, canopy height, aboveground live tree biomass, basal area, and quadratic mean diameter at 10-meter resolution. We demonstrate predictive capability spanning the full spectrum of forest conditions ranging from sparse-canopy/low-biomass to dense-canopy/high-biomass. Results show that our model extends the range at which saturation is commonly encountered in comparable passive-sensor models, and reduces regression-to-mean behavior that commonly produces overestimation of forest attributes in small/sparse conditions and underestimation in large/dense conditions. The VibrantForests framework addresses a key limitation in large-area forest and wildfire planning by delivering coherent wall-to-wall estimates of management-relevant attributes at annual cadence and 10m resolution.

</details>


### [96] [Shifting-based Optimizable Linear Relaxations for General Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20292)
*Philipp Kern,László Antal,Erika Ábráham,Carsten Sinz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLiR是一种基于平移的线性松弛方法，仅需Lipschitz常数或关键点集即可为多种激活函数自动生成可优化的线性松弛，从而支持神经网络形式化验证。实验表明，该方法在多种激活函数上产生紧致松弛，比现有方法多验证高达7.8倍的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络验证方法依赖于为每种激活函数手工设计线性松弛，扩展到新激活函数需要大量人工努力。亟需一种通用、自动化的线性松弛生成方法。

Method: SLiR将松弛参数化为斜率，通过平移（shifting）过程计算对应的偏移量，确保在输入域上产生正确的上界和下界，同时支持高效优化。该方法仅需激活函数的Lipschitz常数或一组关键点即可工作。

Result: SLiR在一系列实际激活函数上产生紧致的线性松弛，相比最先进方法能够多验证高达7.8倍的安全属性。

Conclusion: SLiR提供了一种通用、自动化且高效的线性松弛生成框架，大幅降低了将新激活函数纳入神经网络验证系统的手工成本，同时提升了验证性能。

Abstract: The use of neural networks (NNs) is rapidly increasing, including in safety- and security-critical domains. To provide formal guarantees about NN behavior, many verification methods rely on optimizable linear relaxations of activation functions. However, existing techniques depend on hand-crafted relaxations for each activation function. Extension to state-of-the-art activation functions therefore requires substantial manual effort. In contrast, our approach SLiR (Shifting-based Linear Relaxations) is broadly applicable, requiring only a Lipschitz constant or a set of critical points. SLiR parameterizes relaxations by their slope and computes the corresponding offset via a shifting procedure that ensures sound upper and lower bounds over the input domain, enabling efficient optimization while maintaining correctness. Our experiments show that SLiR produces tight relaxations across a wide range of practical activation functions and enables verification of up to 7.8x more properties compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [97] [Recurrent neural networks approximate continuous functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20325)
*Valentin Abadie,Clemens Hutter,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明存在一个固定的单隐层ReLU循环神经网络（RNN），其权重和隐藏层维度固定，可以通过延长运行时间（而非更换网络）来逼近[-1,1]上的任意连续函数。该构造基于一种新中间模型——带神经单元的图灵机（TMNU），并给出了对应的收敛率和极小化下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典逼近定理在目标精度提高时需要更换神经网络。本文研究相反的可能性：是否可以选择一个固定网络，仅通过让它运行更长时间来提高精度？

Method: 提出一种新的中间模型——带神经单元的图灵机（TMNU），该模型既保留实现多项式逼近方案所需的算法自由度，又足够刚性以至于能被具有显式隐藏层维度和权重界限的RNN模拟。通过TMNU将多项式逼近方案编码为固定RNN的时间演化过程。

Result: 证明了对于[-1,1]上的每个连续函数，存在一个权重和隐藏层维度固定的ReLU RNN，通过时间演化可以一致逼近该函数，且收敛速率反映底层多项式逼近的速率。并给出了极小化下界，证明运行时间在这种固定网络逼近范式下是不可避免的资源。

Conclusion: 固定网络的运行时延展性（runtime scalability）在理论上可行——可以通过延长计算时间来换取精度，但运行时间本身是这种逼近范式下的必要资源。

Abstract: Classical approximation theorems ask for a new neural network whenever the target accuracy is improved. This paper studies the opposite possibility: can the network be chosen once and for all, and can accuracy be bought only by letting it run longer? We prove that this is possible for every continuous function on [-1,1]. More precisely, each such function is uniformly approximated by the time evolution of a single ReLU recurrent neural network with fixed weights and fixed hidden dimension. The mechanism behind the construction is a new intermediate model, the Turing machine with neural units (TMNU). This model retains the algorithmic freedom needed to implement polynomial approximation schemes, while remaining rigid enough to be simulated by RNNs with explicit bounds on hidden dimension and weight magnitude. The resulting convergence rates reflect the underlying polynomial approximation rates. We complement the construction with minimax lower bounds showing that runtime is not merely a proof artifact, but an unavoidable resource in this fixed-network approximation paradigm.

</details>


### [98] [Quantum-classical physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold networks for PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20326)
*Xiang Rao,Yuxuan Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QCPIKAN，一种量子-经典物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络，用于求解偏微分方程。基于切比雪夫多项式KAN层和参数化量子电路，通过嵌入物理约束实现物理一致性。理论证明该设计加速高频误差收敛至指数速率并有效抑制数值色散。在多孔介质三种渗流场景（单相流、组分输运、两相流）中验证，相比现有量子-经典物理信息神经网络，在全局预测精度、局部误差控制、动态演化跟踪和驱替前沿定位方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子-经典物理信息神经网络在求解偏微分方程时存在高频误差收敛慢和数值色散问题，需要一种更高效、更精确的混合框架来改善求解性能。

Method: 构建QCPIKAN混合框架，结合切比雪夫多项式KAN层与参数化量子电路，将物理约束嵌入训练损失函数，并基于逼近理论进行理论分析。

Result: 理论证明该设计实现高频误差指数速率收敛并有效抑制数值色散。在单相流、组分输运和两相流三种多孔介质渗流场景中，QCPIKAN在全局预测精度、局部误差控制、动态演化跟踪和驱替前沿定位方面均优于现有量子-经典物理信息神经网络。

Conclusion: QCPIKAN为求解复杂偏微分方程提供了一种鲁棒且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: We develop QCPIKAN, the first quantum-classical physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold network designed to solve partial differential equations (PDEs). Built upon Chebyshev-polynomial KAN layers and parameterized quantum circuits, this hybrid framework embeds physical constraints into the training loss to enforce physical consistency. Our theoretical investigations grounded in approximation theory prove that this design accelerates high-frequency error convergence to an exponential rate and effectively mitigates numerical dispersion. We validate the framework across three typical seepage scenarios in porous media, including single-phase flow, component transport and two-phase flow. Compared with existing quantum-classical physics-informed neural networks, QCPIKAN achieves superior performance in global prediction accuracy, local error control, dynamic evolution tracking and displacement front localization. This work provides a robust and efficient alternative for solving complex PDEs.

</details>


### [99] [Constrained hybrid modelling to predict microbial dynamics and organic matter turnover in soil systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20329)
*Paul Collart,Juergen Gall,Andrea Schnepf,Holger Pagel,Lars Doorenbos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个混合建模框架，通过神经网络从宏基因组推断的功能特征预测土壤有机质周转过程模型的生物动力学参数，并整合生态理论约束确保模型行为合理，在合成和真实数据上均优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土壤微生物控制有机质循环，但在过程模型中准确表征微生物动力学极具挑战，特别是基因组数据与微生物驱动过程之间的复杂关系尚未解决。将基因组数据整合到过程模型参数化中是一个有前景但未解决的问题。

Method: 提出混合建模框架：使用神经网络将宏基因组推断的功能特征映射到过程模型的生物动力学参数，同时整合生态理论和文献中的约束条件，确保未观测状态变量的行为合理。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据上，该方法优于多个基线模型，能有效学习过程模型中不可测量组分的动力学，即使训练数据集很小也能表现良好。

Conclusion: 该混合建模框架成功将基因组数据整合到土壤有机质周转模型中，为改善过程模型的参数化提供了一种有效途径，尤其适用于数据有限的情况。

Abstract: Soil microorganisms control organic matter cycling and largely determine how soil systems can cope with and mitigate climate change and environmental threats. Representing microbial dynamics in process-based soil models is therefore critical to predict carbon cycling in soils, albeit highly challenging to inform from data. One promising approach to improve their parametrisation is the integration of genomic data, yet modelling the complex and unknown relationship between genomes and the processes the microbes are driving is an unsolved problem. In this work, we present the first hybrid modeling framework for deriving biokinetic parameter values of a process-based soil organic matter turnover model from metagenome-inferred functional traits based on DNA sequencing data. Our model predicts biokinetic parameters of the process-based model from genomic trait data with a neural network and integrates constraints from ecological theory and literature to ensure realistic behavior, even of non-observed state variables. We evaluate our method on synthetic genomic trait datasets of varying complexity and on real data, showing that our approach improves performance over multiple baselines and learns the dynamics of unmeasurable components of the process-based model effectively, even for small training datasets.

</details>


### [100] [Critical Percolation as a Synthetic Data Model for Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20347)
*Aryeh Brill,Tom Ingebretsen Carlson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于临界逾渗团簇的层次化合成数据集，作为可解释性研究的基准测试平台，该数据集具有稀疏性、自相似性、幂律分布和解析可解性，并能在线性时间内生成任意规模的数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据集缺乏真实数据的层次化多尺度结构，限制了其作为可解释性方法评估的逼真玩具模型的价值。

Method: 利用临界平均场逾渗团簇构建稀疏低维分形结构，通过层次化潜在变量生成目标值；利用逾渗团簇、随机树与加性凝聚的映射关系，提出近线性时间算法联合采样随机树及其层次潜在分解。

Result: 探针实验表明，模型真实潜在变量可从神经网络激活中线性解码；数据集具有稀疏性、自相似性、幂律统计和解析可解性。

Conclusion: 临界逾渗为可解释性研究提供了一个原则性的测试平台，弥补了现有合成数据集在层次结构方面的不足。

Abstract: Neural networks learn features that reflect the hierarchical, multi-scale structure of natural data. Synthetic datasets used to evaluate interpretability methods typically lack this structure, limiting their value as realistic toy models. To close this gap, we introduce a family of synthetic datasets consisting of hierarchical functions defined on critical mean-field percolation clusters embedded in a high-dimensional data space. The percolation data consists of sparse, low-dimensional fractal clusters with a power-law size distribution. Latent variables modeling a taxonomic hierarchy generate each data point's target value.
