The data model is analytically tractable with known critical exponents that fix its properties without requiring hyperparameter tuning. We leverage a mapping between percolation clusters, random trees, and additive coalescence to propose an almost linear-time algorithm to jointly sample a random tree and its hierarchical latent decomposition, enabling data generation at arbitrary scale. Using probing experiments, we find that the model's ground-truth latent variables can be linearly decoded from neural network activations. Together, sparsity, self-similarity, power-law statistics, and analytical tractability make critical percolation a principled testbed for interpretability research.

</details>


### [101] [On the Variance of Temporal Difference Learning and its Reduction Using Control Variates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20357)
*Hsiao-Ru Pan,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了表格化表示下相位设置中时序差分学习的方差，揭示了TD通过有效聚合更多独立轨迹来降低方差的机制，并证明了TD方差渐近上界不超过蒙特卡洛估计，且短视更新在固定样本量下方差更小。此外，展示了直接优势估计可视为回归调整控制变量，在大样本极限下比TD有更紧的方差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解TD学习中方差降低的机制，并从理论上证明TD相对于MC的方差优势，以及不同更新视野对方差的影响。

Method: 采用表格化表示的相位设置分析TD方差，通过理论推导证明TD方差上界；将DAE重新解释为回归调整控制变量进行方差分析。

Result: (1) TD方差渐近上界不超过MC估计；(2) 固定样本量下，更短的视野更新产生更小方差；(3) DAE在大样本极限下比TD有更紧的方差界。

Conclusion: TD通过聚合多条独立轨迹降低方差，DAE作为回归调整控制变体可获得更优的方差界。

Abstract: We analyze the variance of temporal difference (TD) learning using the phased setting with tabular representation, and show that one of the mechanisms behind its ability to reduce variance is by effectively aggregating over a larger number of independent trajectories. Based on this insight, we demonstrate that (1) the variance of TD is asymptotically bounded from above by Monte Carlo (MC) estimators, and (2) shorter horizon updates incurs less variance for a fixed number of samples. Beyond TD, we show that Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE), a method for estimating the advantage function, can be seen as a type of regression-adjusted control variate, which achieves a tighter bound on the variance compared to TD in the large-sample limit. Finally, we numerically illustrate the behaviors of these estimators with carefully designed environments.

</details>


### [102] [Train, Retrieve, or Both? A Four-Arm Head-to-Head for Correct Statutory Citation on the Ontario Residential Tenancies Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20359)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对安大略省《住宅租赁法》（RTA）的法定条文引用任务，对比了四种方法（基础零样本、LoRA微调、仅检索、微调+检索混合），发现检索是消除幻觉的关键，且混合方法（SFT+RAG）得分最高（0.481精确匹配），效果优于更大模型和更多数据。所有结果均为初步报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自述租户、房东和帮助台工作人员需要准确引用法律条文（如安大略省RTA）的场景下，作者希望回答一个实证问题：仅靠微调（fine-tuning）就足够完成任务，还是需要混合检索（hybrid retrieval）？

Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上设置了四组对照实验：基础零样本、LoRA SFT-only、RAG-only、SFT+RAG混合。评估指标为条文精确匹配（章节+小节），使用小规模人工验证待定的真实测试集。

Result: 基础模型无法引用RTA；SFT-only会错误召回条文；检索能将幻觉降至零；SFT+RAG混合方法以0.481的精确匹配得分最高，且无幻觉引用。该廉价混合方案（bge-small）性能优于使用更大嵌入模型和交叉编码器重排序器的管线，增大训练集也无帮助。

Conclusion: 在法定条文引用任务中，强性能不需要专门的检索模型或更多数据；检索是消除幻觉的必要条件；混合方法通过SFT提升对高召回候选集的鲁棒性而取得最佳效果。当前结果未达到0.70的精确匹配目标，且全部为初步报告。

Abstract: Self-represented tenants, landlords, and help-desk staff need to be pointed at the provision of law that actually governs a question, with a correct statutory citation. We study this task on the Ontario Residential Tenancies Act, 2006 (RTA) and its core regulation, asking the operator's question empirically: is fine-tuning enough, or is hybrid retrieval needed? We run a four-arm head-to-head on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (base zero-shot, LoRA SFT-only, RAG-only, and an SFT+RAG hybrid), scored on citation exact-match (section+subsection) over a small, human-verification-pending real eval set. The base model cannot cite the RTA and SFT-only mis-recalls sections; retrieval is essential and drives hallucination to zero by construction; and the SFT+RAG hybrid scores highest at 0.481 exact-match with zero hallucinated citations. Its edge comes from SFT making provision selection more robust to the higher-recall candidate sets that hurt zero-shot RAG. Notably, this cheap bge-small hybrid matches or beats a pipeline built on bigger, specialized retrieval models (a larger embedder and a cross-encoder reranker), and a larger/improved training set does not help either: strong statutory-citation performance here does not require specialized retrieval models or more data. The artifact zeroes hallucination and clears the lift-over-base bar but does not reach the aspirational 0.70 exact-match target. All results are on a small, human-verification-pending real eval set and are reported as preliminary.

</details>


### [103] [Judging to Improve: A De-biased VLM-as-3D-Judge Protocol for Single-Image 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20364)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何将VLM-as-3D-judge的排序能力扩展到优化循环中，以低成本、无人工标签的方式微调TRELLIS生成器（家具类）。核心贡献是优化级hardening法官（区分训练/评估法官、位置偏差校正、三种失效模式修复）。实验表明：轻量级PEFT仅能达到基线水平（50%胜率），无法超越；最有效的策略是条件修复（conditioner repair）而非微调flow-DiT；信号需通过质量对比构建，基线样本几乎无可学习偏好（0.94顺序翻转率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM-as-3D-judge已能可靠排序单图转3D网格质量，但能否将法官的偏好信号低成本地（无需人工标注）用于优化生成模型（TRELLIS）在特定类别（家具）上的表现？核心是将法官从排序（ranking）扩展到优化（optimization）。

Method: 1) 构建优化级法官：训练法官（Qwen2.5-VL-7B）与评估法官（InternVL3-8B）分离打破循环；校正位置偏差；修复三种失效模式（图像过载、几何隐藏点云渲染、无参考法官奖励干净但错误输出）。2) 通过质量对比构建可学习信号（基线样本0.94顺序翻转率表明无天然偏好）。3) 探索六种PEFT适配方法、两种输入模式和严重度扫描，最有效的是在严重退化下修复条件编码器（conditioner repair）。

Result: 所有轻量级方法最多达到与基线持平（50%胜率），未能达到65%胜率目标；最有效的conditioner repair在严重退化下达到0.50；flow-DiT微调效果被采样器冲刷；胜率在n=8对象上呈现方向性。校准证据：清晰差距胜率0.83-1.0，基线对基线上约0.5。

Conclusion: 在强公开数据基线上，仅靠轻量级PEFT无法超越，需要更昂贵的适配策略才能突破；但法官协议本身可复用，且揭示了机制性结论：干净输入饱和法官、flow-DiT微调被采样器稀释、条件修复才是改变几何的关键点。

Abstract: A companion study established a de-biased, cross-model VLM-as-3D-judge that reliably ranks single-image-to-3D mesh quality where cheap geometry and CLIP proxies fall short. This paper asks: can that judge's preferences specialize a strong open generator, TRELLIS, on one asset class (furniture), cheaply and without human labels? Taking the judge from ranking to optimization is where the work lives. Pushing a VLM judge into the training and evaluation loop exposes failure modes ranking never triggered, so our contribution is an optimization-grade hardening of the judge: a training judge (Qwen2.5-VL-7B) held distinct from an evaluation judge (InternVL3-8B) to break circularity; position-bias correction; and fixes for three failure modes (image overload, geometry-hiding splat renders, and reference-free judging that rewards clean-but-wrong outputs), with calibration evidence (clear-gap win-rate 0.83-1.0; base-vs-base ~0.5). Using this protocol as an independent evaluator, and working only from public models and data with lightweight parameter-efficient adaptation, we find our methods match the strong base rather than exceed it. Independent base samples carry essentially no learnable preference (0.94 order-flip rate), so signal must be engineered by quality-contrastive construction. Across six adaptation methods, two input regimes, and a severity sweep, the most targeted - conditioner repair under severe degradation - reaches parity (0.50) with the base, while no method clears the >=65% win-rate target. The result is mechanistic: clean inputs saturate the judge, flow-DIT fine-tuning washes out through the sampler, and conditioning repair is the locus that moves geometry. Win-rates are directional at n=8 objects. Matching a strong public-data base with cheap adaptation is itself informative: exceeding it needs more than lightweight PEFT on public data, and the judge protocol is reusable.

</details>


### [104] [CRAX: Fast Safe Reinforcement Learning Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20376)
*Tristan Tomilin,Mourad Boustani,Mickey Beurskens,Thiago D. Simão*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRAX，一个基于JAX和MJX的高效安全强化学习基准，相比CPU基准提速约100倍，包含6个环境套件和3个智能体任务，并评估了6种安全RL方法，发现没有单一方法在所有任务上占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全RL基准在高保真3D物理模拟下计算速度慢，限制了大规模实验和快速原型开发，因此需要更高效的基准平台。

Method: 基于MuJoCo XLA (MJX)物理引擎，利用JAX的向量化操作和硬件加速，构建包含6个环境套件和3个智能体任务（各3个难度级别）的安全RL基准CRAX。

Result: CRAX相比CPU基准提速约100倍；评估6种安全RL方法发现无单一方法在所有任务占优，存在性能与安全的权衡；课程学习和安全迁移在困难设置下能提升性能。

Conclusion: CRAX为安全RL研究提供了高效的计算平台，揭示了现有方法在不同任务上的性能差异，并为提升安全RL性能指明了方向（如课程学习、安全迁移）。

Abstract: Safety is a core concern for deploying reinforcement learning (RL) agents in real-world domains such as robotics and autonomous driving. While benchmarks have been central to progress in RL, existing safety benchmarks with high-fidelity 3D physics remain computationally slow, limiting large-scale experimentation and rapid prototyping. To address this gap, we propose CRAX (Constrained RL Accelerated with JAX). Built on top of the MuJoCo XLA (MJX) physics engine with realistic 3D dynamics, CRAX leverages vectorized operations and hardware acceleration, yielding up to ~100x speedups over comparable CPU-based safety benchmarks. The benchmark features six environment suites and three agent-specific tasks, each spanning three difficulty levels. Evaluating six popular safe RL methods shows that no single approach dominates across all tasks, and reveals the trade-offs between performance and safety. We find that curriculum learning across difficulty levels and safety transfer can improve performance over direct training in harder settings.

</details>


### [105] [Towards Modality-imbalanced Federated Graph Learning: A Data Synthesis-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20382)
*Zhengyu Wu,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedMGS，一种面向多模态联邦图学习中模态不平衡问题的隐式语义合成方法，通过原型引导的潜在语义合成与可靠性校准机制，在四个任务上取得最高17.41%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联邦图学习面临两种粒度的模态不平衡挑战：客户端级别（某些客户端缺失完整模态）和节点级别（单个节点缺少视觉或文本属性）。现有方法主要针对图无关或集中式场景，难以直接适配。

Method: 提出FedMGS，包含三个核心组件：1) 可用性感知图编码器，防止缺失模态污染局部结构传播；2) 原型引导的潜在语义合成器，建立跨客户端的语义锚点用于缺失模态；3) 可靠性校准的语义融合机制，在预测读出前调控恢复的潜在表示的影响。

Result: 在四个任务上进行大量实验，FedMGS持续优于竞争基线，最高提升17.41%，且实现了最佳的效率-性能平衡。

Conclusion: 将模态不平衡的MM-FGL形式化为隐式图感知潜在语义表示合成问题，直接在表示空间中恢复缺失模态语义，最大化与原始数据语义分布的对齐，缓解缺失模态引起的高方差。

Abstract: MultiModal Federated Graph Learning (MM-FGL) offers a natural collaborative training paradigm, but its practical deployment is challenged by two granularities of modality imbalance. Client-level imbalance occurs when certain clients lack entire modalities, while node-level imbalance occurs when individual nodes exhibit missing visual or textual attributes. While several relevant studies exist, our investigation reveals that they predominantly target graph-agnostic or centralized scenarios, rendering them difficult to adapt directly. To address these challenges, we formalize modality-imbalanced MM-FGL as an implicit graph-aware latent semantic representation synthesis problem. This paradigm recovers missing modal semantics directly within the representation space, thereby maximizing alignment with the original data's semantic distribution and mitigating the high variance induced by missing modalities. To this end, we propose FedMGS (Federated Modality-aware Graph Synthesis), which integrates three core components. The availability-aware graph encoder prevents missing modalities from contaminating local structural propagation. The prototype-guided latent semantic synthesizer establishes cross-client semantic anchors for unavailable modalities. The reliability-calibrated semantic fusion mechanism regulates the impact of recovered latent representations prior to predictive readout. Extensive experiments on four tasks show that FedMGS consistently outperforms competitive baselines with gains up to 17.41% with best efficiency-performance tradeoff.

</details>


### [106] [The Significance of Style Diversity in Annotation-Free Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20400)
*Zahra Abbasiantaeb,Zeno Belligoli,Omar Essam,Mohammad Aliannejadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种完全无需人工标注数据的意图分类合成对话生成框架，仅依赖意图定义，利用主题与风格属性提升数据多样性，并通过后置风格化模型和LLM过滤提升质量，在工业和公开数据集上达到人工标注数据93.3%的性能。核心发现：风格多样性比主题多样性对合成数据效用更关键，且生成时融入风格属性优于后置适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中意图分类需要大量人工标注的种子数据，但快速迭代的工业环境往往难以获得此类数据，因此亟需一种完全无需人工标注的合成数据生成方法。

Method: 1) 基于意图定义的双属性（主题与风格）对话生成框架；2) 两种后置风格化模型Univ和Exam，将LLM生成的合成话语转化为更丰富、类人的语言风格；3) LLM-as-a-judge过滤流程提升数据质量。

Result: 在工业和公开数据集上达到使用人工标注训练数据性能的93.3%；风格多样性比主题多样性对合成数据效用更关键；生成时融入风格属性比后置风格适配更有效。

Conclusion: 完全无需人工标注的合成数据生成可行，且风格多样性是提升合成数据效用的关键因素，生成阶段引入风格控制优于后期适配。

Abstract: Generating high-utility synthetic data for intent classification typically requires human-annotated seed data, which is often unavailable in fast-paced industrial settings. In this paper, we propose a framework for synthetic dialogue generation that works entirely without human-annotated data, relying solely on intent definitions. Our proposed dialogue generation framework utilizes two different types of topic and style attributes to improve data diversity. Also, we propose two novel post-hoc stylization models called Univ and Exam to transform synthetic LLM-generated utterances into more varied, human-like linguistic styles. To enhance data quality, we utilize an LLM-as-a-judge filtering process. Experimental results on both industrial and public datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves up to 93.3% of the performance obtained using human-annotated training data. Crucially, the findings reveal that style diversity is more critical than topic diversity for synthetic data utility, as it prevents models from learning spurious stylistic correlations. Furthermore, the study shows that incorporating style attributes during the generation process is more effective than post-hoc style adaptation.

</details>


### [107] [Direct Advantage Estimation for Scalable and Sample-efficient Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20411)
*Hsiao-Ru Pan,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了直接优势估计（DAE）到部分可观测领域，并通过引入离散潜在动力学模型降低计算复杂度，在Arcade Learning Environment上验证了DAE随函数逼近器容量有效扩展且保持高样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAE方法依赖完整环境可观测性且需要建模转移概率，导致高维观测下计算开销大，限制了其在实际场景中的应用。

Method: 1) 将DAE的理论框架扩展到部分可观测领域；2) 引入离散潜在动力学模型来高效近似转移概率，降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在Arcade Learning Environment上，DAE随函数逼近器容量有效扩展，同时保持高样本效率。

Conclusion: 通过解决可观测性限制和计算复杂度问题，DAE在部分可观测、高维观测场景下仍能高效运行。

Abstract: Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE) has been shown to improve the sample efficiency of deep reinforcement learning algorithms. However, its reliance on full environment observability limits its applicability in realistic settings, and its requirement to model transition probabilities incurs substantial computational overhead for high-dimensional observations. In the present work, we address both limitations. First, we extend the theoretical framework of DAE to partially observable domains with minimal modifications. Second, we reduce its computational complexity by introducing discrete latent dynamics models that efficiently approximate transition probabilities. We evaluate our approach on the Arcade Learning Environment and find that DAE scales effectively with function approximator capacity while retaining high sample efficiency.

</details>


### [108] [Pseudo-Feature Padding: A Lightweight Defense Against False Data Injection in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20415)
*Farhin Farhad Riya,Shahinul Hoque,Yingyuan Yang,Jinyuan Sun,Kevin Tomsovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对CPS中DNN易受FDIA攻击的问题，提出了一种轻量级、模型无关的防御框架。该框架通过在输入样本中添加基于统计分布的伪特征填充，以随机化和数据感知的方式增加输入维度，使对抗攻击因扰动不可迁移和填充结构不可预测而计算上不可行。在IEEE 14/30/118/300总线系统上的实验表明，该方法在几乎不影响性能的前提下显著提升了模型鲁棒性，有效抵御了传统防御无法应对的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN在CPS中检测FDIA虽精度高，但CPS独特的基础设施使其易被攻击者利用以逃避检测，而传统防御机制难以应对这种特定场景下的对抗攻击。

Method: 提出一种防御框架，在原始输入层之前增加额外输入层，利用输入样本的统计分布生成伪特征值进行填充。填充过程具有随机性和数据感知性，增加了输入维度，使得对抗扰动在不同样本间不可迁移，且攻击者无法预测填充结构。

Result: 在IEEE 14、30、118、300总线电力系统状态估计任务中，该方法在对抗攻击设置下显著提升了模型鲁棒性，对性能影响可忽略，并能有效缓解传统防御机制无法防御的攻击。

Conclusion: 该填充策略是一种轻量级、模型无关、无需修改核心架构的可行防御方案，适用于真实世界的CPS部署，能有效增强DNN对FDIA的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks DNNs have achieved remarkable accuracy in various tasks including their application in CyberPhysical Systems CPS for detecting False Data Injection Attacks FDIA during critical operations However the unique infrastructure of CPS makes DNNs vulnerable to exploitation by attackers aiming to evade detection Additionally the distinct nature of CPS presents challenges for conventional defense mechanisms against FDIA This paper proposes an innovative defense framework that strengthens DNNs against such attacks by introducing an additional input layer that performs padding in the input samples using pseudofeature values derived from the inputs statistical distribution This padding increases the input dimensionality in a randomized and dataaware manner making adversarial attacks computationally infeasible due to the nontransferable nature of crafted perturbations and the unpredictability of the padded structure Our method is lightweight modelagnostic and requires no modifications to the core architecture making it highly deployable in realworld CPS settings We evaluated our framework on critical power grid applications such as state estimation using the IEEE 14bus 30bus 118bus and 300bus systems Experiments under adversarial settings demonstrate that our padding strategy significantly improves model robustness with negligible impact on performance and effectively mitigates attacks that would otherwise bypass conventional defenses

</details>


### [109] [On the Redundancy of Timestep Embeddings in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20416)
*José A. Chávez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明扩散模型可以在完全移除时间步嵌入的情况下，通过隐式推断噪声尺度达到甚至超越传统条件模型的性能，挑战了时间条件建模的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑扩散模型中显式时间步嵌入的必要性，分析其对U-Net和DiT架构的影响，探索更高效的生成架构。

Method: 从理论和实验两方面展开：理论上证明在特定条件下扩散训练目标的全局最小值无需显式时间条件；实验上在CelebA和CIFAR-10数据集上进行消融研究，比较有无时间嵌入的模型性能。

Result: 时间无关模型在FID、精确率和召回率等指标上可媲美甚至超越有条件模型，保持高结构保真度。架构可在特定假设下从受损输入隐式推断噪声尺度。

Conclusion: 显式时间条件在扩散模型中是冗余的，这一发现挑战了长期以来的时间条件范式，为更高效、更关注结构的生成架构铺平了道路。

Abstract: Diffusion models rely heavily on explicit timestep embeddings to modulate the denoising process across various noise scales. In this work, we challenge the necessity of these temporal signals by analyzing their impact on U-Net and Diffusion Transformer architectures. Beyond empirical evidence, we provide a theoretical framework demonstrating that, under certain conditions, the global minimizer of the diffusion training objective can be achieved without explicit timestep conditioning. Our findings reveal a surprising robustness when timestep embeddings are completely removed. Extensive ablation studies on the CelebA and CIFAR-10 datasets show that these time-agnostic models can maintain high structural fidelity and even surpass their conditioned counterparts in competitive metrics, including FID, precision, and recall. Our analysis suggests these architectures can implicitly infer noise scales from the corrupted input under specific assumptions, rendering explicit temporal conditioning redundant. This study challenges long-standing temporal conditioning paradigms and paves the way for more efficient and structurally focused generative architectures.

</details>


### [110] [Neural network surrogates with uncertainty quantification for inverse problems in partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20417)
*Christian Jimenez-Beltran,Aretha L. Teckentrup,Antonio Vergari,Konstantinos C. Zygalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepGaLA，一种用于微分方程逆问题的不确定性量化神经网络代理模型，结合延迟接受MCMC诊断，在保持计算效率的同时提供可靠的贝叶斯推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯逆问题方法在处理复杂正演模型和高维参数空间时计算成本高昂，现有代理模型（如高斯过程）在高维场景下效率下降，亟需一种兼具不确定量化与可扩展性的替代方案。

Method: 提出DeepGaLA神经网络代理模型，用于替代微分方程求解器；采用延迟接受MCMC短运行作为代理后验近似的诊断工具；代理模型可融入微分方程约束（包括非线性情形），并提供不确定性感知的预测。

Result: DeepGaLA在正演模型近似精度上与高斯过程代理相当，但在参数维度增长时能更好地保持计算效率；可有效融入微分方程约束；延迟接受MCMC可作为有效的后验近似诊断方法。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化的神经代理模型能够为复杂系统中的逆问题提供可扩展且可靠的贝叶斯推断。

Abstract: Inverse problems for differential equations arise throughout science and engineering, where one seeks to infer unknown model parameters from noisy or incomplete observations. Traditional numerical methods for these problems are often computationally expensive, particularly in Bayesian settings where evaluating the likelihood becomes costly for complex forward models and high-dimensional parameter spaces. To address this challenge, we introduce DeepGaLA, a neural-network surrogate for differential equation solvers that provides uncertainty-aware predictions, reducing overconfident inference when training data are limited. To evaluate the fidelity of the surrogate-induced posterior approximations in practice, we show that a short run of delayed-acceptance Markov chain Monte Carlo can serve as an effective diagnostic. Across a range of numerical experiments, DeepGaLA delivers forward-model approximations with accuracy comparable to established Gaussian-process surrogates, while better maintaining efficiency as parameter dimension grows. Moreover, it can incorporate differential-equation constraints, including in nonlinear settings. Overall, these results indicate that uncertainty-quantified neural surrogates can enable scalable and reliable Bayesian inference for inverse problems in complex systems.

</details>


### [111] [Sparsity, Superposition, and Forgetting: A Mechanistic Study of Representation Retention in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20431)
*Jan Wasilewski,Jędrzej Kozal,Michał Woźniak,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可控的玩具世界框架，通过合成生成器-分离器管道定义真实潜在特征，研究持续学习中的遗忘机制，发现叠加随任务边界出现瞬态下降，高稀疏性增加叠加但不必然导致遗忘，任务级有效秩随稀疏性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习系统常遗忘先前知识，但由于真实数据集纠缠多种因素，遗忘机制难以隔离研究。因此需要建立一个可控框架，使这些机制可观察和可测试。

Method: 构建合成生成器-分离器管道，定义真实潜在特征，构建具有可调稀疏性和重叠度的任务；引入表示强度和叠加（特征间方向重叠）的可测量量；通过SINDy拟合保留、叠加和暴露历史之间的稀疏动态关系；基于有效秩进行任务级分析。

Result: (1) 叠加随时间增加，但在任务边界出现瞬态下降，提示边界特定干扰而非稳定漂移；(2) 高特征稀疏性导致更多叠加，但不必然引起遗忘，当表示强度足够时可减少遗忘；(3) 任务级有效秩随稀疏性增长，表明稀疏机制下容量使用更广泛。

Conclusion: 这些结果修正了"更多叠加导致更多遗忘"的直觉，表明重叠与表示强度和容量分配相互作用。该玩具分析为持续学习提供了可证伪的假设和诊断工具。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) systems often forget previously acquired knowledge, yet the mechanisms driving forgetting remain hard to isolate in practice because real datasets entangle many factors. We present a controlled, toy-world framework that makes these mechanisms observable and testable. Using a synthetic generator-separator pipeline, we define ground-truth latent features, build tasks with tunable sparsity and overlap, and introduce measurable quantities for representation strength and superposition (directional overlap among features). We then study retention dynamics-the temporal change of representation strength by fitting sparse dynamical relations (via SINDy) between retention, superposition, and exposure history. A complementary task-level analysis based on effective rank characterizes how representational capacity is allocated across tasks. Our controlled experiments yield three takeaways. (1) Superposition tends to increase over time with transient dips at task boundaries, suggesting boundary-specific interference rather than steady drift. (2) Higher feature sparsity induces more superposition yet does not inevitably cause forgetting; when representations remain strong, forgetting can be reduced despite overlap. (3) Task-level effective rank grows with sparsity, indicating broader capacity usage under sparse regimes. Together, these results nuance the common intuition that more superposition leads to more forgetting by showing that overlap interacts with representation strength and capacity allocation. Our toy analysis provides falsifiable hypotheses and diagnostic tools for CL.

</details>


### [112] [Data Bias Mitigation under Coverage Constraints & The Price of Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20461)
*Bruno Scarone,Alfredo Viola,Renée J. Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的偏差缓解框架，通过引入覆盖约束确保交叉群体（如种族与性别的交集）有足够的数据代表性，并将偏差缓解建模为整数线性规划，在偏差容忍度与数据效率之间进行权衡，实验表明该方法在保持预测精度的同时有效降低了偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在多个敏感属性（如种族和性别）的交叉点上表现出歧视性结果或性能下降，这源于两个挑战：缺乏原则性的偏差量化指标以及交叉子群体在训练数据中代表性不足。

Method: 扩展了现有偏差缓解框架，引入覆盖约束以强制各群体（包括交叉子群体）有足够的数据代表性；将偏差缓解建模为整数线性规划，在所有缓解策略上优化；通过牺牲微小的偏差近似误差换取更高的数据效率。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持多个分类器预测精度的同时有效缓解了偏差，覆盖约束对于保持下游ML性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 该方法为法律合规和数据治理提供了关键支持，使从业者能够在偏差减少与数据修改成本之间做出明智权衡，同时覆盖约束虽出于统计考虑，但对保持模型性能不可或缺。

Abstract: Machine learning models have been shown to exhibit discriminatory outcomes or degraded performance for individuals at the intersection of multiple sensitive attributes, such as race and gender. This stems in part from two interrelated challenges: the lack of principled measures for quantifying bias (potentially intersectional), and insufficient representation of intersectional subgroups in training data. We extend a recent bias mitigation framework to incorporate coverage constraints that enforce sufficient representation across groups, including intersectional subgroups. Since achieving exactly zero bias for all groups may not be data efficient (meaning it may require large amounts of data), our solution trades small approximation errors in bias for greater data efficiency while satisfying coverage constraints. We also formulate bias mitigation as an integer linear program that optimizes over all mitigation strategies, and characterize the price of fairness, the minimum data modification cost, as a function of fairness tolerance. This is essential both for legal compliance, where regulations may mandate specific fairness thresholds, and for data governance, enabling practitioners to make informed trade-offs between bias reduction and data modification (particularly, data purchasing) costs. We evaluate our techniques on publicly available datasets, demonstrating that bias mitigation via our framework preserves predictive accuracy across multiple classifiers, and that coverage constraints, while motivated by statistical considerations, are essential for preserving downstream ML performance.

</details>


### [113] [Fisher-Geometric Sharpness and the Implicit Bias of SGD toward Flat Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20469)
*Md Sakir Ahmed,Kumaresh Sarmah,Hemen Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过 Fisher 信息矩阵定义的黎曼几何框架解决了深度学习中锐度（平坦性）在函数保持重参数化下不变性的问题，证明黎曼锐度是重参数化不变的，并推导出 SGD 的平稳分布指数级集中于黎曼平坦的极小值，结合 PAC-Bayes 泛化界为"平坦极小值泛化更好"提供了严格的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准欧氏平坦性度量（如 Hessian 的迹或最大特征值）在函数保持的重参数化下不具有不变性，这动摇了"SGD 隐式偏好平坦极小值且平坦极小值泛化更好"这一直觉的理论基础。Dinh 等人（"Sharp minima can generalize for deep nets"）的 critique 直接指向这一缺陷，亟需一个具有重参数化不变性的严格几何框架。

Method: 将平坦性建立在由 Fisher 信息矩阵诱导的统计流形的黎曼几何上，定义黎曼锐度（Riemannian sharpness, SR）并证明其在光滑函数保持重参数化下的不变性。将 mini-batch SGD 的梯度噪声建模为协方差与 FIM 成比例的结构，推导对应随机微分方程的平稳分布，证明概率质量指数级集中于黎曼平坦的极小值。利用 PAC-Bayes 泛化界建立 SR 与测试性能的联系。

Result: 在 MNIST 和 CIFAR-10 上的实验表明，SR 能够可靠地跟踪泛化性能，而欧氏锐度不能；SR 随 η/B 的标度行为与理论预测一致。同时指出，实践中使用的对角经验 FIM 估计仅近似继承不变性，完全的不变性需要 K-FAC 等结构化估计器。

Conclusion: 该工作为"平坦极小值泛化更好"提供了严格的、重参数化不变的理论解释，弥合了先前直觉与理论 critique 之间的鸿沟，为理解深度学习中的隐式正则化提供了坚实的几何基础。

Abstract: A widely held intuition in deep learning is that stochastic gradient descent (SGD) implicitly favors flat minima and that flat minima generalize better, but standard Euclidean measures of flatness such as the trace or maximum eigenvalue of the loss Hessian are not invariant under reparametrizations that preserve the network function, which undermines the theoretical foundations of this narrative. In this study we resolve this issue by grounding flatness in the Riemannian geometry of the statistical manifold induced by the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM). We define Riemannian sharpness mathematically and prove that it is invariant under smooth, function-preserving reparametrizations, which directly addresses the critique of Dinh et al. in the paper ``Sharp minima can generalize for deep nets''.We note that this invariance is a property of the true FIM; the diagonal empirical estimator used in practice (and in all experiments below) inherits invariance only approximately, and exact invariance under arbitrary reparametrizations would require structured estimators such as K-FAC. We formalize the gradient noise of mini-batch SGD as having a covariance structure proportional to the FIM, derive the stationary distribution of the resulting stochastic differential equation, and then show that the probability mass is exponentially concentrated at Riemannian-flat minima. A PAC-Bayes generalization bound controlled explicitly by SR formally links this geometric bias to test performance. Our experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 confirm that SR reliably tracks generalization in ways that Euclidean sharpness does not, and that its scaling with $η/B$ matches the theoretical predictions. Together these results provide a rigorous, reparametrization-invariant account of why flat minima generalize.

</details>


### [114] [UltraQuant: 4-bit KV Caching for Context-Heavy Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20474)
*Inesh Chakrabarti,David Limpus,Aditi Ghai Rana,Bowen Bao,Spandan Tiwari,Thiago Crepaldi,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对上下文密集型智能体工作负载，研究了4位KV缓存压缩技术，提出了UltraQuant方案，在AMD GPU上相比FP8基线将P50首令牌延迟降低3.47倍（晚期回合），输出吞吐量提升1.63倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文密集型智能体场景中，KV缓存面临长前缀复用和并发竞争的压力，现有FP8 KV缓存方案在吞吐量和延迟方面仍有优化空间，需要更高效的量化方案来提升服务质量。

Method: 采用TurboQuant风格的旋转和码本量化作为质量锚点，vLLM FP8 KV缓存作为部署锚点；提出非对称K/V处理、Walsh-Hadamard旋转、QJL去除和块级缩放变体等设计；在AMD GPU上实现优化的解码注意力内核和UltraQuant（FP4近似路径，使用FP8查询、FP4 KV张量、UE8M0组缩放和CDNA4原生缩放MFMA支持）。

Result: 在长上下文、多轮智能体工作负载上，UltraQuant在缓存压力大的晚期回合将P50首令牌延迟降低3.47倍，所有回合平均降低2.3倍，输出吞吐量比FP8 KV基线提升1.63倍。

Conclusion: 4位KV缓存压缩在多轮智能体场景中能显著提升系统性能，UltraQuant方案在延迟和吞吐量方面均优于FP8基线，且实际部署中需联合测量任务质量、缓存驻留和服务吞吐量。

Abstract: Context-heavy agents place unusual pressure on the key-value (KV) cache: long prefixes are reused across many short turns, while concurrency determines whether the serving system can keep GPUs utilized. We study 4-bit KV-cache compression for this setting, using TurboQuant-style rotation and codebook quantization as a quality anchor and vLLM FP8 KV caching as the deployment anchor. We report three contributions. First, we frame 4-bit KV caching around multi-round agent workloads where task quality, cache residency, and serving throughput must be measured jointly. Second, we describe the practical design choices needed to make the 4-bit path robust, including asymmetric K/V treatment, Walsh-Hadamard rotation, QJL removal, and block-scale variants. Third, we present serving optimizations on AMD GPUs, including optimized decode-attention kernels and UltraQuant, an FP4 approximation path that uses FP8 queries, FP4 KV tensors, UE8M0 group scales, and native scaled-MFMA support on CDNA4. On a long-context, multi-turn agentic workload, UltraQuant cuts P50 time-to-first-token by 3.47x in the cache-pressured late rounds (2.3x across all rounds) and raises output throughput by 1.63x over the FP8 KV baseline.

</details>


### [115] [Marginal Advantage Accumulation for Memory-Driven Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20475)
*Mingyu Yang,Keye Zheng,Congchao Cheng,Yujie Liu,Xingkang Lu,Fan Jiang,Yefei Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出边际优势累积(MAA)机制，通过跨批次操作级证据累积解决批量式迹蒸馏中矛盾反馈问题，在16个设置中14个最佳，优化阶段token消耗降低约75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量式迹蒸馏中，同一内存操作在不同批次可能收到矛盾反馈，现有方法缺乏跨批次、操作级的证据累积机制，无法区分稳定有效操作与偶然命中。

Method: 形式化两个结构条件（可对齐性和可比性），提出边际优势累积(MAA)：构建差分信号实现跨批次可比性，通过EMA逐操作累积带符号证据，通过语义身份合并确保跨批次可追溯性。

Result: 在4个基准和4个目标模型的16个设置中，MAA在14个设置上取得最佳结果，优于现有批量级蒸馏基线，在多数设置中匹配或超越在线方法，优化阶段token消耗减少约75%。

Conclusion: MAA作为一种后处理架构，有效解决了批量式蒸馏中的跨批次矛盾反馈问题，实现了高效且稳定的知识蒸馏。

Abstract: In batch-style trace distillation, the same memory operation may receive contradictory feedback across different batches. Existing methods lack a cross-batch, operation-level evidence accumulation mechanism, making it impossible to distinguish stably effective operations from accidental hits. This paper formalizes the requirement as two structural conditions, alignability and comparability, and proposes Marginal Advantage Accumulation (MAA). MAA constructs differential signals to make them comparable across batches, accumulates signed evidence per operation via EMA, and ensures cross-batch traceability through semantic identity merging. As a post-processing architecture, MAA achieves the best results in 14 out of 16 settings across 4 benchmarks and 4 target models, consistently outperforming existing batch-level distillation baselines and matching or surpassing online alternatives in most settings, while reducing optimization-phase token consumption by approximately 75%.

</details>


### [116] [Contagion Networks: Evaluator Bias Propagation in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20493)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"传染网络"框架，量化评估者偏差在LLM多智能体系统中的传播。实验发现同模型智能体间偏差传播系数γ∈[0.157,0.352]，存在三种传播机制，将评估委员会从k=1增至k=3可减少72.4%的有效传染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多智能体系统中作为评估者时，其系统性的评估偏差会通过智能体网络传播，但目前缺乏对这一传播过程的量化度量框架。

Method: 提出Contagion Networks理论框架，通过跨智能体传染矩阵Γ_N量化偏差传播；使用DeepSeek-chat构建3智能体实验，设计三种不同的评估偏差画像（结构化、平衡、基于证据），计算并分析传染系数。

Result: 偏差在同模型智能体间持续传播（γ∈[0.157,0.352]），比跨模型系数弱3-5倍；识别出由谱半径ρ(Γ_N)决定的三种传播机制；将评估委员会从k=1增至k=3可减少72.4%的有效传染。

Conclusion: 同模型智能体处于"抑制机制"；增加评估委员会规模是一种可行的缓解策略。

Abstract: When large language models serve as evaluators in multi-agent systems, their systematic evaluation biases propagate through the agent network. We introduce Contagion Networks, a formal framework for measuring how evaluator biases spread across interacting LLM agents. In a controlled 3-agent experiment using DeepSeek-chat with three distinct evaluator bias profiles (structured, balanced, evidence-based), we measure the Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_3 and find that evaluator biases consistently propagate between agents (gamma in [0.157, 0.352]), even within the same underlying model. We identify three propagation regimes governed by the spectral radius rho(Gamma_N), and demonstrate that homogeneous-model agents produce contagion coefficients 3-5x weaker than cross-model coefficients observed in prior work (MM-EPC: gamma approx 0.85-1.3), placing them in the suppression regime. We show that increasing evaluator committee size from k=1 to k=3 reduces effective contagion by 72.4%, providing an actionable mitigation strategy. We release the open-source Contagion Network experimental framework.

</details>


### [117] [Execution-State Capsules: Graph-Bound Execution-State Checkpoint and Restore for Low-Latency, Small-Batch, On-Device Physical-AI Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20537)
*Liang Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出执行状态胶囊（execution-state capsules），一种面向低延迟、小批量、设备端物理AI服务的图绑定检查点与恢复机制。FlashRT系统基于此实现完整执行边界的快照、恢复、分支和回滚，在RTX 5090上实现亚毫秒级GPU恢复，TTFT加速比从2K token的3.9倍提升至16K token的27倍，并与高吞吐KV缓存服务形成互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统主要通过分页或基数KV缓存实现前缀复用，但这种方式仅管理执行状态的一个位置片段（KV缓存）。对于低延迟、小批量、设备端物理AI服务（如交互式LLM智能体、语音系统、机器人策略），需要频繁分支、重置、中断和重新进入，且受严格响应时间预算约束，现有方法无法满足需求。

Method: 提出执行状态胶囊（execution-state capsules），即图绑定的检查点与恢复机制，在提交边界保存完整可恢复状态。FlashRT是基于此的白盒、后端内核运行时，其NVIDIA CUDA后端在连续静态缓冲区上执行捕获的图计划，无需块表间接寻址。由于活动状态是命名缓冲区的闭集，胶囊可对整个执行边界（包括KV状态、循环状态、卷积状态、多token预测状态、元数据）进行快照、恢复、分支或回滚。

Result: 在RTX 5090上，胶囊恢复在存储状态级别字节精确，在贪心解码下token一致。仅KV的消融实验出现分歧，证明循环状态（recurrent state）承载负载。GPU驻留的快照与恢复达到亚毫秒级，TTFT加速比从2K token的3.9倍增长至16K token的27倍。在Jetson AGX Thor和DGX Spark上保持相同的正确性与结构特性。

Conclusion: 执行状态胶囊并非替代高吞吐KV缓存服务，而是定义了一种互补的、以延迟优先的服务点，用于显式执行状态复用。该方法将复用从token寻址的KV片段转移到图绑定的执行状态边界，为交互式物理AI场景提供了新的优化路径。

Abstract: Mainstream LLM serving systems reuse prefix work mainly through paged or radix key-value (KV) caches. This is highly effective for high-throughput, high-concurrency serving, but it manages only one positional fragment of execution state: the KV cache. We study the opposite regime: low-latency, small-batch, on-device physical-AI serving, where interactive LLM agents, speech systems, and robot policies repeatedly branch, reset, interrupt, and re-enter under tight responsiveness budgets. We introduce execution-state capsules, a graph-bound checkpoint and restore mechanism for the complete restorable state at a committed boundary. FlashRT is a white-box, backend-facing kernel runtime whose evaluated NVIDIA CUDA backend runs captured graph plans over contiguous static buffers with no block-table indirection. Because the live state is a closed set of named buffers, a capsule can snapshot, restore, fork, or roll back the whole execution boundary, including KV, recurrent state, convolution state, MTP state, and metadata. This moves reuse from token-addressed KV fragments to graph-bound execution-state boundaries. On an RTX 5090, capsule restore is byte-exact at the stored-state level and token-identical under greedy decode. A KV-only ablation diverges, showing that recurrent state is load-bearing. GPU-resident snapshot and restore are sub-millisecond, and TTFT speedup over cold prefill grows from 3.9x at 2k tokens to 27x at 16k tokens. On Jetson AGX Thor and DGX Spark, the same correctness and structural properties hold. Capsules are not a replacement for high-throughput KV-cache serving; they define a complementary latency-first serving point for explicit execution-state reuse.

</details>


### [118] [Multi-Task Bayesian In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20538)
*Qingyang Zhu,Eric Karl Oermann,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多任务上下文学习框架，用于摊销分层贝叶斯预测推断。该方法通过将先验信息显式表示为上下文数据集的前缀，训练Transformer在序列化的先验任务和目标任务上进行学习，使其能够跨不同先验家族自适应预测。实验表明，该方法在分布外先验和高维潜在结构场景下匹配了贝叶斯最优预测器，同时速度提升数个数量级，并在实际时空温度预测任务中验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯预测推断在不确定性量化、数据效率和鲁棒泛化方面具有优势，但精确推断难以处理，可扩展近似方法计算昂贵或需限制性建模假设。现有先验数据拟合模型和上下文模型与训练先验的支持集紧密耦合，缺乏在测试时适应新先验的机制，导致分布偏移下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出多任务上下文学习框架：将先验信息显式表示为上下文数据集的前缀（prefix），训练Transformer在序列化的先验任务和目标任务上学习，从而跨不同先验家族进行自适应的摊销贝叶斯预测推断。

Result: 在包含分布外先验和高维潜在结构的难度递增的评估套件上，该方法匹配了贝叶斯最优预测器，同时计算速度快数个数量级；在真实世界时空温度预测基准上也展现了实际有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了现有摊销贝叶斯方法在分布偏移下鲁棒性不足的问题，实现了跨先验家族的自适应预测，兼具计算效率与预测性能。

Abstract: Bayesian predictive inference provides a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, data efficiency, and robust generalization. However, exact inference is often intractable, and scalable approximations may remain computationally expensive or require restrictive modeling assumptions that degrade predictive performance. Prior-Data Fitted and in-context models have recently emerged as an amortized alternative by learning to map datasets directly to predictive distributions, but existing approaches are tightly coupled to the support of the training prior and lack explicit mechanisms for adapting to new priors at test time, resulting in limited robustness under distribution shift. We introduce a multi-task in-context learning framework for amortized hierarchical Bayesian predictive inference that explicitly represents prior information as a prefix of in-context datasets. A transformer trained on sequences of prior and target tasks learns to adapt its predictions across families of priors. On a suite of evaluations with increasing difficulty, including out-of-meta-distribution priors and priors with high-dimensional latent structures, our method matches oracle Bayesian predictors while being orders of magnitude faster. We further demonstrate its practical relevance on a real-world spatiotemporal temperature prediction benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/martianmartina/multi-task-bayesian-icl/.

</details>


### [119] [Predictability as a Fine-Grained Measure for Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20546)
*Linda Lu,Karthik Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可预测性隐私"（Privacy via Predictability）框架，通过衡量攻击者在观察到算法输出后预测敏感信息能力的增量来量化隐私泄露，与差分隐私（DP）不可比较但互补，并基于GMM方法给出了渐近分析及扰动方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私（DP）虽然提供了严格的个体级隐私保证，但其最坏情况性质导致隐私-准确性权衡代价高昂。现有框架无法细致刻画攻击者已有知识对隐私泄露的影响，需要更细粒度的隐私度量方法。

Method: 提出可预测性隐私框架，显式建模攻击者的核心知识（被泄露的部分数据集，由随机过程生成）和指定的查询族；利用广义矩估计（GMM）分析平稳遍历混合过程中可预测性的渐近性质；设计可预测性校准的输出扰动机制。

Result: 可预测性与DP一般不可比较（一个很小而另一个可以很大）；在最坏情况（仅一人未泄露、所有二值查询敏感）下，可预测性蕴含互信息DP；GMM框架可给出渐近可预测性分析；基于此提出校准扰动方案。

Conclusion: 可预测性隐私提供了比DP更细粒度的隐私度量，能够针对特定的敏感信息和攻击者模型进行定制化隐私保护，可与DP互补使用以实现细粒度隐私控制。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) ensures rigorous individual-level privacy guarantees against even the most knowledgeable attackers, but its worst-case nature can impose a costly privacy-accuracy tradeoff. We introduce privacy via predictability, a fine-grained framework that explicitly incorporates the attacker's core knowledge, a compromised portion of the dataset generated by a stochastic process, and a specified family of queries. Predictability measures privacy leakage as the incremental gain in an attacker's ability to predict sensitive information about unknown individuals after observing the algorithm's output, beyond what can already be inferred from the compromised data. We show that predictability and DP are generally incomparable: each can be small while the other is large. However, in the worst-case regime where all but one individual is compromised, and all binary queries are considered sensitive, predictability implies mutual-information DP. More generally, predictability provides a finer-grained privacy metric tailored to specific sensitive information and specific attacker models. We introduce a general framework, using the generalized method of moments (GMM), to analyze asymptotic predictability when the compromised data is generated by a stationary, ergodic, mixing process. Using this analysis, we derive a predictability-calibrated output perturbation scheme for ERM. Our approach is complementary to DP and can be used alongside DP to provide fine-grained privacy control.

</details>


### [120] [The Token Is a Group Element: On Lie-Algebra Attention over Matrix Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20547)
*Przemyslaw Musialski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出李代数注意力（Lie-Algebra Attention），将注意力token定义为矩阵李群元素（裸变换，无特征载荷），相对姿态的代数范数作为闭合形式的注意力分数，无需不可约表示、球谐函数或学习核，可处理包含尺度和剪切的仿射全帧群，在SE(2)、SO(3)和Aff(2)上的实验表明其闭合形式分数匹配甚至超越学习型MLP核，参数减少50-80倍，且向量token基线破坏不变性达5-12个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制中token通常为特征向量，处理群结构时依赖不可约表示或满射指数映射方法，无法处理包含尺度和剪切的非紧致非交换仿射全帧群（如Aff(2)）。

Method: 将token直接定义为矩阵李群元素$g_i$，相对几何为$g_i^{-1}g_j$，注意力分数为$s_{ij} = -\|\log(g_i^{-1}g_j)\|_\lambda^2/\tau$，即负平方代数范数，在块加权Frobenius内积下构成规范邻近核，无需任何表示论工具。

Result: 在SE(2)、SO(3)和Aff(2)序列补全实验中，闭合形式分数匹配学习型MLP核并在SE(2)上超越，参数减少50-80倍；向量token基线破坏不变性达5-12个数量级。

Conclusion: 李代数注意力为任意矩阵李群（包括非紧致非交换仿射群）提供了无需表示论器械的规范注意力构造，其固有不变性在理论和实验上均优于传统方法。

Abstract: We place the attention token on the group: a token is an element $g_i$ of a matrix Lie group $G$ -- a bare transformation, with no feature payload and no external action $ρ(g)$ carrying it. To our knowledge this is the first attention construction whose tokens are bare matrix Lie group elements: their score is the closed-form algebra norm of the relative pose rather than a learned kernel, and it reaches the affine full-frame groups that every irrep- or surjective-exp-based method must exclude. We call it Lie-Algebra Attention. Once tokens are group elements, the rest follows with none of the usual representation-theoretic machinery. The relative geometry of a pair is canonical, $g_i^{-1} g_j$, so the pairwise invariant $w_{ij} = \log(g_i^{-1} g_j)$ is intrinsic rather than designed; equivariance under the diagonal $G$-action is tautological, and the cocycle condition holds automatically. The attention score is the negative squared algebra norm, $s_{ij} = -\|\log(g_i^{-1} g_j)\|_λ^2/τ$: the canonical proximity kernel under a block-weighted Frobenius inner product, with no irreducible representations, spherical harmonics, Clebsch-Gordan products, or learned kernel. The construction applies to any matrix Lie group on a chosen logarithm chart containing the relative poses, including the non-compact non-abelian affine groups with scale and shear that no vector-token attention method reaches: neither the irrep tradition nor surjective-exp methods. Three sequence-completion experiments, on SE(2), SO(3), and Aff(2), bear this out: the closed-form score matches a learned MLP kernel on the same invariant and outperforms it on SE(2), using 50 to 80x fewer score parameters, while a vector-token baseline breaks invariance by five to twelve orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [121] [Optimal Deterministic Multicalibration and Omniprediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20557)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了多校准中随机化是否对最优样本复杂度必要的问题，给出了一个达到极小极大最优样本复杂度的确定性预测器算法，并推广到结果不可区分性、全能预测和泛预测等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前所有达到极小极大最优样本复杂度 $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ 的多校准预测器都是随机化的，确定性预测器的样本复杂度明显更差。CLNR26 等文献明确提出了随机化是否必要的问题，本文旨在回答这一开放问题。

Method: 提出了一个能达到极小极大最优样本复杂度的确定性多校准算法，并将其推广到处理有限或有限覆盖测试集合的结果不可区分性（OI），进而得到确定性全能预测器和泛预测器。

Result: 首次给出了达到最优样本复杂度的确定性多校准预测器，解决了随机化必要性的开放问题；同时给出了确定性全能预测器和泛预测器的最优样本复杂度结果，解决了 OKK25 和 BHHLZ25 中提出的开放问题。

Conclusion: 随机化对于多校准的最优样本复杂度并非必要，确定性预测器同样可以达到 $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ 的极小极大最优样本复杂度。

Abstract: A model is multicalibrated on a collection of group weights $G$ if it is calibrated -- i.e. unbiased even conditional on its prediction -- not just overall, but also after reweighting contexts by each $g \in G$. It is a useful property for many downstream applications and is a basic desideratum of trustworthy machine learning. Before this work, all predictors known to attain the minimax-optimal $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ sample complexity rate for $\varepsilon$-multicalibration were randomized, while deterministic predictors were known only with substantially worse sample complexity. Whether randomization is necessary for optimal sample complexity in multicalibration was explicitly asked by [CLNR26] and implicitly in several prior works.
  We resolve this open problem by giving a minimax-optimal multicalibration algorithm that outputs a deterministic predictor. We then generalize the algorithm to produce optimal deterministic predictors that satisfy outcome indistinguishability (OI) with respect to finite or finitely covered collections of tests. As an application, this also gives deterministic omnipredictors and panpredictors with optimal sample complexity, resolving open problems posed by [OKK25] and [BHHLZ25].

</details>


### [122] [How Transparent is DiffusionGemma?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20560)
*Joshua Engels,Callum McDougall,Bilal Chughtai,Janos Kramar,Senthoran Rajamanoharan,Cindy Wu,Arthur Conmy,Asic Q Chen,Jean Tarbouriech,Min Ma,Brendan O'Donoghue,João Gabriel Lopes de Oliveira,Rohin Shah,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究DiffusionGemma（扩散语言模型）的推理透明性，发现其虽在连续潜空间中计算，但可通过可解释的token瓶颈将不透明序列深度降至仅Gemma 4的1.1倍。然而其算法透明性因所有token可同时变化而更难，研究发现了非时序推理、token/序列模糊等扩散特有现象，最终显示其可监控性与Gemma 4相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理透明性对于理解模型决策、减轻误用和调试异常行为至关重要。扩散模型在连续潜空间中执行大量计算，引发其推理是否更不透明的问题。

Method: 将透明性分解为变量透明性（理解中间计算状态）和算法透明性（用状态重建推理过程）。通过可解释的token瓶颈映射信息流，降低不透明序列深度；通过案例研究分析扩散特有推理现象；通过下游任务测试可监控性。

Result: 变量透明性方面，将不透明序列深度从28.6倍Gemma 4降至1.1倍；算法透明性方面发现了非时序推理、token/序列模糊、中间上下文推理等扩散特有现象；可监控性与Gemma 4相当。

Conclusion: 扩散模型虽表面更不透明，但可通过设计缓解变量透明性问题，其算法透明性面临更大挑战但存在独特的可解释性机会，整体可监控性未显著劣于自回归模型。

Abstract: LLM reasoning transparency is a critical affordance for understanding model decisions, mitigating misuse and misalignment, and debugging surprising model behaviors. However, DiffusionGemma performs a larger fraction of its computation in a continuous latent space; does this make its reasoning less transparent? We study this question by decomposing transparency into two components: variable transparency, whether we understand intermediate snapshots of a model's computational state; and algorithmic transparency, whether we can use these snapshots to reconstruct the process by which the model arrived at its outputs. Naively, DiffusionGemma has poor variable transparency: its opaque serial depth, the amount of serial computation that occurs in between interpretable model states, seems at first 28.6X higher than the corresponding autoregressive Gemma 4 model. However, we show that we can map the information flowing between denoising steps through an interpretable token bottleneck with no decrease in downstream performance. Treating these intermediate states as interpretable reduces the opaque serial depth to just 1.1X that of Gemma 4. Algorithmic transparency is harder for diffusion models than for autoregressive models because all token predictions in the canvas can change at every denoising step, giving the model the power to implement complicated distributed algorithms during the denoising process. To begin bridging this gap, we conduct a suite of interpretability case studies, uncovering initial evidence of novel diffusion-specific phenomena such as non-chronological reasoning, token and sequence smearing, and intermediate-context reasoning. Finally, we test monitorability, a key application of transparency that measures whether model outputs are useful for downstream tasks. We find that DiffusionGemma is similarly monitorable to Gemma 4.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Supporting Design Decisions in Rule-Based Model Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19342)
*Dejan Stojimirovic,Sinisa Neskovic*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在基于规则的模型转换中显式建模和管理设计决策的方法，通过决策模型、绑定模型和配置模型将设计决策与转换实现分离，并建立了形式化数学框架，通过扩展领域特定语言验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型转换中设计决策嵌入在转换源代码中，导致灵活性、可重用性和可追溯性受限。需要将设计决策显式化、分离管理。

Method: 提出三种机制：决策模型（独立于源语言捕获决策及选项）、绑定模型（连接决策与元模型概念）、配置模型（记录元素级选项选择）；运行时动态解析转换规则中的变点；建立形式化数学框架定义核心概念并证明性质。

Result: 通过扩展嵌入式领域特定语言支持可变性，并以ER-to-Relational转换示例验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 显式建模设计决策可提升转换的灵活性、可重用性和可追溯性；形式化框架保证了转换的正确性；实际架构支持集成到现有MDE工具中。

Abstract: Model Driven Engineering relies on model transformations to automate the derivation of target models or source code from source models. However, the design decisions that govern how source elements are mapped to target artifacts typically remain embedded in the transformation source code, limiting flexibility, reusability, and traceability. This paper proposes an approach to explicitly model and manage design decisions in rule-based model transformations. Design decisions are separated from transformation implementation through three mechanisms. First, a decision model captures design decisions and their options independently of any source modeling language for a given transformation domain. Second, a binding model connects these decisions to specific metamodel concepts in a given source modeling language, enabling reuse of the same design knowledge across transformations from similar languages. Third, a configuration model records the specific option chosen for each applicable source model element, with defaults pre-selected automatically. During execution, variability points in transformation rules are resolved dynamically according to configured choices. A trace model records which rules and options were applied to produce each target element. We establish a formal mathematical framework that defines the core concepts of variability-based transformations and proves key properties of the resulting transformations. The formal concepts are realized in a practical architecture of four interconnected artifact models. We demonstrate practical feasibility by extending an existing embedded domain-specific language for model-driven engineering with variability support and illustrate the approach with a complete ER-to-Relational transformation example.

</details>


### [124] [AgentArmor: A Framework, Evaluation, \& Mitigation of Coding Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19380)
*Kenneth Ge,Andre Assis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究AI编码代理在软件工程部署中的三种失败模式（欠规格、能力错误、代理工具错误），并提出AgentArmor代理工具修改方案来缓解这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理的广泛部署暴露出罕见但极具破坏性的失败模式，现有系统缺乏对这些失败模式的系统分类和有效缓解措施。

Method: 通过8种不同评估（共20个编码环境和59个合成对话模板），基于真实部署失败案例，设计AgentArmor方案，包含扩展系统提示、独立命令分类器、"三振出局"策略、确定性护栏和自主上下文编辑工具。

Result: AgentArmor在统计显著数量的样本上表现出更高的安全性，有效缓解了三种失败模式。

Conclusion: 为当前编码代理提供了具体缓解措施，并为未来代理工具功能设计提出了指导哲学。

Abstract: Software engineering and deployment are increasingly being delegated to AI coding agents. The scale of their adoption is surfacing rare, but highly destructive, failure modes. In this paper, we study these failure modes as stemming from three distinct mechanisms: underspecification, where default model behavior is unsafe; capability errors, where the safe action is available but the model does not adhere to it due to bias or capability limitations; and agent harness errors, where the model fails to execute the safe action through the harness. We evaluate these across 8 different evaluations, each inspired by real-life deployment failures, totaling 20 coding environments and 59 synthetic transcript templates. Based on this evaluation, we propose AgentArmor, an agent harness modification, to mitigate these errors. By adding an extended system prompt, a separate command classifier, a ``3 strikes'' policy, deterministic guardrails, and tools for the agent to edit its own context, we show that AgentArmor is safer across a statistically significant number of samples. Thus, we suggest concrete mitigations for current coding agents and a design philosophy for future agent harness features.

</details>


### [125] [DynAMO:Dynamic Asset Management Orchestration via Topological Multi-Agent Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19382)
*Kanishk Kushwaha,Vikrant Vinod Bansode,Harsh Vardhan,Dhaval C. Patel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DynAMO是一个基于Plan-then-Execute架构的工业资产管理引擎，通过动态识别独立任务实现并行工作流执行，在AssetOpsBench基准上相比串行编排将端到端延迟中位数降低1.6倍（高度并行场景达1.8倍），同时保持结构正确性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的智能体虽能为工业资产生命周期提供端到端自动化，但在实际工业4.0部署中面临延迟、并发不稳定性和安全风险三大挑战，亟需一个兼顾效率与安全性的部署就绪引擎。

Method: 提出DynAMO引擎，采用Plan-then-Execute架构生成可验证的工作流图，支持SequentialWorkflow（拓扑执行）和ParallelWorkflow（依赖感知并发）两种模式，通过动态识别独立任务在保证结构正确性和安全性的前提下实现受控推理重叠以提升效率。

Result: 在AssetOpsBench基准的六项控制实验中：并行执行中位数延迟降低1.6倍（高度并行场景1.8倍）；LLM推理与编排仍占执行时间90%以上；结构化上下文剪枝减少约30%推理延迟；在受控故障注入下保持功能正确且优雅降级；并行调度降低了延迟方差。

Conclusion: DynAMO为工业4.0自动化流水线中可扩展、安全且延迟感知的智能体部署提供了实用蓝图。

Abstract: While LLM-powered agents offer end-to-end automation for industrial asset lifecycles, real-world Industry 4.0 deployment is hindered by latency, concurrency instability, and safety risks. We present DynAMO (Dynamic Asset Management Orchestration), a deployment-ready engine using a Plan-then-Execute architecture to generate verifiable workflow graphs. DynAMO supports both SequentialWorkflow (topological execution) and ParallelWorkflow (dependency-aware concurrency). By dynamically identifying independent tasks, DynAMO preserves structural correctness and safety while significantly improving efficiency through controlled reasoning overlap.
  Across six controlled experiments on the AssetOpsBench industrial benchmark, DynAMO demonstrates substantial performance and robustness gains. Parallel execution reduces end-to-end latency by a median of 1.6x over sequential orchestration, rising to 1.8x on highly parallelizable workflows. After instrumenting external tool calls with realistic latencies, a latency decomposition shows that LLM reasoning and orchestration still account for more than 90% of execution time, identifying model inference as the primary system bottleneck. Structured context pruning reduces inference latency by approximately 30%, and DynAMO maintains correct functional behaviour (task completion, agent sequencing, and output quality) while exhibiting graceful degradation under controlled fault injection. Reproducibility analysis further confirms stable execution under repeated runs, with parallel scheduling reducing latency variance. These findings establish DynAMO as a practical blueprint for scalable, safe, and latency-aware agent deployment in Industry 4.0 automation pipelines. Code is available at: https://github.com/kushwaha001/DynAMO

</details>


### [126] [Bistable by Construction: Wall-Clock-Calibrated State Monitors Have No Moment-Detection Regime at Agent Cadence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19386)
*Manvendra Modgil*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发现之前报告的智能体运行时监控器"状态饱和陷阱"实际上是由纯累加器（dt=0）导致的错误结论。核心问题在于监控器的动态校准方式：基于挂钟时间（wall-clock）的泄漏积分器在智能体流上存在双稳态（要么持续报警，要么完全静默），而基于样本时间（sample-time）的CUSUM则不受影响。最终结论：挂钟时间校准的泄漏积分器无法在智能体流上作为瞬态检测器使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正此前发布的"状态饱和陷阱"（State Saturation Trap）错误结论，并深入探究监控器动态校准方式（样本时间vs挂钟时间）对智能体运行时监控行为的影响。

Method: 首先对之前发布的论文发布勘误（erratum v2），将原始缺陷视为一个实验。随后进行预注册实验：在20条轨迹上对均匀间隔（dt∈{0..600}s）进行扫描，对比挂钟时间触发器和样本时间CUSUM的行为差异，并分析上升沿触发器（rising-edge trigger with hysteresis）的表现。

Result: 挂钟时间水平触发器存在两个截然不同的状态：dt≤1s时持续报警（20/20，中位数18次触发）；dt≥60s时完全静默。每个关键dt值落在(1,30]s区间。实际智能体运行的中位数延迟为1.53s（p90 2.33s），恰好处于陷阱状态内。最小挂钟时间累加器复现了相同的悬崖效应，而样本时间CUSUM完全不受dt影响（20/20）。上升沿触发器在所有条件下每轨迹触发0-3次。

Conclusion: 挂钟时间校准的泄漏积分器监控器在智能体流上不存在能作为瞬态检测器工作的状态空间；过渡检测（rising-edge trigger）在每个节奏下都能逃逸陷阱，但无法恢复人工干预的时间点。

Abstract: Runtime monitors for autonomous agents commonly threshold an accumulated internal state - a behavioural baseline, a drift statistic, or, in our prior work, a modelled affective state. We previously reported a State Saturation Trap: threshold-on-state triggers over a continuous affect engine become near-constant alarms on SWE-bench debugging agents (Modgil 2026). A post-release audit found the engine received dt=0 between actions, so its exponential decay never operated: the published trap is a pure-accumulator result. We correct the record (erratum, v2) and treat the flaw as an experiment. The key variable it exposes is whether a monitor's dynamics are calibrated in sample time (per observation, as in CUSUM) or wall-clock time (half-lives in seconds, as in affect models and EMA baselines). On fixed-rate streams these coincide; on agent streams, where inter-action time varies by orders of magnitude, they do not. A pre-registered sweep over uniform intervals (dt in {0..600}s) on 20 trajectories shows the wall-clock level trigger has two regimes: at dt<=1s a constant alarm (20/20; median 18 firings); at dt>=60s silent. Every critical dt lies in (1,30]s. Real agent runs measure latency at median 1.53s (p90 2.33s); real coding cadence sits inside the trap regime, vindicating the empirical finding under a corrected mechanism. The structure is a property of the calibration class, not the engine: a minimal wall-clock accumulator over the raw error stream reproduces the same cliff, while a sample-time CUSUM over the identical stream is exactly dt-invariant (20/20). A rising-edge trigger with hysteresis fires 0-3 times per trajectory in every condition. We conclude that wall-clock-calibrated leaky-integrator monitors admit no regime in which they act as moment detectors on agent streams; transition detection escapes the trap at every cadence, but does not recover human intervention timing.

</details>


### [127] [Interpretable and Verifiable Hardware Generation with LLM-Driven Stepwise Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19387)
*You Li,Samuel Mandell,David Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出结合LLM创造力与形式化方法严谨性的硬件生成框架，通过定义覆盖设计决策和硬件特性的变换规则集，使LLM智能体能够迭代地将设计规范转换为正确性有保障的RTL程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件开发生成中表现出色，但在寄存器传输级（RTL）硬件生成中存在幻觉问题（引入语义和逻辑错误），而芯片设计和制造的高风险性使得硬件工程师不愿依赖LLM进行RTL生成。

Method: 设计了一套覆盖多种设计决策和硬件特性的变换规则，LLM智能体通过迭代应用这些规则，将设计规范逐步转换为RTL程序，利用形式化方法保证转换过程正确性。

Result: 实验结果表明该框架在RTL生成任务上兼具有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: LLM与形式化方法的结合是解决LLM在硬件生成中幻觉问题的可行路径，为高风险领域的LLM应用提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success in software development. However, they are susceptible to hallucinations, meaning that they can introduce subtle semantic and logical errors. Due to the high stakes in chip design and manufacturing, hardware engineers are still reluctant to rely on LLMs for register-transfer level (RTL) generation. In this paper, we propose a hardware generation framework that combines the creativity and broad knowledge of LLMs with the explainability and mathematical rigor of formal methods. Specifically, we devise a set of transformation rules that cover various design decisions and hardware features. By iteratively applying these rules, an LLM agent can convert a design specification into an RTL program with guaranteed correctness. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the framework.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond the GUI Paradigm: Do Mobile Agents Need the Phone Screen?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19388)
*Li Gu,Zihuan Jiang,Linqiang Guo,Zhixiang Chi,Ziqiang Wang,Huan Liu,Yuanhao Yu,Tse-Hsun Chen,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文论证移动平台上的命令行界面（CLI）应被赋予与图形界面（GUI）同等重要的地位。实验表明，无需移动端后训练，Claude Code (Opus 4.7) 在AndroidWorld和MobileWorld上达到71.8%和51.9%，超越所有可复现的GUI基线。作者还引入CLI-Advantage任务套件，涵盖批量操作、多条件过滤等五类任务，所有CLI代理均全面超越GUI基线，且步骤更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前移动代理研究主要聚焦GUI范式，但移动平台也提供CLI接口，可直接访问设备服务与数据。作者认为CLI应获得与GUI同等的重视，以弥补GUI范式在处理批量操作、多条件过滤、跨应用工作流等任务上的不足。

Method: 在AndroidWorld和MobileWorld两个基准上，评估三种编码代理（Claude Code、Terminus-2、mini-swe-agent）跨四种模型API的表现，与三种可复现GUI基线对比。代理未经过任何移动端后训练。同时构建Oracle CLI解决方案确定上限，并设计CLI-Advantage任务套件（45个模板，五类任务）进行专项评估。

Result: Claude Code (Opus 4.7) 在AndroidWorld达71.8%（GUI基线最高69.3%），MobileWorld达51.9%（GUI基线最高43.2%）。所有CLI配置均具竞争力。Oracle上限为88.8%（AndroidWorld）和86.3%（MobileWorld）。CLI-Advantage套件中，所有CLI代理全面优于所有GUI基线，且每任务步骤数更少（10.7 vs. 18.6）。

Conclusion: 移动CLI代理范式极具潜力，无需移动端专门训练即可超越GUI方法，且在处理日常用户意图方面具有显著优势。作者将开源所有实现、Oracle解决方案、任务套件及评估基础设施，以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in mobile agents are dominated by the GUI paradigm, in which agents perceive UI information and emit screen interactions. However, mobile platforms also expose a command-line interface (CLI) that provides direct access to device services and data. We argue CLI deserves first-class consideration alongside GUI. We evaluate three coding agents (Claude Code, Terminus-2, mini-swe-agent) across four model APIs on AndroidWorld and MobileWorld without any mobile-specific post-training, comparing against three reproducible GUI baselines (GUI-Owl-1.5-32B, MAI-UI, Qwen3-VL-32B). Claude Code (Opus 4.7) reaches 71.8\% and 51.9\%, outperforming every reproducible GUI baseline (69.3/68.1/57.8\% on AndroidWorld; 43.2/26.3/13.3\% on MobileWorld), while every other CLI configuration remains competitive. To establish the paradigm's ceiling, we provide oracle CLI solutions that reach 88.8\% on AndroidWorld (103/116 tasks CLI-solvable) and 86.3\% on MobileWorld (101/117 tasks CLI-solvable), indicating substantial room for future improvement. To cover everyday user intents beyond the GUI scope, we introduce the \textbf{CLI-Advantage Task Suite}, comprising 45 templates across five categories: bulk operations, multi-condition filtering, aggregation, cross-app workflows, and hidden device state. Every CLI agent outperforms every GUI baseline in all five categories, with substantially fewer steps per task (10.7 vs.\ 18.6). To support future research on mobile CLI agents, we will open-source agent implementations, oracle solutions, the CLI-Advantage suite, and evaluation infrastructure.

</details>


### [129] [Execution-bound advisory automation for agentic AI: a reproducible AIBOM-driven CSAF-VEX framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19390)
*Petar Radanliev,Omar Santos,Carsten Maple,Kay Atefi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一个协议驱动框架，将SBOM和AIBOM工件绑定到确定性环境捕获和结构化运行时遥测中，通过组合静态和运行时证据生成CSAF VEX公告，并在约10000个组件条目上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏将SBOM/AIBOM工件与运行时遥测相结合以计算可利用性并生成可信安全公告的系统性框架，需要一种确定性可验证的方法。

Method: 构建协议驱动框架，绑定SBOM和AIBOM工件到确定性环境捕获与结构化运行时遥测；从声明工件、观察激活条件和强制执行策略计算可利用性；基于静态与运行时证据生成CSAF VEX公告，进行加密签名并通过确定性重放验证。

Result: 在约10000个组件条目上进行了评估，涵盖50到5000个组件的合成Agentic AI工作负载，使用了OSV、GitHub Advisory、KEV和EPSS数据集。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效将SBOM/AIBOM与运行时遥测结合，通过确定性重放验证生成的可利用性评估和CSAF VEX公告具有可信性和可复现性。

Abstract: A protocol driven framework is presented that binds SBOM and AIBOM artefacts to deterministic environment capture and structured runtime telemetry. Exploitability is computed from declared artefacts, observed activation conditions, and enforced execution policies. CSAF VEX advisories are generated from combined static and runtime evidence, cryptographically signed, and validated through deterministic replay. Evaluation uses approximately 10000 component entries across synthetic Agentic AI workloads 50 to 5000 components, incorporating OSV, GitHub Advisory, KEV, and EPSS datasets.

</details>


### [130] [DevOps and General Developers: Insights from Stack Overflow's 2023 Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19395)
*Hasan Abdulla,Fatema AlJazeeri,Fawzi AlBalooshi,Jaflah Al-Ammary*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于Stack Overflow 2023开发者调查的定量分析，研究DevOps专家与通用软件开发者在工具、技术、方法论和人口统计方面的角色差异，发现两者在工具偏好上无显著差异，但存在年龄分布和工作模式适应上的不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献缺乏对DevOps专家和通用软件开发者这两个专业群体在工具使用、技术偏好、方法论及人口统计特征方面差异化角色的系统性研究，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用定量研究方法，对Stack Overflow 2023开发者调查数据进行对比分析，使用Python的Pandas库进行数据分析，聚焦于技术偏好、人口统计信息和职业经验的比较。

Result: DevOps专家与通用开发者在工具和技术偏好上无显著差异，两者均使用Docker和Kubernetes等工具强调效率与自动化；通用开发者使用更多样化工具应对不同角色需求；人口统计方面，通用开发者更年轻，DevOps专家多为职业中期从业者；两组群体均正在适应远程和混合工作模式。

Conclusion: DevOps专家与通用开发者在软件开发生态中扮演互补角色，DevOps的重要性日益增长，研究为学术界和行业从业者理解软件开发者角色动态演变提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Purpose: To investigate the distinct roles of DevOps specialists and general software developers, examining their varying use of tools, technologies, methodologies, and demographics in the current software development environment. In addition, to differentiate these two professional groups regarding their unique contributions and challenges in the field.
  Design/Methodology/Approach: The research uses a quantitative approach to analyze data from the Stack Overflow 2023 Developer Survey. It focuses on a comparative analysis of technological preferences, demographic information, and professional experiences between DevOps specialists and general developers, highlighting key trends and differences. The data analysis was conducted using Python's Pandas library for data analysis.
  Findings: The research indicates no significant difference in the tool and technology preferences between DevOps specialists and general software developers, highlighting their complementary roles. DevOps specialists and general software developers use tools like Docker and Kubernetes, emphasizing efficiency and automation. While general developers employ diverse tools for various role demands, demographic trends show younger general developers and mid-career DevOps professionals. This age range reflects growing experience in DevOps, and both groups are adapting to remote and hybrid work models in the evolving tech industry.
  Practical Implications: This research offers perspectives on the dynamic roles within software development, emphasizing the growing importance of DevOps. It is a valuable resource for academic and industry professionals to understand the evolving dynamics in software development roles.
  Originality/Value: This research fills a significant gap in the existing literature regarding the evolving dynamics of software development roles.

</details>


### [131] [JustDiag!: A Diagnostic Justification Engine for Accountable Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19407)
*Tingzhu Bi,Xinrui Jiang,Xun Zhang,Pengcheng Su,Congjie He,Jinglin Li,Ping Wang,Meng Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出JustDiag，一种面向根因分析的可问责诊断推理引擎，通过维护显式的过程状态（证据、发现、竞争假设、矛盾、下一步检查）来提升诊断透明度和可审计性。在66个真实事件上，相比对照组，JustDiag在最终答案质量和过程质量上均表现更优，虽因更谨慎的未终结判断而略降低完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能生成流畅的根因分析，但在高风险运维场景中，仅凭流畅的最终答案不足以支持可问责性。工程师需要知道诊断依据了哪些证据、考虑了哪些替代方案、存在哪些矛盾以及系统是否保留了不确定性。

Method: 构建JustDiag诊断推理引擎，显式维护过程状态，包括证据、发现、竞争假设、冲突和下一步检查。采用双层评估协议，分别对最终答案质量和过程质量进行评分。

Result: 在66个真实事件上，JustDiag相比无诊断推理的对照组取得了更高的结果质量和过程质量评分，但终端完成率略低，原因是其更校准的非终结判断（即更谨慎地保留不确定性）。

Conclusion: 可问责的RCA不仅需要流畅的最终答案，还需要显式的诊断推理制品和面向过程质量的评估方法。

Abstract: Large language models can produce fluent root cause analyses, but fluent final answers alone are insufficient evidence for accountability in high-stakes operations. In real incident response, engineers need to know what evidence supported a diagnosis, which alternatives were considered, where contradictions remained, and whether the system resolved the case or preserved uncertainty. We address this gap with JustDiag, a diagnostic justification engine for RCA that maintains an explicit process state over evidence, findings, competing hypotheses, conflicts, and next checks. We evaluated the system on 66 real-world incidents using a two-layer protocol that separately scores final-answer quality and process quality. Relative to a matched control without diagnostic justification, JustDiag achieved stronger outcome and process scores, while accepting slightly lower terminal completion due to more calibrated non-closure. These results suggest that accountable RCA requires explicit diagnostic justification artifacts and process-aware evaluation, not only fluent final answers.

</details>


### [132] [OpenRath: Session-Centered Runtime State for Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19409)
*Fukang Wen,Zhijie Wang,Ruilin Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OpenRath提出了一种以Session为核心一等运行时抽象的多智能体、多会话编程模型，使运行时状态可分支、可检查、可重放、可组合，从而解决智能体系统状态碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统的运行时状态高度碎片化：对话记录、工具影响、记忆事件、工作区布局、分支来源和重放证据被分别记录，难以检查或复现。

Method: 借鉴PyTorch的编程模型（类比核心一等运行时抽象而非张量计算），核心抽象是Session——在智能体和工作流之间传递的运行时值。Session具有可分支、可检查、可重放、可感知后端、可组合等特性，记录对话片段、沙盒位置、谱系元数据、令牌使用、待处理工作和工具证据，同时定义记忆交互进入运行时记录的位置。此外定义了Sandbox、Tool、Agent、Memory、Workflow、Selector等组件，其中Selector将控制流转化为运行时路由决策。

Result: 提出了编程模型、架构、审计里程碑和证据协议。声明限于可控运行时属性，定量比较、实时提供商质量、可选后端可用性和记忆质量留待后续评估。

Conclusion: 核心论点是Session为智能体系统提供了一等运行时值，支持可审计的组合。

Abstract: Modern agent systems often suffer from fragmented runtime state: transcripts, tool effects, memory events, workspace placement, branch provenance, and replay evidence are recorded separately and become difficult to inspect or reproduce. OpenRath addresses this issue with a PyTorch-like programming model for multi-agent, multi-session systems. The analogy concerns the role of a central first-class runtime abstraction, not tensor computation. Its core abstraction is Session, the runtime value passed between agents and workflows. A Session is branchable, inspectable, replayable, backend-aware, and composable. It records conversation chunks, sandbox placement, lineage metadata, token usage, pending work, and tool evidence, while defining where memory interactions enter the runtime record. Since this state is carried by the same value used in program execution, fork, merge, and replay become explicit runtime operations rather than states reconstructed from external traces. OpenRath further defines Sandbox, Tool, Agent, Memory, Workflow, and Selector, with Selector turning control flow into runtime-routed decisions. This report presents the programming model, architecture, audited milestones, and evidence protocol. Its claims are limited to controlled runtime properties, while broad quantitative comparisons, live-provider quality, optional-backend availability, and memory quality are left for follow-on evaluation. The central thesis is that Session provides agent systems with a first-class runtime value for auditable composition.

</details>


### [133] [FAPO: Fully Autonomous Prompt Optimization of Multi-Step LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19605)
*Paul Kassianik,Baturay Saglam,Huaibo Zhao,Blaine Nelson,Supriti Vijay,Aman Priyanshu,Amin Karbasi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FAPO提出了一种全自动的LLM流水线优化框架，能够在标准化代码库中通过评估、中间步骤检查、故障诊断、范围化修改和变体验证来优化多步骤LLM流水线，同时支持先尝试提示优化、再在必要时进行结构变更。在18个模型-基准比较中，FAPO在15个上优于基线GEPA，平均提升+14.1pp，在结构变更场景中平均提升+33.8pp，并在安全任务上也有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步骤LLM流水线中，检索、推理和格式化步骤之间存在交互影响，仅靠提示优化无法定位流水线中的瓶颈环节，因此需要一种能够自动诊断并优化整个流水线（包括结构）的方法。

Method: FAPO框架在标准化代码库中运行，通过循环执行评估→检查中间步骤→诊断故障→提出范围化修改→验证变体的流程来优化流水线。优先尝试提示编辑，当提示优化不足以解决问题且归因分析识别出结构性瓶颈时，才允许在限定范围内修改链式结构。

Result: 在6个基准和3个任务模型上，FAPO在18个模型-基准比较中15个优于GEPA，其中11个的均值±试验标准差范围不重叠，平均增益+14.1pp。在HoVer和IFBench的6个结构变更比较中全部胜出，平均增益+33.8pp。在安全任务CTIBench-RCM上，提示优化仅提升GPT-5 +4.0pp、Foundation-Sec-8B-Instruct +7.1pp、Foundation-Sec-8B-Reasoning +2.0pp。

Conclusion: FAPO是一种面向通用任务和安全任务的最先进流水线优化技术，证明了先提示优化再结构变更的层次化策略能有效提升多步骤LLM流水线性能。

Abstract: Multi-step LLM pipelines fail through interactions among retrieval, reasoning, and formatting steps, so prompt-only optimization can miss bottlenecks in the chain. We present FAPO (Fully Autonomous Prompt Optimization), a framework that lets Claude Code optimize an LLM pipeline inside a standardized codebase. FAPO evaluates a pipeline, inspects intermediate steps, diagnoses failures, proposes scoped changes, and validates variants repeatedly to optimize against a score function. It first tries prompt edits and, only when prompt optimization appears insufficient, changes chain structure within the permitted scope when attribution identifies a structural bottleneck. Across six benchmarks and three task models, FAPO beats the baseline GEPA in 15 of 18 model-benchmark comparisons. In 11 model-benchmark comparisons, FAPO wins with non-overlapping mean $\pm$ trial-standard-deviation ranges, and the mean FAPO-GEPA gain is +14.1 pp. In the six HoVer and IFBench comparisons where prompt-first search escalated to structural changes, FAPO wins all six with a mean gain of +33.8 pp. FAPO also improves performance on security tasks: on CTIBench-RCM, a security CVE-to-CWE task, prompt-only FAPO lifts test accuracy by +4.0 pp on GPT-5, +7.1 pp on Foundation-Sec-8B-Instruct, and +2.0 pp on Foundation-Sec-8B-Reasoning. These results position FAPO as a state-of-the-art pipeline optimization technique for both general-purpose and security-focused tasks.

</details>


### [134] [StaminaBench: Stress-Testing Coding Agents over 100 Interaction Turns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19613)
*Vlad Sobal,Shuo Yang,Yuting Zhang,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: StaminaBench是一个评估编码智能体能连续处理多少轮变更请求的基准测试。实验发现所有模型在5-6轮内失败，但允许基于测试反馈重试可将通过轮次提升最多12倍，且好的agent框架对性能至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试大多关注任务解决率这一静态指标，但实际"氛围编码"场景中，会话通常包含数十甚至数百轮连续的变更请求。作者希望弥补这一差距，评估编码智能体在长时间连续交互中的"耐力"。

Method: 构建REST API服务器作为任务基础，支持可调节数量的程序化生成变更请求（实验中为100轮）。测试完全通过程序化生成（无LLM参与），变更序列来自硬编码或LLM驱动的采样器，均受限于结构化动作空间。智能体与服务器在隔离环境中通过HTTP通信，实现黑盒、语言无关的测试。评估6种agent框架配7种开源LLM，共20个场景各100轮。

Result: (1) 所有模型在5-6轮内失败，证实无充分测试的氛围编码产生大量bug；(2) 将测试反馈传回agent并允许重试可将通过轮数提升最多12倍；(3) 好的框架对性能至关重要：强模型在不同框架间表现差距可达6倍，弱模型在任何框架下均失败。

Conclusion: 现有编码智能体的多轮连续交互能力严重不足，需要更好的测试反馈机制和agent框架设计。作者开源了基准测试代码和生成的任务，以推动相关研究。

Abstract: We introduce StaminaBench, a benchmark that measures the stamina of coding agents: how many consecutive interaction turns (change requests) they can handle before failing. Unlike the prevailing fraction-of-tasks-solved metric, this matches real vibe-coding where sessions run dozens or hundreds of turns. In StaminaBench, agents implement a REST API server and modify it across a tunable number of procedurally generated follow-up change requests - 100 in our experiments, resulting in codebases of up to 6,000 lines. Tests are generated fully programmatically without LLM involvement, ensuring reproducibility and reliability; change sequences are drawn from either a hardcoded or LLM-driven sampler, both constrained to a structured action space to ensure changes are valid. The agent and the server run in an isolated environment and communicate with the benchmark through HTTP, making testing fully black-box and language-agnostic. We evaluate six agent harnesses paired with seven open-source LLMs across 20 scenarios of 100 turns each and find that: (1) all the tested models fail within 5-6 turns, confirming that vibe-coding-style programming without thorough testing produces bugs; (2) passing test feedback back to the agent and allowing it to retry improves passed turn count by up to 12x; and (3) a good harness is required for strong performance: stronger models exhibit up to a 6x gap between their best and worst harness, while weaker models fail with any harness. We release the benchmark and the generated tasks to enable further research into multi-turn coding agent behavior. Benchmark code and data: github.com/amazon-science/StaminaBench.

</details>


### [135] [Before the Pull Request: Mining Multi-Agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19616)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出grite这一基于git的去中心化协调基础设施，解决多智能体编码协作中的冲突和重复工作问题，通过append-only签名事件日志将协调过程透明化，实验表明重复工作从78%降至0%，吞吐量提升3倍以上，并可从日志中自动恢复多种故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主编码智能体虽能大量生成PR，但接受率低，存在协调与信任鸿沟，而PR层面的遥测无法解释这一现象。本文认为缺失的信号在于PR之前的并发智能体如何声明、划分和碰撞共享工作的过程。

Method: 设计并实现了grite——一个去中心化的协调基础设施，无需中央服务器，将append-only签名事件日志直接存储在git内部，记录智能体之间的完整协调过程。

Result: (i) 共享协调基板将重复/冲突工作从78%降至0%，有用吞吐量提升3倍以上；(ii) 所有智能体的日志副本收敛到相同状态，无写入静默丢失，而基于文件的追踪器会丢失并发写入；(iii) 日志可挖掘，能自动恢复冲突编辑、锁饥饿、冗余发现、竞相关闭等故障模式，其中多种在PR历史中不可见。

Conclusion: 论文证明了任务前协调过程的可观测性对提升多智能体协作效率至关重要，grite通过将协调记录嵌入git提供了可行的解决方案，并开源了数据集、测试框架和挖掘工具包。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents now open millions of pull requests, yet large-scale studies find their PRs are produced faster but accepted less often - a coordination and trust gap that pull-request-level telemetry cannot explain. We argue the missing signal lives before the PR, in how concurrent agents claim, divide, and collide over shared work. We study this process through grite, our open-source coordination substrate that needs no central server and stores its records inside git itself, so its append-only, signed event log captures the coordination process directly. We show that (i) this shared substrate reduces duplicate and conflicting work at bounded overhead - the share of work that merely re-does a teammate's task falls from 78% to 0% while useful throughput more than triples; (ii) every agent's copy of the log converges to the same state with no write silently dropped, where a file-based tracker loses concurrent writes; and (iii) the log is a mineable artefact from which concrete failure modes - conflicting edits, lock starvation, redundant rediscovery, race-to-close - are automatically recoverable with provenance, several invisible in pull-request history. We release the dataset, harness, and mining toolkit.

</details>


### [136] [Prompt Quality and Pull Request Outcomes: A Stage-Based Empirical Study of LLM-Assisted Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19644)
*Richard Sserunjogi,Daniel Ogenrwot,John Businge*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析了265个开发者-ChatGPT交互案例，从上下文、具体性和验证三个维度研究提示结构对拉取请求中代码生成、采纳和集成深度的影响，发现不同维度在不同工作流阶段发挥差异化作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注对话有用性、生产力或粗粒度的采纳指标，对提示结构如何影响协作式拉取请求的下游结果缺乏理解。

Method: 收集265个人工验证的开发者-ChatGPT交互（来自开源PR中自我报告的ChatGPT使用），从上下文、具体性和验证三个维度操作化提示结构；评估LLM辅助标注与人类判断的一致性，并分析提示结构对代码生成、采纳和集成深度的影响。

Result: 具体性维度与人类判断的一致性最稳定；上下文维度的LLM评分系统性偏低；验证维度难以一致评估。具体性和上下文与可操作的代码生成最相关；验证是代码采纳的主要预测因子；上下文与集成深度最相关。

Conclusion: 提示特征对AI辅助软件工程工作流具有显著的、阶段依赖的影响，通过上下文基础、任务具体性和可评估性线索影响下游采纳和集成行为。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered tools such as ChatGPT are increasingly used in collaborative software engineering workflows, yet little is known about how prompt structure influences downstream pull request (PR) outcomes. Prior studies primarily examine conversational helpfulness, productivity, or coarse-grained adoption metrics, leaving the role of prompt structure in collaborative integration behavior insufficiently understood. We analyze 265 manually validated developer-ChatGPT interactions derived from self-admitted ChatGPT usage in open-source pull requests.
