Building on prior research on developer-facing artifacts and prompt engineering, we operationalize prompt structure using three dimensions: Context, Specificity, and Verification. We first evaluate whether LLM-assisted annotation can reliably reproduce human judgments of prompt structure, finding substantial variation across dimensions and workflow contexts. Specificity shows the most stable agreement with human judgments; Context is systematically under-scored by the LLM; and Verification remains difficult to assess consistently, motivating a hybrid human-LLM annotation strategy. Using this validated framework, we then examine how prompt structure influences actionable code generation, code adoption, and integration depth across AI-assisted PR workflows. Specificity and Context are most strongly associated with actionable code generation; Verification emerges as the primary predictor of code adoption; and integration depth is most strongly associated with Context. Overall, our findings show that prompt characteristics exert distinct, stage-dependent effects across AI-assisted software engineering workflows, influencing downstream adoption and integration through contextual grounding, task specificity, and evaluability cues.

</details>


### [137] [Library-Aware Doubles and Iterative Repair for Large Language Model-Generated Unit Tests in OpenSIL Firmware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19725)
*Ma Toan Bach,Yuchi Zheng,Haingo Razafindranto,Tanvir Alam,Aric Leather,Ranveer Sandhu,Jitesh Arora*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM多智能体管线的自动化单元测试生成工作流，用于openSIL固件代码库。该工作流结合测试脚手架生成、桩/模拟/伪造代码创建以及基于构建日志和行覆盖率反馈的迭代编译-修复循环。在76个函数中成功为73个生成了可编译的UT，行覆盖率从73.9%（无引导）提升至98.8%（覆盖率引导）和94.7%（结合向量数据库检索）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低级C固件中的单元测试因严格构建约束（缺失头文件、未解析符号、依赖不匹配）导致编译和链接频繁失败，手动编写UT成本高昂且脆弱。

Method: 提出一个LLM引导的多智能体管线工作流，包括：(1) 自动化生成测试脚手架；(2) 库感知的桩/模拟/伪造代码创建或复用；(3) 基于构建日志和行覆盖率反馈的迭代编译-分发修复循环。在有无行覆盖率引导和检索增强两种配置下进行评估。

Result: 76个函数中73个生成可编译UT；无引导配置下平均行覆盖率73.9%；48个函数子集上，覆盖率引导下达98.8%，结合向量数据库检索下达94.7%。

Conclusion: 自动化生成-修复管线能显著提升受限固件环境下UT创建效率和覆盖率，减少手动调试工作量。

Abstract: Validating changes in low-level C firmware is expensive because unit tests (UTs) are fragile under strict build constraints, where missing headers, unresolved symbols, and dependency mismatches frequently prevent compilation and linking. This study introduces an automated UT authoring workflow for the Open-Source Silicon Initialization Library (openSIL) firmware codebase maintained by Advanced Micro Devices (AMD) that reduces manual effort through a large language model (LLM) guided multi-agent pipeline. The workflow combines automated generation of test scaffolds, library-aware creation or reuse of stubs, mocks, and fakes, and an iterative compile-dispatch repair loop driven by build logs and line-coverage feedback. We evaluate the approach using compilation success, repair iterations, dispatch success, and line coverage, with time, cost, and token usage as secondary measures. Across 76 functions under test, the workflow generated compilable UTs for 73 functions. In a configuration without line coverage guidance or retrieval augmentation, mean line coverage reached 73.9%. On a 48-function subset evaluated under both configurations, mean line coverage reached 98.8% with line-coverage guidance alone and reached 94.7% when combined with vector-database retrieval. Results show that automated generation-and-repair pipelines can substantially improve UT creation efficiency and coverage for constrained firmware environments while reducing manual debugging effort.

</details>


### [138] [Agentic Electronic Design Automation: A Handoff Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19795)
*Jiawei Liu,Peiyi Han,Yuntao Lu,Su Zheng,Fengyu Yan,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文以**交接有效性（handoff validity）**为组织原则，系统综述了82个EDA智能体系统，将其分为Stage-Bound、Flow-Bound、Organization-Bound三类边界，并提出五层EDA智能体通信协议（EACP），旨在为可信赖的智能体EDA建立通用词汇和研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDA流程本质上是多阶段且频繁交接的，设计工件、脚本和工程决策跨工具、会话和组织边界传递，每次传递携带的显式和隐式需求可能未被阶段内检查完全捕获。LLM智能体直接调用EDA工具、嵌入检索知识、跨阶段传递状态，其输出影响下游工程决策，因此必须保证交接满足消费者接受条件，即实现**交接有效性**。

Method: 综述82个系统，按交接边界分为三类：Stage-Bound（单阶段/有界验证任务内建立有效性）、Flow-Bound（跨工具、调用和会话保持工作流状态一致性）、Organization-Bound（跨知识和权限边界维护来源、溯源、范围与可采信性）。对每类分析交接契约、交接对象、协调机制和开放问题，并由此提出五层EDA智能体通信协议（EACP）：智能体发现、智能体消息、工具调用、工作流编排、安全与IP协议。

Result: 分类总结了82个系统在三种边界下的交接有效性保障机制，并提出了五层EACP协议框架作为统一通信规范。

Conclusion: 交接有效性是组织可信赖智能体EDA的核心原则；现有方法按边界各有侧重但缺乏统一协议，EACP为跨边界的智能体协作提供了系统化的解决方案和研究方向。

Abstract: Electronic design automation (EDA) is inherently multi-stage and handoff-heavy. Design artifacts, flow scripts, and engineering decisions cross tool, session, and organizational boundaries before final implementation, signoff, or release. Each transfer carries explicit and implicit requirements that may not be fully captured by stage-local checks. LLM-based agents now invoke EDA tools directly, embed retrieved knowledge in executable scripts, and hand off state across sessions and stages. Once their outputs condition downstream engineering decisions, the transferred object must satisfy a handoff contract and meet the assumptions of its next consumer. This survey introduces handoff validity as its organizing principle. A handoff is valid when the transferred object satisfies the consumer's acceptance conditions and carries sufficient context, evidence, and provenance for downstream use. We review 82 systems and classify them into three boundary classes. Stage-Bound systems establish validity within a single EDA stage or bounded verification task. Flow-Bound systems preserve coherent workflow state across tools, invocations, and sessions. Organization-Bound systems maintain source grounding, provenance, scope, and admissibility across knowledge and authority boundaries. For each class, we analyze handoff contracts, handoff objects, coordination mechanisms, and open questions. These analyses motivate a five-layer EDA agent communication protocol (EACP), covering the agent discovery, agent message, tool invocation, workflow orchestration, and security and IP protocols. We aim to provide a common vocabulary and research agenda for trustworthy agentic EDA.

</details>


### [139] [The Hidden Environmental Cost of Poor Coding Practices in TensorFlow and Keras Applications: A Study on Resource Leaks and Carbon Emissions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19799)
*Bashar Abdallah,Gustavo Santos,Rola Al Bataineh,Alain Abran,Mohammad Hamdaqa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了两种常见资源泄漏异味（IMR和UTR）在TensorFlow/Keras中对能耗和CO2排放影响的初步实证研究。实验表明，IMR和UTR分别使电力消耗增加约32%和46%，碳排放相应增加，且差异具有统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管资源泄漏可能隐藏效率问题并增加能耗和碳排放，但缺乏量化其环境影响的实证证据。

Method: 针对IMR和UTR两种资源泄漏异味，在TensorFlow和Keras工作负载中执行受控实验，将相同训练任务与无异味基线进行对比，测量电耗和碳排放差异，并进行配对统计检验。

Result: IMR和UTR分别导致电耗增加约32%和46%，CO2排放相应增加，配对统计检验表明差异系统且显著。

Conclusion: 资源泄漏异味对ML能源效率和环境可持续性构成可衡量的风险，需将资源生命周期管理和能效考量纳入ML开发实践。

Abstract: Efficiency and sustainability are critical considerations in the development and deployment of machine learning (ML) applications. Among the factors influencing sustainability, resource leaks in ML code can introduce hidden inefficiencies that elevate energy consumption and CO2 emissions. Despite this, empirical evidence quantifying their environmental impact remains limited. This emerging results paper presents an initial empirical investigation of two common resource-leak smells, namely Improper Model Reuse (IMR) and Unreleased Tensor References (UTR), and their impact on energy consumption and CO2 emissions in TensorFlow and Keras workloads. Controlled experiments were conducted for each smell by executing identical training tasks while comparing against a smell-free baseline. Our preliminary results show that both smells consistently increase estimated electricity usage and carbon emissions. IMR and UTR increased electricity consumption by approximately 32% and 46%, respectively, with proportional increases in CO2 emissions. Paired statistical tests indicate that these differences are systematic and statistically significant, providing initial empirical evidence that resource-leak smells may degrade ML energy efficiency and environmental sustainability. These findings suggest that resource-leak smells pose measurable risks to both software quality and sustainability, emphasizing the importance of integrating resource-lifecycle management and energy-efficiency considerations into ML development.

</details>


### [140] [CoRaCommit: A VS Code Extension for Commit Message Generation with Exemplar Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19814)
*Chaoran Cai,Bo Xiong,Chong Wang,Lulu He,Peng Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CoRaCommit是一个VS Code扩展，通过检索相似提交示例作为提示上下文、并行调用多个大语言模型生成候选消息、并基于用户反馈动态推荐LLM，有效提升了提交消息生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VS Code扩展直接调用LLM生成提交消息，缺乏相似提交示例作为参考，且不支持用户反馈驱动的LLM推荐，导致生成质量不稳定、表达方式不统一。

Method: 提出CoRaCommit扩展，包含三个核心组件：1) 检索相似提交示例作为提示上下文；2) 并行调用多个LLM生成候选提交消息并进行比较；3) 基于用户反馈动态推荐最优LLM。

Result: 在ApacheCM数据集上的945个提交上，CoRaCommit在BLEU、CIDEr、METEOR和ROUGE-L指标上均优于现有VS Code扩展。

Conclusion: 检索增强的上下文能够有效提升提交消息生成质量，多LLM并行与用户反馈机制进一步增强了扩展的实用性。

Abstract: Commit messages are essential textual artifacts that describe the intent behind code changes, and play a critical role in version control, code review, and historical tracking. However, in practice, commit messages are primarily authored manually, which is time-consuming and often results in inconsistent quality and non-uniform expression. Existing VS Code extensions for commit message generation typically directly invoke large language models based on the code diff, without leveraging similar commit exemplars as references, and rarely support user feedback-driven LLM recommendation. To address these limitations, this paper presents CoRaCommit, a VS Code extension that enhances commit message generation by retrieving similar commit exemplars as prompt context, invoking multiple LLMs in parallel for candidate commit message comparison, and dynamically recommending LLMs based on user feedback. Experimental results on 945 commits from the ApacheCM dataset show that CoRaCommit outperforms existing VS Code extensions across BLEU, CIDEr, METEOR, and ROUGE-L metrics, demonstrating the effectiveness of retrieval-augmented context for commit message generation.

</details>


### [141] [JAMER: Project-Level Code Framework Dataset and Benchmark on Professional Game Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19830)
*Jianwen Sun,Chuanhao Li,Zizhen Li,Yukang Feng,Fanrui Zhang,Yifei Huang,Yu Dai,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了JamSet和JamBench，首个基于专业游戏引擎（Godot）的项目级游戏代码框架数据集和基准，包含8,133个经过验证的开源游戏项目，用于评估AI在大型游戏工程项目上的代码生成和补全能力。评估9个前沿模型发现，随着项目规模扩大存在能力悬崖，运行时通过率从80.4%降至5.7%，代码智能体虽提升编译率但无法改善运行时行为质量，瓶颈在于架构设计而非语法正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI驱动游戏开发在资源生成、玩法设计和网页游戏编码方面取得进展，但在专业游戏引擎上的项目级代码工程仍未被探索，原因在于缺乏大规模数据集和确定性评估方法。

Method: 利用Game Jam竞赛中产生的开源项目，基于Godot引擎的文本化格式和无头执行模式，设计了从文件完整性到运行时行为收集的确定性验证流水线，从超过240,000个仓库中蒸馏出8,133个验证项目（300个手工验证为JamBench，其余为JamSet），定义了主题驱动生成和代码补全任务，通过编译通过率、结构完整性得分和行为对齐得分进行评估。

Result: 评估9个前沿模型显示项目规模增大时存在能力悬崖：运行时通过率从小项目的80.4%骤降至大项目的5.7%。代码智能体能提升编译率但无法改善运行时行为质量。实验验证了JamSet作为训练数据的有效性。

Conclusion: 项目级游戏工程代码生成的瓶颈在于架构设计而非语法正确性，JamSet和JamBench为该领域提供了标准化评估基准和训练数据。

Abstract: Current AI-driven game development has made substantial progress in asset generation, gameplay design, and web-based game coding, yet project-level code engineering on professional game engines remains largely unexplored due to the absence of large-scale datasets and deterministic evaluation methods. We present JamSet and JamBench, the first project-level game code framework dataset and benchmark built on a professional game engine. Our key insight is that Game Jam competitions, community events where developers build complete games under tight time constraints, yield thousands of open-source projects suitable for this purpose. Building on the Godot engine's text-based format and headless execution mode, we design a deterministic verification pipeline from file integrity to runtime behavior collection, distilling 8,133 verified projects from over 240,000 repositories. Of these, 300 manually verified projects form JamBench; the rest constitute JamSet. JamBench defines theme-driven generation and code completion tasks, evaluated through a pipeline combining compilation pass rates, Structural Completeness Score (SCS), and Behavioral Alignment Score (BAS). Evaluation of 9 frontier models reveals a capability cliff as project scale increases, with runtime pass rates dropping from 80.4% on small projects to 5.7% on large ones (Task2a). Code Agents improve compilation rates yet yield no gains in runtime behavioral quality, indicating that the bottleneck lies in architectural design rather than syntactic correctness. Experiments validate JamSet as effective training data. All data and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [142] [Repository-Level Solidity Code Generation with Large Language Models: From Prompting to Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19988)
*Shi Chen,Rongcun Wang,Yuan Tian,Xiaoyuan Xie,Wei Song,Rubing Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含5,470个仓库级Solidity智能合约的基准测试SolityBench，并提出了面向Solidity领域约束的语义评估指标SolityScore。实验评估了多种代码LLM（Qwen2.5-Coder、DeepSeek-Coder、CodeLlama）在不同提示策略下的表现，发现通用模型在仓库级Solidity生成中存在系统性结构缺陷；在无参数方法中，检索增强生成效果最佳，而上下文学习在超过两个示例后因上下文饱和而性能下降；监督微调通过将Solidity特定约束内化到模型参数中取得了最大改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准和指标不足以评估仓库级Solidity智能合约生成，而该领域对语言规范、安全性和软件工程约束要求严格，需要专门的基准和评估方法。

Method: 构建SolityBench基准（5,470个仓库级Solidity合约-自然语言描述对），提出SolityScore语义评估指标（强调安全修饰符、合约声明、Solidity特定关键词）。使用Qwen2.5-Coder、DeepSeek-Coder、CodeLlama等模型在零样本提示、思维链推理、上下文学习、检索增强生成和监督微调等多种策略下进行系统评估。

Result: 通用模型在仓库级Solidity生成中存在系统性结构缺陷；检索增强生成在无参数方法中表现最佳；上下文学习超过两个示例后因上下文饱和而性能下降；监督微调取得最大改进。

Conclusion: 本文为仓库级Solidity代码生成提供了全面基准，表明高质量领域数据结合监督微调是提高LLM生成智能合约可靠性的最有效策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in general-purpose code generation, but their effectiveness in specialized software domains remains underexplored. Solidity smart contracts represent a high-stakes domain where generated code must satisfy strict language-level, security, and software-engineering constraints. Existing benchmarks and metrics remain insufficient for repository-level Solidity generation, where models must synthesize complete contracts from natural language requirements. To address this gap, we introduce SolidityBench, a benchmark of 5,470 repository-level Solidity smart contracts paired with natural language descriptions. We also propose SolidityScore, a Solidity-aware semantic metric that emphasizes domain-critical constructs such as security modifiers, contract declarations, and Solidity-specific keywords. Using this benchmark, we evaluate representative code LLMs, including Qwen2.5-Coder, DeepSeek-Coder, and CodeLlama, across zero-shot prompting, Chain-of-Thought reasoning, in-context learning, retrieval-augmented generation, and supervised fine-tuning. The results show that general-purpose models exhibit systematic structural deficiencies in repository-level Solidity generation. Among non-parametric methods, retrieval-augmented generation performs best, while in-context learning degrades beyond two examples due to context saturation. Supervised fine-tuning achieves the largest improvement by internalizing Solidity-specific constraints into model parameters. Overall, our study provides a comprehensive benchmark for repository-level Solidity code generation and shows that high-quality domain data combined with supervised fine-tuning is the most effective strategy for improving the reliability of LLM-generated smart contracts.

</details>


### [143] [Beyond Static Endpoints: Tool Programs as an Interface for Flexible Agentic Web Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19992)
*Mugeng Liu,Shuoqi Li,Yixuan Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ToolPro将LLM代理的工具调用意图表示为可执行工具程序，通过约束引导构建、效果感知重放和profile驱动策略，在MCP风格服务上实现高达53.4%的延迟降低和96.1%的流量减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理调用的Web服务多为静态端点，难以表达包含循环、条件分支、连接和重试的长周期工作流，导致多步骤服务交互效率低下。

Method: ToolPro将代理的工具意图编码为可执行工具程序，包含三个核心组件：约束引导的程序构建（constraint-guided program construction）、效果感知重放（effect-aware replay）确保状态修改操作恰好执行一次，以及profile驱动策略（profile-driven policy）动态决定何时程序执行优于逐步调用。系统基于MCP风格服务并通过WebAssembly沙箱实现。

Result: ToolPro在多样化真实工作流上实现端到端延迟降低最高53.4%，客户端流量减少最高96.1%，在网络延迟更高和工作流更复杂的情况下收益更大。

Conclusion: ToolPro通过将工具调用意图程序化表示，有效解决了LLM代理在多步服务交互场景下的效率问题，尤其适用于高延迟网络和复杂工作流环境。

Abstract: In the agentic web era, LLM-based agents increasingly invoke web services as tools, yet most interfaces remain \emph{static endpoints} that poorly express long-horizon workflows with loops, conditionals, joins, and retries. We present ToolPro, which represents an agent's tool intent as an \emph{executable tool program} that compactly encodes multi-step service interactions with explicit effect types. ToolPro combines constraint-guided program construction, effect-aware replay for exactly-once state-modifying calls, and a profile-driven policy that decides when program execution outperforms stepwise calling. We instantiate ToolPro over MCP-style services with WebAssembly sandboxing and evaluate it on diverse workflows of real-world applications. ToolPro reduces end-to-end latency by up to 53.4\% and client-side traffic by up to 96.1\%, with larger gains under higher network latency and workflow complexity.

</details>


### [144] [When Lower Privileges Suffice: Investigating Over-Privileged Tool Selection in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20023)
*Kaiyue Yang,Yuyan Bu,Jingwei Yi,Yuchi Wang,Biyu Zhou,Juntao Dai,Songlin Hu,Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出ToolPrivBench，研究LLM代理在存在足够低权限替代工具时仍选择高权限工具的“过权限工具选择”问题。实验发现主流LLM代理普遍存在此问题且被瞬态失败放大，通用安全对齐无法可靠迁移，而权限感知的后训练防御能有效减少不必要的高权限工具使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具选择研究仅关注安全无关的元数据偏好，忽略了权限敏感的选择——即代理在有足够低权限替代工具时仍选择或升级到高权限工具，这带来安全风险。

Method: 构建ToolPrivBench评估框架，涵盖8个领域和5种重复风险模式，测量代理的初始工具选择以及瞬态故障后的升级行为，并提出权限感知的后训练防御方法。

Result: 主流LLM代理普遍存在过权限工具选择问题，瞬态失败会进一步放大该问题；通用安全对齐无法可靠迁移到最小权限工具选择，提示级控制在瞬态失败下仅提供有限缓解。

Conclusion: 权限感知的后训练防御能显著减少不必要的高权限工具使用，同时保持代理的通用能力。

Abstract: As LLM agents increasingly select tools autonomously, their choices among tools with different privileges become safety-relevant. However, prior tool-selection studies focus on safety-agnostic metadata preferences, leaving privilege-sensitive choices underexplored. To address this gap, we study over-privileged tool selection, in which an agent selects or escalates to a higher-privilege tool despite a sufficient lower-privilege alternative. We introduce ToolPrivBench to evaluate whether agents choose higher-privilege tools despite sufficient lower-privilege alternatives, measuring both initial selection and escalation after transient tool failures. Across eight domains and five recurring risk patterns, we find that over-privileged tool selection is common among mainstream LLM agents and is further amplified by transient failures. We further find that general safety alignment does not reliably transfer to least-privilege tool choice, while prompt-level controls provide only limited mitigation under transient failures. We therefore introduce a privilege-aware post-training defense that teaches agents to prefer sufficient lower-privilege tools and escalate only when necessary. Our mitigation experiments show that this defense substantially reduces unnecessary high-privilege tool use while preserving general capabilities.

</details>


### [145] [The Correctness Illusion in LLM-Generated GPU Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20128)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对LLM生成GPU内核的基准测试（KernelBench、TritonBench、GEAK）使用固定形状、小样本的allclose式检查来评估正确性，但这种方法存在局限。本文构建了24个Triton和CPU替代内核（15个正确对照+9个LLM风格错误变体），采用基于op-schema感知的种子化模糊测试和高精度fp64 CPU参考进行重新评估。该种子化Oracle成功识别全部9个错误内核并通过全部15个正确对照，零精度损失。扩展到26个操作后，在5种GPU（RTX 3060, A10, L40S, A100 SXM4, H100 NVL）上结果一致：10/10幻觉被捕获，16/16对照通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成GPU内核基准测试采用固定形状、小样本的allclose式检查来判定正确性，该方法可能无法有效检测出LLM常见的转录错误（如形状、数据类型或精度问题），导致错误内核被误判为正确。本文旨在实证检验现有Oracle的有效性，并提出更可靠的评估方法。

Method: 构建受控语料库：24个Triton和CPU替代内核（15正确+9含LLM风格转录错误变体）；采用op-schema感知的种子化模糊测试，配合高精度fp64 CPU参考和每(op, dtype)绝对容差进行重新评估；将语料扩展至26个操作（加入flash-attention对），在5种GPU上重复实验。

Result: 种子化Oracle在24内核语料上：捕获9/9错误内核，通过15/15正确对照，零精度损失；在26操作扩展语料及5种GPU上：捕获10/10幻觉，通过16/16对照，结果完全一致。

Conclusion: 现有固定形状allclose式基准测试存在盲区，无法识别LLM风格的转录错误；提出的op-schema感知种子化模糊测试方法能可靠检测这些错误，且结果在多种GPU上可复现。该结果聚焦于LLM风格转录错误的检测能力，而非特定LLM的bug率。

Abstract: Benchmarks for LLM-generated GPU kernels (KernelBench, TritonBench, GEAK) score correctness through fixed-shape, small-sample allclose-style checks. The number of inputs varies between benchmarks. The shape, dtype, and tolerance are fixed for each kernel. We test that oracle empirically. We construct a controlled corpus of 24 Triton and CPU stand-in kernels (15 correct controls and 9 LLM-style buggy variants seeded with documented transcription errors) and re-evaluate it under op-schema-aware seeded fuzzing with a high-precision (fp64) CPU reference and per-(op, dtype) absolute tolerances. The seeded oracle flags 9 of 9 buggy kernels and passes 15 of 15 correct controls, at zero precision cost on controls. We extend the corpus to 26 ops (adding a flash-attention pair) and re-run the same protocol on five GPU classes (RTX 3060, A10, L40S, A100 SXM4, H100 NVL). The verdicts are identical across all five GPUs: 10 of 10 illusions caught and 16 of 16 controls clean. The corpus result is about LLM-style transcription bugs that the allclose-on-one-shape oracle certifies as correct, not about the bug rate of any specific deployed LLM. Every flagged failure replays byte-for-byte from a stored seed.

</details>


### [146] [Learning Critical Testing Literacy Through Puzzles: an Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20129)
*Niels Doorn,Bart Th. Knaack,Tanja E. J. Vos,Beatriz Marín*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了通过谜题学习批判性测试素养（CTL）的十三个工作坊的经验与观察，发现谜题本身并非干预手段，完整的“解决-汇报-反思”序列才是关键；参与者通过实验和反思构建意义，并开发了开源分析工具以支持工作坊定制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件测试重要但教学困难；现有方法缺乏有效的教学框架，尤其缺乏基于批判性测试认知模型的谜题式学习活动。

Method: 基于P4TEST教学框架，面向学生、测试员、教师和小学生开展了十三个工作坊；其中十一个采用半结构化方式（变化谜题、材料和时长），另外两个加入工作手册和出声思维会话以收集更深入的学习体验数据。

Result: 参与者普遍认为自己是在“做实验”；学生倾向于收敛答案，专业人士则持续探索；情绪通过行为可见但难以通过书面反思表露；出声思维揭示即时推理过程，书面反思则引发更多元认知反思；核心主题为“意义建构/行动中反思”。

Conclusion: 谜题本身不是真正的教学干预，完整的“解决-汇报-反思”序列才是。未来工作应更刻意地设计该序列；此外开发了开源Web应用及内置分析工具以支持工作坊定制。

Abstract: In this paper, we report our experiences and takeaways from workshops using puzzles to learn CTL.
  Background: Software testing is important yet difficult to teach. We introduced a BoK of puzzle-based learning activities to teach CTL, based on a model of critical tester's cognition, leading to the pedagogical framework P4TEST. We conducted thirteen workshops with students, testers, teachers, and primary school pupils to assess puzzle-based teaching of critical testing literacy.
  Experience: Across eleven workshops, we used a semi-structured approach, varying puzzles, materials, and timing. In two additional workshops, we introduced workbooks and think-aloud sessions to gather more data on the learning experience.
  Observations: Participants consistently perceived themselves as experimenting while solving puzzles. Students tended to converge on solutions, while professionals continued exploring. Emotions were visible in behaviour but hard to surface through written reflection alone. Think-aloud sessions revealed immediate reasoning; written reflections elicited more meta-cognitive reflection. The theme Sensemaking / reflection-in-action captured how participants framed problems, navigated dead ends, and shifted strategies.
  Reflections: Puzzles are not the intervention: the entire sequence of solving, debriefing, and reflecting is. Designing that sequence more deliberately is the work ahead. We also developed an open-source web application with built-in analytics to customise workshops.

</details>


### [147] [N-Version Programming with Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20158)
*Javier Ron,Benoit Baudry,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文在现代AI编程代理的背景下重新审视了N版本编程的经典概念。通过重现Knight-Leveson实验，研究了不同代理系统、模型和实现语言之间的多样性是否能产生不同的故障模式。使用发射拦截器程序规范，评估了48个代理生成的实现，在共享预言机和100万随机测试输入上进行了测试。结果显示存在显著的共因故障，且许多共现故障可追溯到规范中困难或模糊的部分。此外，多数投票三版本单元的平均故障数从单版本的387.44降至130.99，并且有11,844个N版本单元表现出零观测故障。这些结果表明，使用编程代理的N版本编程是一种有用的工程策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代AI编程代理广泛应用的背景下，重新审视N版本编程的经典概念，探究不同代理系统、模型和实现语言的多样性是否能创造多样化的故障模式，从而验证N版本编程在AI代理时代是否仍然是一种有效的工程策略。

Method: 基于Knight-Leveson经典的发射拦截器程序规范，使用不同代理系统、模型和实现语言生成48个程序实现，通过共享预言机和100万随机测试输入进行系统性评估，并采用多数投票机制构建三版本单元进行故障分析。

Result: 发现了显著的共因故障模式，许多共现故障可追溯到规范中困难或模糊的部分。多数投票三版本单元的平均故障数从单版本的387.44降至130.99，有11,844个N版本单元实现了零观测故障。

Conclusion: 编程代理的多样性提供了实际效益，使用编程代理的N版本编程是一种有用的工程策略，这是迄今为止最有力的证据。

Abstract: This paper revisits the classical concept on N-version programming in the setting of contemporary AI coding agents. Revisiting the seminal Knight-Leveson experiment, we study whether diversity across agent systems, models, and implementation languages creates diverse failure modes. Using the Knight-Leveson's, Launch Interceptor Program Specification, we evaluate 48 agent-generated implementations on a shared oracle and a campaign of 1,000,000 randomized test inputs. The results show substantial common-mode failure, along the findings of Knight-Leveson. Further analysis that many of those co-occuring failures can be traced to where is specification is particularly hard or ambiguous. We also demonstrate that diversity from coding agents provides practical benefit: across majority voting three-version units, the mean failure count drops from 387.44 for single versions to 130.99 for triples, and 11,844 N-version units exhibit zero observed failures. Our original results is the strongest evidence to date that N-Version Programming with coding agents is a useful engineering strategy.

</details>


### [148] [Qiskit Code Migration with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20173)
*Jose Manuel Suarez,Luis Mariano Bibbo,Joaquin Bogado,Alenandro Fernandez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合LLM与检索增强生成（RAG）的混合方法，利用自动生成的迁移场景分类体系作为结构化知识源，实现Qiskit代码跨版本自动迁移。实验表明，基于分类体系的RAG架构（尤其是限制性检索方案）能显著减少幻觉、提升描述质量，其中Google Gemini Flash-2.5在检测复杂重构场景方面表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子开发工具包（QDKs）快速演进导致技术债务，影响代码可维护性和软件复用；量子软件工程（QSE）领域缺乏高质量训练数据且框架波动性高，通用大语言模型（LLMs）易产生不可靠或幻觉结果。

Method: 提出LLM与RAG相结合的混合方法：自动生成迁移场景分类体系作为结构化、版本特定的知识源以引导模型，通过自动化可扩展工作流评估LLM（Google Gemini Flash-2.5和OpenAI Gpt-oss-20b）在两种检索方案（无约束与限制性）下的表现。

Result: 基于分类体系的RAG架构（尤其是限制性检索方案）显著减少幻觉并提升描述质量；Google Gemini Flash-2.5在检测复杂重构场景方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动的分类学方法能够促进技术独立性、提供抵御API过时的智能助手、确保量子算法在快速演变的生态系统中的长期可用性，并降低量子软件工程的学习曲线。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of Quantum Development Kits (QDKs) introduces a specific form of technical debt that compromises code maintainability and hinders software reuse. In the specialized domain of Quantum Software Engineering (QSE), this challenge is intensified by the scarcity of high-quality training data and the high volatility of emerging frameworks, which often lead general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) to produce unreliable or hallucinated results. This paper proposes a hybrid approach integrating LLMs with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to automate the migration of Qiskit code across versions. The proposed methodology enhances the precision and reliability of migration suggestions by leveraging an automatically generated taxonomy of migration scenarios as the structured, version-specific knowledge source to guide the models. The approach is implemented through an automated, extensible workflow evaluating LLMs (Google Gemini Flash-2.5 and OpenAI Gpt-oss-20b) under different retrieval schemes (unconstrained and restrictive). Results demonstrate that the taxonomy-based RAG architecture, particularly under the restrictive scheme, significantly reduces hallucinations and improves descriptive quality, with Google Gemini Flash-2.5 showing superior performance in detecting complex refactoring scenarios. These findings confirm the potential of this data-centric methodology to foster technological independence and provide robust, intelligent assistants that mitigate API obsolescence, ensuring the long-term availability of quantum algorithms within a rapidly shifting ecosystem and flattening the learning curve within Quantum Software Engineering (QSE).

</details>


### [149] [SysML Modeling of Digital Twins for Renewable Energy Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20230)
*Mohammad Samadi,Luís Miguel Pinho,Andrey Sadovykh,Gabriela Lucas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次探索将SysML与SAREF4ENER本体结合，用于可再生能源社区数字孪生的基于模型的系统工程（MBSE）工作流，通过重新表达工业验证的REC领域模型，识别了SysML在语义上的四个缺口，并提出引入SAREF本体作为参考包来填补这些缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源社区（REC）的数字孪生因设备、合同和运行时数据的异构性而难以构建，亟需一种系统工程方法来规范化建模。

Method: 从工业验证的REC领域模型出发，使用Modelio工具将代表性房屋子集用SysML重新表达，生成两个模块定义图（设备分类图和社区组织视图），并讨论SysML的四个语义缺口，提出引入SAREF4ENERGY本体作为参考包来弥补。

Result: 生成两个SysML图（设备分类与社区组织视图），识别出SySML在REC数字孪生建模中的四个语义缺口，并初步论证了SAREF本体作为补全方案的可行性。

Conclusion: 将SysML与基于SAREF的语义结合用于智能能源数字孪生仍待深入探索，本文为这一方向迈出了第一步。

Abstract: Renewable Energy Communities (RECs) are emerging as a key organizational model for local and global sharing of renewable generation, storage, and flexible loads. Engineering Digital Twins of RECs is made difficult by the heterogeneity of devices, contracts, and runtime data involved. In this paper, we take a first step toward a Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) workflow for REC's Digital Twins. Starting from an industrially-validated REC domain model, we re-express a representative house subset in SysML using the open-source Modelio tool, yielding two Block Definition Diagrams - a device taxonomy and a community organizational view. We then discuss four semantic gaps that plain SysML leaves open and sketch how the SAREF4ENER ontology could be imported as a reference package to close them. Combining SysML with SAREF-based semantics for smart-energy Digital Twins remains largely unexplored, and we position this paper as a first step along that line.

</details>


### [150] [Phoenix: Safe GitHub Issue Resolution via Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20243)
*Kipngeno Koech,Muhammad Adam,Baimam Boukar Jean Jacques,Joao Barros*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Phoenix是一个多智能体LLM系统，通过7层安全控制和基线测试评估策略，从分类到PR创建解决GitHub问题，在SWE-bench Lite的24实例子集上实现75%的oracle解决率且无回归，在14个仓库的42个真实问题中达到100%正确性保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM系统在自动化解决GitHub问题时缺乏足够的安全保障和可靠性，导致代码修改可能引入回归问题；需要通过多智能体协作架构和安全控制机制，在保持正确性的同时提高自动化问题解决的成功率。

Method: 构建由6个专门智能体（规划器、复现器、编码器、测试器、失败分析器、PR智能体）组成的多智能体系统，通过基于标签的GitHub webhook状态机协调；引入7层安全控制和基线感知的测试评估策略，在PR创建前对所有变更进行基线测试运行验证。

Result: 在SWE-bench Lite的24实例子集（生产webhook路径运行）上实现75%的oracle解决率，无pass-to-pass回归；在14个仓库42个真实问题中达到100%正确性保持（硬层级平均122秒）；约一半PR为精确定位的修复，另一半代码路径不正确（规划器定位局限性）。

Conclusion: Phoenix展示了多智能体LLM系统在安全控制下自动化解决GitHub问题的可行性，但规划器的代码定位能力存在局限性，需通过检索增强来改进；部署失败模式（WAF过滤、令牌过期、权限边界、不稳定的CI）推动了各安全机制的设计。

Abstract: We present Phoenix, a multi-agent LLM system that resolves GitHub issues from triage through pull-request creation, combining seven layered safety controls with a baseline-aware test evaluation strategy. Phoenix decomposes the work across six specialized agents. Planner, reproducer, coder, tester, failure analyst and Pull Request (PR) agent, all coordinated by a label-based GitHub webhook state machine. Every change is checked against a baseline test run before a pull request is opened. On a 24-instance slice of SWE-bench Lite. run on the production webhook path, Phoenix oracle-resolves 75% of instances with no pass-to-pass regressions on successful runs; this curated slice is not directly comparable to full-split leaderboard results, and we discuss the limits of the comparison. A complementary pilot on 42 real issues across 14 repositories yields 100% correctness preservation (CP; mean 122s on the hard tier). Manual inspection shows that about half of the resulting pull requests are well-targeted fixes. The other half place code at incorrect paths, a planner localization limitation we are addressing with retrieval. We also report the deployment failure modes (WAF filtering, token expiry, permission boundaries, flaky CI) that motivated each safety mechanism.

</details>


### [151] [Token-Operations-Oriented Inference Optimization Techniques for Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20295)
*Shiguo Lian,Kai Wang,Zhaoxiang Liu,Wen Liu,Minjie Hua,Yutong Liu,Jiangze Yan,Xin Wang,Cong Wang,Yilin Zhang,Yi Shen,Jieyun Huang,Fang Zhao,Huanlin Gao,Ping Chen,Xinyu Yang,Kaikai Zhao,Yao Zhao,Xinggang Wang,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao,Junping Du,Tao Chen,Xiang Gao,Qinghuai Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文围绕以Token为中心的推理优化技术，首次提出四层技术架构（多模型融合、模型优化、算模融合、算网模融合），系统综述了各层关键技术及产业现状，分析了相关技术在真实业务场景中的应用价值，为降低Token生产成本、提升服务效率、保障供应稳定、推动大模型服务从“可用”到“可运营”提供了技术路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型模型推理优化是大模型服务可扩展、低成本、高稳定运行的关键基础。当前Token生产成本高、服务效率不足、供应稳定性差，亟需系统性技术架构来推动大模型服务从“可调用”向“可运营”转型。

Method: 首次提出了以Token为中心的四层推理优化技术架构：多模型融合（Multi-model Fusion）、模型优化（Model Optimization）、算模融合（Compute-Model Fusion）、算网模融合（Compute-Network-Model Fusion），并系统综述了各层面关键技术及产业现状。

Result: 构建了四层技术架构体系，梳理了各层的核心技术和产业应用现状，分析了相关技术在降低Token成本、提升服务效率和保障供应稳定方面的实际业务应用价值。

Conclusion: 本文提出的四层架构为大模型推理优化提供了系统性技术路径，有助于推动大模型服务从“可用”走向“可运营”，实现Token生产成本降低、服务效率提升和供应稳定性保障。

Abstract: Large model inference optimization serves as a key foundation for supporting the scalable, low-cost, and highly stable operation of large model services. Centered on token-oriented inference optimization technology, this paper proposes for the first time a four-layer technical architecture consisting of Multi-model Fusion, Model Optimization, Compute-Model Fusion, and Compute-Network-Model Fusion. It systematically reviews the key technologies and current industry status across these four levels and analyzes the application value of related technologies in real-world business scenarios. This paper provides a practical technical path for reducing token production costs, improving token service efficiency, ensuring the stability of token supply, and driving the transition of large model services from being merely callable to being operable.

</details>


### [152] [A Model-Driven Approach for Developing Families of Reinforcement Learning Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20324)
*Xiaoran Liu,Istvan David*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型驱动的方法，通过混合遗传算法和模型转换技术，自动生成强化学习训练所需的环境家族（environment families），以减少人工开发成本，并在野火缓解场景和课程学习中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习训练需要多个相似但略有不同的环境（环境家族），而人工开发这些环境家族耗时且易出错，难以规模化，因此迫切需要一种自动化、可扩展的方法来生成环境家族。

Method: 提出模型驱动的方法：采用混合遗传算法（结合种群全局搜索与启发式局部搜索），将变异和约束表达为模型转换，通过先进的模型转换引擎实现搜索过程的自动化，并开发了原型工具。

Result: 在野火缓解场景和课程学习（依赖环境家族的特定学习范式）中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该模型驱动方法能有效自动化生成RL训练环境家族，提升开发效率，并在实际场景中表现良好。

Abstract: Virtual training environments are software-intensive systems in which reinforcement learning (RL) agents learn, adapt, and demonstrate meaningful behavior. Virtual training environments offer a safe and cost-efficient alternative to training agents in real-world settings. However, to converge, most realistic RL problems require training in multiple, mostly similar but slightly different environments - i.e., families of environment variants. The typical development process of environment families is a labor-intensive and error-prone manual endeavor that does not scale well. To alleviate these issues, in this paper, we propose a model-driven approach for developing families of RL training environments. To obtain the family of environments, we develop an approach and prototype tool. In our approach, a hybrid genetic algorithm - a combination of population-based global search and heuristic local search - generates environment families. Mutations and constraints are expressed as model transformations and are operationalized into a search process by a state-of-the-art model transformation engine. We demonstrate the soundness of our approach in a wildfire mitigation scenario and curriculum learning - a particular learning paradigm that relies on environment families.

</details>


### [153] [AutoPass: Evidence-Guided LLM Agents for Compiler Performance Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20373)
*Zepeng Li,Jie Ren,Zhanyong Tang,Jie Zheng,Zheng Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AutoPass是一个多智能体框架，通过打开编译器黑箱，让LLM查询编译器内部优化状态和中间表示，并利用运行时反馈迭代优化编译配置，在无需训练的情况下在x86-64和ARM64上分别实现1.043x和1.117x的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在代码编译任务中展现出潜力，但将其应用于运行时性能调优面临复杂微架构效应和噪声运行时测量的挑战。现有自动调优方案将编译器视为黑箱，缺乏对编译器内部状态的感知能力。

Method: 提出AutoPass多智能体框架：1) 开放编译器给LLM，支持查询编译器内部优化状态和分析中间表示；2) 利用编译器和运行时证据引导LLM生成优化决策；3) 通过迭代搜索过程，使用运行时反馈诊断回归并指导延迟改进编辑；4) 推理-only、免训练范式。

Result: 在LLVM编译器上实现，在服务器级x86-64和嵌入式ARM64系统上评估。相比LLVM -O3，在x86-64上实现1.043x几何平均加速比，在ARM64上实现1.117x几何平均加速比，优于专家调优启发式和经典自动调优方法。

Conclusion: AutoPass通过将编译器内部状态暴露给LLM并结合运行时反馈，在无需离线训练或任务特定微调的条件下，实现了超越专家经验和经典自动调优方法的编译性能优化效果，且具有良好的跨平台迁移能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for code compilation tasks, but applying them to runtime performance tuning is difficult due to complex microarchitectural effects and noisy runtime measurements. We present AutoPass, a multi-agent framework for compiler performance tuning that uses compiler and runtime evidence to guide LLM-generated optimization decisions. Rather than treating the compiler as a black box like prior auto-tuning schemes, AutoPass opens up the compiler to the LLM, enabling it to query compiler-internal optimization states and analyze the intermediate representation to orchestrate compiler options. The search process iteratively refines optimization configurations using measured runtime feedback to diagnose regressions and guide latency-improving edits. AutoPass operates in an inference-only, training-free setting and requires no offline training or task-specific fine-tuning, making it readily applicable to new benchmarks and platforms. We implement AutoPass on the LLVM compiler and evaluate it on server-grade x86-64 and embedded ARM64 systems. AutoPass outperforms expert-tuned heuristics and classical autotuning methods, achieving geometric-mean speedups of 1.043x and 1.117x over LLVM -O3 on x86-64 and ARM64, respectively.

</details>


### [154] [Probe-and-Refine Tuning of Repository Guidance for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20512)
*Asa Shepard,Jeannie Albrecht*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 probe-and-refine tuning 方法，通过合成 bug 探测任务迭代优化仓库的 AGENTS.md 指导文件。实验表明，该方法在 SWE-bench Verified 上比静态知识库和未指导基线分别提升 4.7 和 7.5 个百分点（均 p<0.001），提升来源于覆盖率增加而非补丁质量改进。进一步实验显示指导文件能让智能体有效利用更大步数预算，且调优循环在模型生成诊断能力不足时会退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的编码智能体需要仓库层面的操作知识（如文件组织结构、测试运行方式、历史修复模式），这些知识不存在于代码本身。工程师用 AGENTS.md 文件提供上下文，但 LLM 生成的指导究竟有益还是有害存在争议，本文旨在解决这一争议并找到有效生成指导的方法。

Method: 提出 probe-and-refine tuning 流程：使用合成 bug-fix 探测任务，通过单次 LLM 调用迭代诊断和修补仓库的指导文件。调优过程中不使用智能体循环或工具调用。实验使用 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 在 SWE-bench Verified 上运行 200 步，进行四次独立试验。

Result: probe-and-refine 达到 33.0% 平均解决率，对比静态知识库 28.3% 和未指导基线 25.5%（均 p<0.001）。改进来自覆盖率：可评估补丁的实例增加 14.5 个百分点，而每个补丁的精度统计上不变（~59%，p=0.119）。步数预算实验表明指导能让智能体有效利用更多步数。跨模型实验（NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B）发现调优循环在模型生成诊断能力不足时退化。

Conclusion: 指导文件的生成方式（而非其存在与否）是影响编码智能体性能的关键变量。probe-and-refine 调优通过增加覆盖率提升性能，而非提高补丁质量。该方法需要模型具备足够的诊断能力才能有效工作。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents need higher-level operational knowledge about a repository (which files house which subsystems, how to run the test suite, which workflows have historically led to wrong fixes) that does not exist in the code itself. Engineers typically maintain \texttt{AGENTS.md} files to supply this context as instructions for coding agents, but whether they help is contested: recent studies disagree on whether LLM-generated guidance improves or harms agent performance. In this paper we show that how the guidance is produced is the decisive variable, and introduce \emph{probe-and-refine tuning}: a procedure that uses synthetic bug-fix probes to iteratively diagnose and patch a repository's guidance file through single-shot LLM calls, with no agent loop or tool use during tuning. On SWE-bench Verified across four independent trials with Qwen3.5-35B-A3B at 200 steps, probe-and-refine achieves 33.0\,\% mean resolve rate vs.\ 28.3\,\% for the static knowledge base used to initialize it and 25.5\,\% for an unguided baseline ($p < 0.001$ for both probe-and-refine contrasts). The improvement comes from coverage rather than precision: refined guidance produces evaluable patches for 14.5 percentage points (pp) more instances while per-patch precision remains statistically constant ($\sim$59\,\%, $p = 0.119$), showing that improved guidance helps agents reach the correct file rather than improving the quality of the changes they make. Further, a step-budget experiment shows that guidance is what lets the agent use a larger step budget productively, and a cross-model experiment with NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B finds that the tuning loop degrades when the model cannot generate sufficiently diagnostic output, though per-patch precision remains constant even then.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [MonaVec: A Training-Free Embedded Vector Search Kernel for Edge and Offline AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19458)
*Oğuzhan Yenen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MonaVec是一个确定性、训练无关的嵌入式向量搜索内核，专为边缘和离线AI设计。它通过随机Hadamard变换将任意分布映射到标准正态分布，再使用预计算Lloyd-Max表实现4-bit量化（8倍压缩），无需训练数据或数据扫描。索引存储为单个.mvec文件，内嵌ChaCha20旋转种子确保跨架构结果可重现。在AG News数据集上，4-bit BruteForce以27MB内存达到0.960 Recall@10，超越float32 FAISS-IVF和8-bit usearch。支持L2数据的全局标准化扩展，以及IvfFlat和HNSW后端。纯Rust实现，提供Python绑定和SIMD分发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量搜索系统依赖持久服务器、大量RAM或训练数据扫描，无法满足边缘和离线AI场景（无服务器、无网络、无训练数据）。MonaVec旨在填补这一空白，提供类似SQLite的部署模式：一个文件、一个函数调用、随处运行。

Method: 1) 采用随机Hadamard变换（RHDH）将输入分布条件化为N(0,1)，实现数据无关预处理；2) 使用预计算Lloyd-Max量化表进行4-bit无训练量化；3) 内嵌ChaCha20旋转种子确保跨架构字节级确定性；4) 全局标准化（fit()）扩展支持L2数据；5) 提供IvfFlat和HNSW后端处理百万级向量；6) 纯Rust实现，支持AVX-512/AVX2/NEON/SIMD分发。

Result: 在AG News语义嵌入（45K x 1024维BGE-M3，cosine相似度）上，MonaVec 4-bit BruteForce以27MB内存达到0.960 Recall@10，在召回率上领先float32 FAISS-IVF和8-bit usearch，但在峰值吞吐量上有所牺牲以换取字节级确定性。

Conclusion: MonaVec成功将SQLite式部署理念引入向量检索领域，为边缘AI、离线智能体、嵌入式检索等场景提供了训练无关、确定性、高压缩比的轻量级解决方案，特别适合资源受限环境下的向量搜索需求。

Abstract: We present MonaVec, a deterministic, embedded vector-search kernel for edge and offline AI -- settings where server infrastructure, network connectivity, and training data are all unavailable. Existing vector-search systems assume a persistent server, gigabytes of RAM, or a training pass over the corpus; MonaVec instead targets the deployment profile of SQLite: one file, one function call, runs anywhere. Its quantization core is training-free by default and data-oblivious: a Randomized Hadamard Transform (RHDH) conditions any input distribution toward N(0,1), so precomputed Lloyd-Max tables quantize to 4 bits (8x smaller) with no learned codebook and no data pass. The index persists as a single .mvec file whose embedded ChaCha20 rotation seed makes results reproducible across architectures and byte-identical within a build -- a determinism guarantee that parallel-build graph libraries cannot offer.
  On semantic embeddings (AG News, 45K x 1024-dim BGE-M3, cosine), MonaVec 4-bit BruteForce reaches 0.960 Recall@10 in 27 MB -- leading float32 FAISS-IVF and 8-bit usearch on recall -- while trading peak throughput for byte-identical determinism. A single-pass global standardization (fit()) extends the same data-oblivious pipeline to magnitude-sensitive L2 data, and optional IvfFlat and HNSW backends carry it to million-vector corpora.
  MonaVec is implemented in pure Rust with Python bindings and runtime SIMD dispatch (AVX-512/AVX2/NEON/scalar). It targets on-device RAG, offline agents, and embedded retrieval -- the niche SQLite occupies for relational data: one file, one call, runs anywhere.

</details>


### [156] [VCG: A Multimodal Retrieval Framework for E-Commerce Video Feeds under Extreme Cold-Start Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19627)
*Katya Mirylenka,Egor Malykh,Mahdyar Ravanbakhsh,Michael Gygli,Marco-Andrea Buchmann,Andrew Dzhoha,Svitlana Borzenko,Francesca Catino,Mohamed Gaafar,Maarten Versteegh,Thomas Kober,Dario d'Andrea,Ellie Langhans*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VCG的多模态检索系统，用于解决短格式视频电商场景中的极端冷启动和位置/时长偏置问题。该系统基于领域适应后的CLIP模型，将用户和视频映射到共享语义空间实现零样本检索，在线A/B测试显示深度视频完成率提升50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字电商从静态搜索驱动转向动态沉浸式视频流，导致(1)新短视频缺乏交互历史，传统协同过滤失效；(2)沉浸式feed引入强烈的位置和时长偏差，扭曲标准参与信号。

Method: 构建VCG系统：(1)基于领域适应后的CLIP视觉-语言模型，将用户和视频映射到共享语义空间；(2)采用判别式（CLIP）而非生成式（LLM）嵌入以避免嵌入空间坍塌；(3)支持三种双向检索场景：产品到视频、视频到产品、零样本语义搜索。

Result: 生成式模型在属性预测上表现优异，但在检索任务中存在嵌入空间坍塌问题；在线A/B测试表明VCG有效缓解了参与偏差，深度视频完成率提升50%。

Conclusion: VCG系统通过视觉内容而非行为历史实现零样本检索，成功解决电商短视频场景的极端冷启动和偏置问题，为动态视频feed中的候选生成提供了可扩展的多模态检索方案。

Abstract: The digital commerce landscape is shifting from static, search-driven catalogs to dynamic, immersive video feeds. This transition introduces an ``extreme cold-start'' problem: unlike traditional items, new short-form videos lack the dense interaction history required for collaborative filtering. Furthermore, immersive feeds introduce strong position and duration biases that distort standard engagement signals. In this paper, we demonstrate the Video Candidate Generation (VCG) system, a scalable multimodal retrieval engine designed to solve these challenges in a large-scale e-commerce environment. By leveraging a domain-adapted vision-language model (based on CLIP), we map users and videos into a shared semantic space, enabling zero-shot retrieval based on visual content rather than behavioral history. We detail the system's architecture and present a rigorous evaluation comparing generative (LLM) vs. discriminative (CLIP) embeddings. Our results show that while generative models excel at attribute prediction, they suffer from embedding space collapse in retrieval tasks. Online A/B testing demonstrates that VCG effectively mitigates engagement biases, yielding a 50\% uplift in deep video completion. To showcase the system's capabilities, we present an interactive demonstration featuring three bi-directional retrieval scenarios: Product-to-Video, Video-to-Product, and Zero-Shot Semantic Search.

</details>


### [157] [Token Factory: Efficiently Integrating Diverse Signals into Large Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19635)
*Xilun Chen,Shao-Chuan Wang,Baykal Cakici,Lukasz Heldt,Lichan Hong,Raghu Keshavan,Aniruddh Nath,Li Wei,Xinyang Xi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Token Factory框架，将传统推荐信号转化为软令牌（soft tokens），使大型推荐模型（LRMs）能够高效集成异构输入特征，避免提示长度爆炸并提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将推荐信号文本化或创建离散项表示会导致提示过长、内存占用大和计算开销高，因此需要一种更高效的方式将传统信号集成到基于Transformer的LRM中。

Method: 提出Token Factory框架，将传统信号转化为LRM可直接处理的软令牌（soft tokens），实现异构输入特征的高效集成与压缩。

Result: 在工业级生产推荐环境中验证了Token Factory的有效性，能够防止提示长度爆炸并提升模型性能。

Conclusion: Token Factory通过软令牌机制有效解决了传统信号与LRM架构集成的效率和效果难题，为大规模推荐系统提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Recommendation Models (LRMs) have demonstrated promising capabilities in industry-scale recommendation tasks. However, holistically integrating traditional signals into these transformer-based architectures effectively and efficiently remains a major challenge. Conventional approaches that "textualize" these signals directly or create discrete item representations often lead to excessively long prompts, substantial memory footprints, and high computational overhead. To overcome these limitations, we propose "Token Factory", a framework designed to transform traditional signals into "soft tokens" that can be directly processed by LRMs. This approach enables efficient integration and compression of heterogeneous input features, preventing prompt length explosion while enhancing model performance. We detail the architecture of Token Factory and present experimental results validating its effectiveness in a production-scale recommendation environment.

</details>


### [158] [SAFE-Cascade: Cost-Adaptive Vision-Language Routing for Chart Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19646)
*Ayush Dwivedi,Qixin Wang,Ashvi Soni,Ruoteng Wang,Han Li,Animesh Mahapatra,Neeraj Agrawal,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SAFE-Cascade 是一个面向图表问答的成本自适应系统，通过 OCR+文本语言模型快速回答，并利用学习到的路由器决定是否升级到视觉语言模型（VLM），在保持与全 VLM 基线相当准确率（69.1% vs 67.7%）的同时，将 VLM 调用减少 26.9%、成本降低 9.3%。系统提供了透明的决策过程可视化界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）在图表问答中虽然强大，但每次查询都调用 VLM 成本过高。许多问题仅凭 OCR 文本和轻量级语言推理即可回答，无需昂贵的视觉理解，因此需要一种成本自适应的路由机制来减少不必要的 VLM 调用。

Method: SAFE-Cascade 采用级联架构：首先通过 OCR 提取图表文本，利用纯文本语言模型（gpt-5-mini）获取初步答案；然后通过一个基于随机森林的学习路由器，根据推理时特征判断是否接受文本答案，或者将查询升级到 VLM（gemini-2.5-flash-image）。系统提供交互式界面，展示 OCR 证据、路由概率、升级决策、成本与延迟等信息，并允许用户调整升级阈值来探索准确率-成本前沿。

Result: 在 ChartQA 测试集（375 个样本）上，SAFE-Cascade 达到 69.1% 的统一准确率并调用 73.1% VLM，而全 VLM 基线为 67.7% 准确率与 100% VLM 调用。准确率提升 1.4 个百分点但统计上不确定，因此系统被认为与全 VLM 性能相当，同时减少 26.9% VLM 调用和 9.3% 估计成本。

Conclusion: 选择性模态路由（Selective Modality Routing）能够使多模态知识系统更加透明、可调优和成本感知。SAFE-Cascade 证明通过智能路由可以在不牺牲性能的前提下显著降低计算成本，并为用户提供了理解视觉理解何时真正必要的透明接口。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are powerful for chart question answering, but invoking a VLM for every query can be unnecessarily expensive when many questions are answerable from OCR text and lightweight language reasoning. We demonstrate SAFE-Cascade, an interactive system for cost-adaptive chart question answering. Given a chart image and a natural-language question, SAFE-Cascade first extracts chart text with OCR, obtains a provisional answer from a text-only language model, and then uses a learned router to decide whether to accept the text answer or escalate to a VLM. The demo exposes this decision process to users: OCR evidence, text-only answer, routing probability, escalation decision, final answer, estimated cost, and estimated latency are shown side by side. SAFE-Cascade is designed as a transparent interface for understanding when visual grounding is actually needed. Users can upload or select charts, ask questions, inspect the evidence used by each pathway, compare text-only and VLM answers, and adjust the escalation threshold to explore the accuracy-cost frontier. The system is implemented with Azure Document Intelligence for OCR, gpt-5-mini as the text-only model, gemini-2.5-flash-image as the VLM, and a Random Forest router trained on inference-time features. On a held-out ChartQA test split of 375 examples from a 2,500-example experiment, SAFE-Cascade achieves 69.1% unified accuracy with 73.1% VLM invocation, compared with 67.7% accuracy and 100% VLM invocation for the full-VLM baseline. The observed +1.4 percentage-point difference is statistically uncertain, so we interpret SAFE-Cascade as matching full-VLM performance while reducing VLM calls by 26.9% and estimated cost by 9.3%. The demonstration shows how selective modality routing can make multimodal knowledge systems more transparent, tunable, and cost-aware.

</details>


### [159] [Closing the Calibration Gap in Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19719)
*Aditeya Baral,Radoslav Ralev,Iliya Sotirov Zhechev,Srijith Rajamohan,Jen Agarwal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文指出现有语义缓存系统使用PR-AUC评估存在严重缺陷，提出P-CHR AUC和CRR两个新指标，并证明模型选择本质是校准问题而非排序问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义缓存系统使用PR-AUC作为评估指标，但该指标仅衡量排序质量而忽略固定阈值下的可用性，导致部署时表现最好的模型在实际运行中反而最差。

Method: 提出P-CHR AUC（衡量不同缓存利用率下的精确度）和CRR（量化离线排序质量在部署时的保留程度），将离线与部署质量差距分解为可恢复的校准分量和由数据集正例率决定的不可约结构分量。

Result: 校准差距由训练目标决定而非数据规模，事后校准仅能部分弥合该差距；离线最优的模型在部署时往往表现最差。

Conclusion: 语义缓存的模型选择本质是一个校准问题而非排序问题，评估校准是缩小离线-部署差距的第一步。

Abstract: Semantic caching cuts LLM inference costs by serving a cached response to semantically similar queries. Standard practice evaluates these systems using PR-AUC, a metric that only measures how well scores rank and ignores whether they are usable at a fixed threshold. We show this mismatch leads to systematically poor deployment choices, as models with the highest PR-AUC are often the worst in operation. We introduce Precision-Cache Hit Ratio (P-CHR) AUC, a cache-aware metric that measures precision across cache utilization levels, and Calibration Retention Rate (CRR), which captures how much offline ranking quality survives at deployment. We decompose the operational gap between offline and deployed quality into a recoverable calibration component and an irreducible structural component fixed by the dataset's positive rate. Our experiments show that the calibration gap is governed by the training objective rather than data scale, and post-hoc calibration only partially closes it. Ultimately, model selection for semantic caching is a calibration problem, not a ranking one, and measuring it is the first step to closing the gap.

</details>


### [160] [Stellar: Scalable Multimodal Document Retrieval for Natural Language Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19960)
*Yuxiang Guo,Zhonghao Hu,Yuren Mao,Yuhang Liu,Congcong Ge,Xiaolu Zhang,Jun Zhou,Yunjun Gao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Stellar是一个可扩展的多模态文档检索框架，通过磁盘存储令牌级嵌入、基于词汇表示过滤减少候选集、以及磁盘支持的动态加载机制，在保持检索效果的同时将内存开销和查询延迟降低1-2个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的多模态文档检索方法使用多向量表示（令牌级嵌入）和延迟交互机制，虽效果好但内存开销巨大，导致可扩展性差，难以在实际中部署。

Method: Stellar包含两个核心组件：(i) 基于词汇表示的过滤（LRF），微调多模态大语言模型作为稀疏编码器生成高质量词汇表示，高效过滤文档以缩小候选集；(ii) 高效的磁盘支持延迟交互（DLI），通过平衡聚类算法优化磁盘上令牌嵌入的存储布局，并利用成本模型动态加载必要的嵌入到内存。

Result: 在四个真实世界基准和一个新构建的大规模数据集上，Stellar相比现有方法将内存开销和查询延迟降低1-2个数量级，同时不损害检索效果。

Conclusion: Stellar通过磁盘存储和动态加载策略有效解决了多向量表示方法的内存可扩展性问题，为多模态RAG系统的实际部署提供了一种高效的检索方案。

Abstract: Multimodal document retrieval--selecting the most relevant multimodal document from a large corpus to answer a natural language query--plays an essential role in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems. State-of-the-art methods represent each document and query with multiple token-level embeddings and use late interaction to achieve high effectiveness. However, such multi-vector representations incur substantial memory overhead during retrieval, leading to poor scalability and hindering real-world deployment. In this paper, we present Stellar, a scalable multimodal document retrieval framework that stores token-level document embeddings on disk and loads only a small set of candidate embeddings into memory for late interaction. Stellar comprises two key components: (i) Lexical Representation-based Filtering (LRF), which fine-tunes a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a sparse encoder to produce high-quality lexical representations, enabling efficient and effective document filtering to significantly reduce the candidate set; (ii) Efficient Disk-backed Late Interaction (DLI), which designs an on-disk token embedding storage layout guided by a balanced clustering algorithm, and dynamically loads only the necessary token embeddings into memory using a simple yet effective cost model. Extensive experiments on four real-world benchmarks and a newly presented large-scale dataset demonstrate that Stellar reduces memory overhead and query latency by 1-2 orders of magnitude compared to existing methods without compromising retrieval effectiveness.

</details>


### [161] [PACMS: Submodular Context Selection as a Pluggable Engine for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20047)
*Manu Ghulyani,Arunabh Singh,Karan Bharadwaj,Ankit Nath,Suranjan Goswami*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文指出现有LLM智能体在上下文窗口管理中普遍采用基于时间顺序的截断机制（如最近优先截断+周期性摘要），这种主题盲视策略会导致早期建立的关键事实被丢弃，而无关的近期内容却被保留。作者提出需要一种将对话轮次、记忆条目和工具输出统一视为候选池，在提示组装时基于相关性进行选择的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的上下文管理机制（最近优先截断）是主题盲视的，会导致早期重要事实被丢弃而无关近期内容被保留，无法满足需要跨多轮对话回忆信息的智能体需求。

Method: 将记忆条目、对话轮次和工具输出统一视为一个候选池，在提示组装时根据与当前查询的相关性进行选择，而非基于时间顺序的截断。

Result: 现有方法（RAG和上下文压缩）均无法解决该问题：RAG处理外部文档而非已有上下文，上下文压缩是查询盲视且有损压缩，两者均未将不同来源的上下文统一视为可基于相关性选择的候选池。

Conclusion: 需要一种新的上下文选择机制，在提示组装时刻基于相关性从所有已积累的上下文（对话、记忆、工具输出）中进行选择，而非基于时间顺序的截断。

Abstract: Conversational and tool-using LLM agents operate over a context window that fills from several directions simultaneously. As a session proceeds, the agent accumulates user and assistant turns, entries drawn from a persistent memory store, and often largest of all, the verbatim outputs of tool calls such as file reads, search results, and API responses. Once the cumulative context exceeds the model's token budget, the framework must decide what to keep.
  The prevailing mechanism is recency truncation, sometimes paired with periodic summarization. This is topic-blind: a fact established early in a session is discarded simply because it is old, even when the current user query is about exactly that fact; conversely, verbose but irrelevant recent material is retained. Agents that must recall information across many turns, the defining case for memory, are precisely where recency truncation fails.
  Existing alternatives sit outside the agent's assembly step. Retrieval augmented generation fetches external documents into the prompt but does not arbitrate the agent's \emph{already-present} pooled context. Context-compression methods reduce token count by rewriting or pruning text, but operate query-blind and lossily. Neither treats memory entries, conversation turns, and tool outputs as a single candidate pool to be selected from by relevance at the moment the prompt is assembled.

</details>


### [162] [Generative Engine Optimization at Scale: Measuring Brand Visibility Across AI Search Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20065)
*Pratyush Kumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文基于2026年3月至5月期间对100+品牌在Ranqo平台上的10万+次AI提示响应分析，首次大规模量化了生成引擎优化（GEO）中的品牌可见性。研究发现品牌在AI引擎中的可见性呈现明显的三层梯队结构：全球知名品牌出现率73%，成熟中端/区域品牌44%，小众/小品牌仅11%。引用来源中78%来自企业网站，最被引用的内容格式是“最佳榜单”文章（约21%）。品牌情感极性波动是提及频率波动的6.7倍。论文最后提出七项v1.1协议建议，用于测试提升AI可见性的因果方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着用户越来越多地直接从ChatGPT、Claude、Perplexity、Gemini等AI搜索引擎获取答案，传统的搜索引擎优化（SEO）正面临转型。品牌需要了解AI引擎如何表示、引用和推荐它们，但针对品牌在AI引擎中可见性的量化测量方法尚属空白，尤其是对于非头部品牌（中小企业、D2C品牌、创作者、早期初创企业）而言。

Method: 研究基于Ranqo平台追踪的100+品牌，收集2026年3月至5月期间10万+次AI提示响应数据。通过分析品牌在AI回答中的出现频率、引用来源类型、内容格式分布以及情感倾向等维度，量化品牌可见性并建立分级评估体系。

Result: 1）品牌可见性呈现三层梯队：全球知名品牌73%、成熟中端/区域品牌44%、小众/小品牌仅11%，每层约30个百分点的落差。2）引用来源中78%为企业网站，YouTube是非企业来源中的领先者，其次为Reddit、编辑媒体和维基百科。3）最高杠杆内容格式为“最佳榜单”文章，占总引用约21%。4）品牌情感极性（正面/负面）的波动频率是提及与否波动频率的6.7倍，情感信号极不稳定。

Conclusion: AI品牌可见性可以被量化测量，且因平台而异，并随品牌成熟度强烈变化。论文为衡量GEO提供了首个大规模基线，并提出了七项v1.1协议建议，旨在测试特定推荐策略能否因果性地提升AI可见性，为后续研究和方法论标准化奠定基础。

Abstract: People increasingly get answers straight from AI search engines like ChatGPT, Claude, Perplexity, and Gemini rather than scrolling search results. Brands that once focused on search engine optimization (SEO) must now optimize for how these engines represent, cite, and recommend them -- a shift variously called Generative Engine Optimization (GEO), Answer Engine Optimization (AEO), and AI Search Visibility. We treat AEO and AI Visibility as part of GEO, and study how to measure brand visibility across AI engines: what they value when they cite a brand, which sources they rely on, and what content large language models surface. The hard case is everyone outside the already-authoritative top brands -- SMEs, D2C brands, creators, and early-stage startups.
  We analyze 100K+ prompt responses across 100+ brands tracked on Ranqo between March and May 2026. First visibility runs form a clear three-tier brand-stature ladder: global household names (e.g., Stripe, Nike) appear in 73% of relevant AI answers on their first run; established mid-market and regional brands (e.g., Olipop, Klaviyo) in 44%; niche and small brands in just 11% -- about 30 percentage points per step. When engines cite sources, about 78% go to corporate websites; among non-corporate sources YouTube leads, ahead of Reddit, editorial media, and Wikipedia. The highest-leverage page is the ranked "best-of" listicle, the most-cited content format at about 21% of all citations. Sentiment is the unstable signal: whether a brand is framed positively or negatively flips about 6.7 times more often than whether it is mentioned at all.
  These findings provide a first large-scale baseline for measuring GEO: AI brand visibility can be measured, differs by platform, and varies strongly by brand maturity. We close by proposing seven v1.1 protocols to test whether specific recommendations can causally improve AI visibility.

</details>


### [163] [ScholarQuest: A Taxonomy-Guided Benchmark for Agentic Academic Paper Search in Open Literature Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20235)
*Tingyue Pan,Mingyue Cheng,Daoyu Wang,Yitong Zhou,Jie Ouyang,Qi Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ScholarQuest是一个大规模、基于分类法引导的学术论文搜索基准测试，用于评估基于LLM的智能体在真实开放文献环境下的学术搜索能力。实验表明，现有最优智能体方法仅达到0.314的Recall@100和0.355的Recall@All，性能提升空间巨大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学术论文搜索基准不足以在真实开放文献环境下系统评估基于LLM的智能体搜索能力，缺乏大规模、多维度、可复现的评估框架。

Method: 基于超过1000个计算机科学主题和四种代表性研究意图（方法导向、设定锚定、比较型、范围控制）构建基准；提供可扩展的答案构建机制和共享检索后端ScholarBase以实现可复现评估。

Result: 智能体方法优于单次检索基线，但最佳智能体仅达到0.314 Recall@100和0.355 Recall@All；进一步分析了搜索效率、意图级别鲁棒性和失败案例。

Conclusion: ScholarQuest能够为学术论文搜索智能体提供多维度的评估信号，当前最佳方法仍有显著提升空间，该基准可有效推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Academic paper search is a core step in scientific research, and LLM-based search agents are emerging as a promising paradigm for iterative, intent-driven literature exploration. However, existing benchmarks are insufficient for systematically evaluating agentic academic search under realistic open literature environments. We propose ScholarQuest, a large-scale, taxonomy-guided benchmark for agentic academic paper search. ScholarQuest is constructed from over 1,000 computer science topics and four representative research intents, including method-oriented, setting-anchored, comparison-based, and scope-controlled queries. It further provides scalable answer construction and a shared retrieval backend ScholarBase for reproducible evaluation. Benchmarking results show that agentic methods outperform single-shot retrieval baselines, yet the best-performing agent only achieves 0.314 Recall@100 and 0.355 Recall@All, indicating substantial room for improvement. In addition, analyses of search efficiency, intent-level robustness, and failure cases further highlight the benchmark's ability to provide multi-dimensional evaluation signals for academic paper search agents.

</details>


### [164] [ELVA: Exploring Ranking-Driven Universal Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20280)
*Yuhan Liu,Pei Fu,Hang Li,Yukun Qi,Chao Jiang,Jingwen Fu,Zhen Liu,Bin Qin,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Jingmin Xin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ELVA框架，通过基于规则的强化学习（RLVR）缓解多模态大模型在通用多模态检索中的"粒度盲视"问题，即对比学习忽略查询中粒度信息导致的检索困难。ELVA通过排序驱动的MLLM优化负样本排序并扩大正负样本相似度差距，同时引入MRBench基准评估多粒度查询性能。在标准检索基准上达到SOTA，MRBench上提升13.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比学习范式在通用多模态检索中存在粒度盲视问题——模型忽略查询中的粒度级信息，因为对比学习将样本视为二元分类（正/负），忽略了每个负样本携带的不同信息，导致无法有效处理复杂多粒度查询。

Method: 1) 将RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）扩展到检索任务，无需奖励模型即可探索新的排序行为；2) 利用基于规则的奖励联合优化负样本排序，同时扩大正负样本相似度差距；3) 引入多粒度查询基准MRBench进行评测。

Result: 在标准检索基准上达到最先进水平，在MRBench上提升13.1%，有效缓解了粒度盲视问题。

Conclusion: ELVA通过排序驱动的RL框架有效解决了多模态检索中的粒度盲视问题，证明了负样本差异化处理以及基于规则的RL在检索任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) via contrastive learning has become a mainstream paradigm for improving the performance of Universal Multimodal Retrieval (UMR). However, previous works have ignored the grain blindness when adapting the contrastive paradigm into retrieval tasks. Grain blindness refers to the tendency of the model to overlook grain-level information contained in the query, which is crucial for effectively handling complex queries. This stems from contrastive learning treating samples as a binary classification (positive/negative), while ignoring the different information carried by each negative sample. To address this, we argue that negatives should be treated differently according to their similarity to the positive sample, enabling the model to learn distinct grain information from each negative. In this paper, we introduce a simple but effective framework, called ELVA, a novel rule-based RL framework that mitigates grain blindness through ranking-driven MLLMs. 1) Instead of relying on reward models, we extend Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards
  (RLVR) to retrieval tasks, allowing the model to explore new ranking behaviors without explicit ranking labels. 2) By utilizing rule-based rewards, our approach jointly optimizes the ranking of negative samples while enlarging the similarity gap between positive and negative. To more precisely measure grain blindness, we further introduce MRBench, a new benchmark specifically designed for multi-grain query scenarios. ELVA achieves state-of-the-art results across standard retrieval benchmarks, and its notable 13.1% improvement on MRBench further demonstrates its effectiveness in alleviating grain blindness.

</details>


### [165] [Structuring and Tokenizing Distributed User Interest Context for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20554)
*Ruizhong Qiu,Yinglong Xia,Dongqi Fu,Hanqing Zeng,Ren Chen,Xiangjun Fan,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: G2Rec是一个统一了全局图协同建模与语义标记化的大规模生成式推荐框架，通过捕获用户兴趣原型来提升工业级序列推荐性能，并在线上部署和公开数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法在建模用户行为时，图方法难以同时捕获全局信息并保证可扩展性，而语义标记化方法缺乏显式监督信号导致表示不准确，两者均无法有效组织并注入复杂的行为和语义上下文。

Method: 提出G2Rec框架，统一了基于图的全局用户协同建模与语义标记化方法，无需真实用户兴趣标签即可捕获语义化的用户兴趣原型，实现更全面的行为上下文建模。

Result: 在多个产品表面的线上部署及公开数据集上的大量实验表明，G2Rec优于现有方法。

Conclusion: G2Rec通过统一图建模与语义标记化，有效解决了工业级生成式推荐中的用户兴趣建模问题，具有可扩展性和优越性能。

Abstract: Generative recommendation is an emerging paradigm that has shown promise in industrial recommendation systems, aiming to predict users' next interactions from their historical behaviors. At the core of generative recommendation lies item tokenization, which bridges item semantics and recommendation models. However, existing methods often struggle to effectively organize and inject complex user-behavioral and item-semantic contexts into recommendation models simultaneously. On the one hand, existing graph-based integration methods, such as graph serialization and graph neural networks, either suffer from scalability issues or exploit only local graph information. On the other hand, existing semantic tokenization methods typically rely on heuristics and lack explicit supervision signals, which may lead to inaccurate or suboptimal semantic representations. To address these limitations in user interest context modeling, we propose G2Rec, a scalable framework that unifies holistic graph-based user co-engagement modeling with semantic tokenization for industrial-scale generative recommendation. Overall, G2Rec enables recommendation models to capture holistic and semantically grounded user interest prototypes without requiring ground-truth user interests, thereby providing more comprehensive and accurate modeling of user behavior contexts in industrial sequential recommendation. Online deployment across product surfaces and extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate the superiority of G2Rec over existing methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Deontic Policies for Runtime Governance of Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19464)
*Anupam Joshi,Tim Finin,Karuna Pande Joshi,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgenticRei框架，基于道义策略语言（OWL+Rei），在LLM驱动的自主AI智能体中实现义务管理、豁免、策略冲突解决及本体推理，弥补XACML/Rego/Cedar等现有引擎在治理结构上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主AI智能体引入新型安全、隐私与合规挑战，现有策略引擎（XACML、Rego、Cedar）仅支持许可/禁止子集，无法管理义务生命周期、元策略冲突、豁免及本体推理等企业治理需求。

Method: 采用基于Rei框架的道义策略语言，以OWL表示，在LLM外部由高性能逻辑引擎运行时评估，统一管控工具调用与智能体间消息。

Result: 示例表明道义策略能捕获现有生产引擎无法表达的安全与隐私治理约束，且与A2AS等行业标准框架自然兼容。

Conclusion: AgenticRei实现了义务、豁免、策略冲突解决及本体推理等关键治理需求，弥补了现有策略引擎的全面性不足。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic AI systems driven by Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce a new class of security, privacy, and compliance challenges: an agent that can invoke tools, manipulate data, install software, and coordinate with peer agents across organizational boundaries must be constrained not just by authentication and access control, but by the full structure of enterprise governance. This includes specifying what agents are permitted and prohibited from doing, what they areobliged to do after certain actions (e.g., notify the CISO), under what conditions a standing obligation may be waived, and which rules take precedence when policies conflict. This governance problem exceeds what current policy engines provide. Systems such as XACML, Rego, and Cedar address only the permit/prohibit subset of this governance structure. They do not provide obligation lifecycle management, meta-policy conflict resolution, dispensations that waive obligations in specific circumstances, and ontological reasoning over domain class hierarchies commonly found in applications such as healthcare, cybersecurity, or data privacy. We propose AgenticRei, which realizes key governance requirements such as obligations, dispensations, policy conflict resolutions, and reasoning over policies, as well as the basic permit/prohibit constraints. We use a deontic policy language built on the Rei framework, expressed as OWL (Web Ontology Language) and evaluated at runtime by a high-performance logic engine entirely outside the LLM. The same pipeline governs both tool invocations by the agent and agent-to-agent messages. We show through examples that deontic policies capture governance constraints around security and privacy that mostly cannot be expressed in current production engines. Our approach composes naturally with industry-standard frameworks like A2AS.

</details>


### [167] [Measuring Curriculum Alignment across Topical Coverage, Competency, and Cognitive Depth: A Longitudinal Framework Applied to CS2013 and CS2023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19469)
*Sherzod Turaev,Mary John,Saja Aldabet,Mamoun Awad,Nazar Zaki,Khaled Shuaib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个人类参与的人机协作流水线，用于衡量计算机科学本科项目对国际课程指南（CS2013与CS2023）的覆盖程度，并纵向应用于一个认证项目。项目对两个指南的覆盖率分别为49.7%和50.9%，几乎恒定。通过检索-确认设计进一步分析能力表述和认知深度，发现CS2023推荐深度覆盖率（76%）显著低于CS2013（95%），反映了新指南期望提升而非项目退步。该工具可复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学本科教育受国际课程指南（约十年更新一次）约束，但缺乏可靠、可复现的方法来衡量项目对当前指南的覆盖程度，以及指南重构时覆盖情况如何变化。

Method: 提出人类参与的人机协作流水线：将项目课程和指南分别构建为结构化语料库，通过语义检索生成候选课程-知识单元匹配，再由人工依据明确定义确认匹配。在七个检索器中，倒数秩融合集成表现最佳，长上下文模型反而不如小型句子模型。由独立第二评分者验证地图（Cohen's kappa 0.64-0.69）。

Result: 项目对CS2023覆盖率为49.7%，对CS2013为50.9%，十年间几乎不变。能力表述覆盖约88%的已覆盖单元；认知深度方面，CS2023推荐深度覆盖76%，CS2013为95%。纵向比较揭示了持久性结构缺口（并行与分布式计算、编程语言基础、系统基础）与因指南演变导致的差异。

Conclusion: 该流水线可复用，能有效分离项目自身缺陷与指南标准演变带来的差异，为课程评估和指南修订提供可量化的依据。

Abstract: Undergraduate computer science is governed by international curricular guidelines revised about once a decade, yet programs lack a reliable, reproducible way to measure how completely they cover the current guidelines and how that coverage shifts when the guidelines are restructured. We address this with a human-in-the-loop pipeline that measures a program's coverage of an external body of knowledge, applied longitudinally to one accredited BSc in Computer Science against Computer Science Curricula 2013 (CS2013) and 2023 (CS2023). The pipeline represents the program and each guideline as structured corpora, generates candidate course-to-knowledge-unit matches by semantic retrieval, and confirms them through human judgment under an explicit coverage definition. Of seven benchmarked retrievers, a reciprocal-rank-fusion ensemble was strongest, and a reputed long-context model underperformed a small sentence model, so retriever choice must be measured. Both maps were validated by an independent second rater (Cohen's kappa 0.64 for CS2023, 0.69 for CS2013). The program covers 49.7% of CS2023 and 50.9% of CS2013 knowledge units, near-constant across a decade. Extending the same retrieve-then-confirm design to competency articulation and cognitive depth shows that the program articulates the competency for ~88% of covered units under each guideline, yet delivers it at the recommended depth for 76% of present units under CS2023 against 95% under CS2013, a gap reflecting the newer guideline's raised expectations, not the program. The longitudinal comparison separates persistent structural gaps (parallel and distributed computing, foundations of programming languages, systems fundamentals), uncovered against both guidelines and ABET, from differences that reflect the standard's evolution. The instrument is reusable and available from the authors on request.

</details>


### [168] [Diffusion Language Models: An Experimental Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19475)
*Thomas Bertolani,Davide Bucciarelli,Leonardo Zini,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对八种最先进的扩散语言模型（DLMs）在八个基准上进行了系统实验分析，涵盖推理、编码、翻译、知识问答和结构化问题解决等任务，评估了生成质量和计算效率，并分析了去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小等推理时因素的影响，揭示了DLMs在不同任务、架构和推理预算下的性能与效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有许多基于扩散的语言模型被提出，但由于评估协议、数据集、推理预算和生成超参数的不一致，难以公平比较不同DLM的能力并理解其性能与效率之间的权衡。

Method: 采用系统性实验分析方法，在八个基准上评估八种最先进的DLMs，同时考虑生成质量和计算效率；分析去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小和并行解掩码策略等推理时关键因素的影响；并在相同条件下进行小规模模型的受控对比实验。

Result: DLMs的行为受生成时设计选择的强烈影响，在不同的任务、架构和推理预算下呈现出差异化的性能与计算效率权衡。

Conclusion: 研究提供了关于当代DLMs能力与部署特性的实用见解，表明生成时设计选择对模型表现有显著影响。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized language modeling through autoregressive generation, enabling strong performance across a wide range of tasks. Recently, Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have emerged as an alternative paradigm that generates text through iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, allowing parallel refinement of entire sequences. While numerous diffusion-based architectures have been proposed, differences in evaluation protocols, datasets, inference budgets, and generation hyperparameters make it difficult to compare their capabilities and understand the trade-offs they offer. In this work, we present a systematic experimental analysis of modern DLMs. Specifically, we evaluate eight state-of-the-art DLMs across eight benchmarks spanning reasoning, coding, translation, knowledge, and structured problem solving, while explicitly considering both generation quality and computational efficiency. Beyond downstream evaluation, we analyze the impact of key inference-time factors, including denoising steps, context length, block size, and parallel unmasking strategies, and complement large-scale experiments with controlled comparisons of smaller models trained under identical conditions. Our analysis highlights the strengths and limitations of diffusion-based language modeling across different tasks, architectures, and inference budgets. We show that the behavior of DLMs is strongly influenced by generation-time design choices, leading to distinct trade-offs between performance and computational efficiency. Overall, our study provides practical insights into the capabilities and deployment characteristics of contemporary DLMs.

</details>


### [169] [Hidden Anchors in Multi-Agent LLM Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19494)
*Apurba Pokharel,Ram Dantu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体LLM deliberation的闭环动力学模型，引入"锚点"（anchor）概念表征智能体隐藏的内在信念。实验表明，锚点可从 deliberation 过程中恢复，并能解释模型置信度超越初始信念凸包的现象。跨三个开源模型家族验证，锚点影响普遍存在但位置不同，仅当锚点远离初始意见时 deliberation 才能突破凸包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM deliberation（多轮交换和修订答案）已被广泛用于提升推理和准确性，但其工作原理缺乏理论建模。现有经典意见动力学模型（如DeGroot、Friedkin-Johnsen）仅能刻画群体效应（herd effect），忽视了智能体内部内在信念的影响。

Method: 将多智能体 deliberation 建模为闭环动力系统，每个智能体携带隐藏的内在信念（锚点），该锚点持续拉扯其意见。从 deliberation 过程中恢复锚点，并通过检查恢复的锚点是否能预测held-out运行（泛化能力）来验证模型是否真正由锚点驱动。

Result: 在三个开源模型家族上验证，锚点的影响呈谱系分布（非全有或全无）。所有模型锚点的影响强度相近，但锚点位置不同——仅当锚点远离初始意见时，deliberation 才能突破凸包，此时需要完整的闭环模型来描述。

Conclusion: 锚点机制是解释多智能体 deliberation 中超越初始信念现象的关键因素。通过恢复锚点及其泛化测试，可判断模型是否真正由内在信念驱动。这项工作为理解LLM多轮协商行为提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM deliberation, where agents exchange and revise answers over several rounds, is increasingly used to improve reasoning and accuracy, yet how and why it works is rarely modelled. Such deliberation mirrors how humans reach decisions. As social animals we are pulled both by the group, the herd effect that classical opinion-dynamics models such as DeGroot and Friedkin--Johnsen capture, and by our own internal belief, which they do not. We model multi-agent deliberation as a closed-loop dynamical system in which each agent carries a hidden internal belief, its anchor, that continually pulls its opinion regardless of its neighbours. We show this anchor can be recovered from the deliberation alone, and that it explains a behaviour classical consensus rules forbid: an agent's confidence in the correct answer can climb past where any agent started, escaping the space (convexhull) formed by the initial beliefs. Checking whether the recovered anchor also predicts held-out runs (generalizes) gives a simple test for when a model is truly driven bysuch an anchor. Across three open-weight model families this is a spectrum, not all-or-nothing. All anchors' influence are about equally strongly, but they differ in where the anchor sits, and only when it sits far from the initial opinions does deliberation escape the hull and need the full closed-loop model.

</details>


### [170] [DeXposure-Claw: An Agentic System for DeFi Risk Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19501)
*Aijie Shu,Bowei Chen,Wenbin Wu,Cathy Yi-Hsuan Chen,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DeXposure-Claw系统，通过结构化证据路由LLM决策，用于DeFi监管的风险暴露预测与预警；同时提出DeXposure-Bench六轴评估框架，在真实数据上验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeFi监管面临快速变化的网络化信用风险，通用LLM智能体存在过度解读弱证据、推荐高风险干预、缺乏监管对齐评估等问题。

Method: (1) DeXposure-FM：图时间序列基础模型预测未来暴露网络；(2) 确定性监控与压力场景将预测转化为类型化警报、归因信号和场景证据；(3) 数据健康与置信度门控限制升级，最后输出可审计监管工单。评估采用DeXposure-Bench六轴框架，含决策轴（监管对齐绝对损失+虚假干预率）。

Result: 在五年周级真实数据上实验，系统全面支持所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: DeXposure-Claw通过结构化证据路由与门控机制，有效降低了LLM在DeFi监管中的误报风险，提供了监管对齐的评估框架。

Abstract: Decentralized finance exposes supervisors to fast-moving, networked credit risks. General-purpose LLM agents fit this setting poorly: they over-read weak evidence and recommend high-stakes interventions, while existing evaluations offer no regulator-aligned way to measure the resulting false alarms. We introduce DeXposure-Claw, a forecast-grounded agentic supervision system that routes LLM decisions through structured evidence: (1) DeXposure-FM, a graph time-series foundation model, forecasts future exposure networks; (2) deterministic monitors and stress scenarios then turn those forecasts into typed alerts, attribution signals, and scenario evidence; and (3) data-health and confidence gates constrain escalation before DeXposure-Claw emits auditable supervisory tickets with rationales. We further develop DeXposure-Bench, a six-axis evaluation harness, whose decision axis scores tickets against a regulator-aligned absolute-loss ground truth and an explicit false-intervention rate. Experiments on five years of weekly real data fully support our system. Code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/DeXposure-Claw.

</details>


### [171] [LLM Doesn't Know What It Doesn't Know: Detecting Epistemic Blind Spots via Cross-Model Attribution Divergence on Clinical Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19509)
*Akshat Dasula,Prasanna Desikan,Jaideep Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在结构化临床数据任务中能否识别自身知识局限。通过跨模型归因发散分析（对比Qwen 2.5 7B与XGBoost），发现：LLM口头置信度是认知空洞的（恒定输出0.856-0.937，与实际准确性无关）；存在逆难度效应（XGBoost高度正确时LLM准确率反而下降）；少样本示例与SHAP特征证据具有正交超加性，可将归因分歧分数从1.54降至0.38、准确率从49%提升至75.3%；基于归因发散信号的跨模型校准器可将期望校准误差从0.254降至0.080，无需访问模型内部或重复推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
